Original on Archive of Our Own

Summary

When Lady Lena challenges Princess Kara for the throne of Krypton they’re both shocked to discover they’ve met before, and more shocked to discover there’s a requirement for a princess to be “bound in marriage” to take the throne.
As parliament is suspended until a new monarch is crowned the law cannot be changed. With the church opposed to the union of two women they’re forced to find husbands to ensure at least one of them gets crowned Queen before the deadline is up.
Shenanigans ensue as they try to convince Krypton they’re in love with their future husbands while falling deeper in love with each other.

TW:
Mentioned past long term depression and suicide of one character (Lex)
Some emotional abuse (parental figure and romantic partner)
Church against same sex unions

1. Welcome Home

Author's Note:

Hello Everyone,
I was gonna be restrained and wait until I had everything absolutely 100% drafted but I apparently need to have a regular deadline otherwise I can't get myself to stick with a project the way I want to (Yay ADHD), so I'm going to start posting here to help fill the void while we wait for the rest of the final season.
This story is loosely based on/inspired by the Princess Diaries 2: Royal Engagement after I read a fanfic AU of it and got inspired (If anyone knows which fanfic that was and can tell me the name please let me know in the comments so I can share it for those interested. As usual I cannot remember the name or the tags)

Minor Spoilers for TW purposes:
Mon-el is not a good guy in this so sorry to the Mon-el Stans but yeah...
Also a strong thread in Lena's story is her still dealing with the loss of her brother to suicide years before so if that could be difficult for you please be aware before reading. I'll try and remember to specifically put warnings for the parts with the major Lex stuff but be aware he is a through line for Lena in both good and sad ways.

Kara smiled as the plane hit the tarmac and taxied towards the waiting red carpet. She was back, she was home. Krypton. She looked over and saw Alex smiling as she picked up her leather jacket and tucked her red medallion into her shirt. The medallion marked her as a member of the royal entourage, specifically medical adviser to the crown princess.

When Kara had to name her personal medical adviser she’d gone straight to her sister and begged her to take the role. Alex had been hesitant, worried about the optics of her not only being a young doctor but also Kara’s adoptive sister. Kara had pointed out she needed someone she trusted absolutely to make sure she not only understood but who she would feel safe talking to. She also added that it was a mostly honorary title as anything more than a cold would result in a half dozen doctors fussing over her.

Alex had still only agreed after Eliza had joined the conversation gently reminding Alex she'd been the one who explained to Kara what was happening when she got her first period and it had also been her who gave Kara the sex talk. Eliza asked whether she thought anyone else explaining such things to Kara would go well. Alex had looked at her blushing sister and decided then and there to take the role much to Kara’s relief. 

“You ready Princess sis?” Alex asked giving Kara a bright smile.

Kara took a deep breath and nodded. Eliza leaned over and squeezed her hand.

“We’ll be with you every step of the way,” she promised.

Kara felt a wave of affection for her adopted family. After her mother’s death when she was twelve she’d been worried her father would force her to come to Krypton and live with him, but it had seemed he didn’t care or perhaps he had forgotten her entirely, and so she got to stay with her American family. Eliza and Alex having lost Jeremiah in the same crash were not in the best place themselves but the three of them had bonded together and become a close family helping each other through their losses. 

When her Aunt Astra had turned up a year later to tell her that her father was dead Kara had been scared that she would be taken away. Instead she had simply found herself with more family. She may not be Inah to anyone again but her Aunt had made her ‘little one’ and gave her the connection to her home and culture she had been missing since her mother passed.

Every month Astra would call her without fail and for every birthday and Kryptonian holiday she would call and send Kara a gift. Every time making sure she never lost her connection to her people. The rare times she came to America Astra always made time to visit Kara, always in secret so as not to disturb her regular life, but every visit had been precious to Kara. 

Astra's husband Non died when Kara was sixteen. After the funeral Astra had come to see Kara. They spent two days together Astra listening to Kara talk about her favourite singers and everything she was doing with Alex at school and her crush on Kenny. At night Astra let her stay up late the two of them wrapped in the red Kryptonian blanket Astra had while she taught Kara the names of the stars and told their Kryptonian myths, just as her mother had when she was a child. 

Kara had known in the abstract that her father had been brother to the King of Krypton. But it was not until Non died and she became princess that she truly realised what that meant. Astra had held her the two of them wrapped in the blanket as Astra explained to her as gently as she could that Kara was the heir to the throne. She was an illegitimate child but her father had recognised her when she was very little and so her name was in the book of nobles next to her cousin Kal. 

When Non had died childless leaving Astra Queen it meant that Kara was the next in line, Kal was next after her but when he joined the church at 18 he gave up his birthright. She was the only royal child, the last daughter of the house of El. 

When Kara had asked if that meant she would be queen Astra had held her very tightly and told her only if she wanted to be. Alex had come in then and joined them in the blanket Eliza staying by the door. Astra had promised Kara would not need to ascend to the throne yet. She could not legally become Queen until she turned 23 anyway. She need not make any decisions until she was older.

When Kara turned 18 with the support of Alex and Eliza she made her first return trip to Krypton quietly entering the country and spending the summer with her Aunt learning about her country again. When she’d finished high school she spent a year in Krypton picking up odd jobs and enjoying the return to life among her people.

It cemented her decision. She went to Astra at the end of the year and said she was certain, she wanted to be Queen, she wanted to help her people. Astra had always told her she didn’t have to, but the relief on her face at Kara's decision had told Kara how much she had wanted for her to. 

On her twentieth birthday Kara had been presented formally to the people of Krypton as their crown Princess and her life was irreversibly changed. She had left Krypton soon after to begin her studies at Oxford studying international relations, then two years touring the various embassies and attending meetings with dignitaries gradually being introduced to her future role as Queen and the responsibilities involved. 

She had been the very model of what a princess should be. Apart from during probably the best month of her life in Australia, but that had been her last chance to really enjoy a little freedom before her life truly became all about her people. According to Astra everyone got a little time to enjoy themselves before they finally ascended the throne, and enjoy herself she had. 

Kara sighed softly remembering. Green eyes, thick dark hair, and that smirk. Rao that smirk had done her in every time. If only… In another life perhaps.

“Are you ready your highness?” Winn asked snapping Kara out of her daydreaming and alerting her that the plane had stopped.

She smiled at him. Winn was her personal assistant and had been with her for three years. In her final year of University Astra had sent her Winn to help manage everything and instruct her on any etiquette she might have forgotten. Kara felt confident, despite her position as his boss, saying they were good friends. She’d concluded this after they’d both gotten very drunk one night after a hellish day and sang ABBA songs at the top of their lungs jumping on her bed until the early hours of the morning. Something about sharing a hangover and the judgemental eyes of the hotel staff with someone bonded you like nothing else.

Nia came over and did a final outfit and makeup check. Nia was newer having only joined her a year before as her social media and style adviser but the two had become close very quickly. Nia gave her a double thumbs up and she turned to nod to Kara’s bodyguard of the last five years, J’onn. 

“Ready when you are,” Kara said smiling at Winn. 

He grinned back. With one last look at Alex and Eliza Kara moved to stand near the doors as they were disarmed. J’onn smiled at Kara and nodded to the air hostess. The flight crew stepped out. J’onn exiting directly after them. He gave Kara a thumbs up and she took a deep breath, made sure she was smiling, and stepped out into the bright Kryptonian sun. 

She could hear the Kryptonian national anthem begin the moment she was visible and she waved at the crowd assembled. She descended the steps careful not to trip as she went and went to shake the hands of the waiting dignitaries. 

The Prime Minister Drom-Mosk and the speaker of the house Turn-Kith were first both men greeting her politely. As she turned to greet the representative from the church her face broke into a wide smile and breaking protocol she threw her arms around her cousin. Kal laughed and hugged her back lifting her easily off her feet.

“Look at you!” she said excitedly indicating his new yellow and white robes. “A full priest of the church.”

Kal laughed and nodded.

“Yes, I am now allowed to do weddings and forgive sins,” he said laughing.

Kara beamed at him her eyes soaking in the very welcome sight of her dear cousin.

“Well in that case I shall have to get you for mine one day,” she said.

He laughed again.

“I think the sins yes, but I fear the high priests may throw somewhat of a fit if you tried to let anyone below three white lines bind you in matrimony.”

Kara sighed.

“Well then I shall just have to wait for you to become ambitious and get those stripes so I can still have you do it.”

Kal laughed again and Winn coughed quietly telling her she needed to move on. She gave Kal a final smile and he returned it stepping back so she could next greet Vosha-Vall the head of Argo university. The greetings finished she moved over to smile and pose for photos with some of the waiting crowd. After five minutes J’onn signalled she had to go and she waved goodbye letting herself be guided to the armoured car and got in. Alex, Eliza, Nia, and Winn joined her with J’onn getting in the front with the driver. 

They car took off and she let out a breath.

“How’d I do?” she asked looking at Nia.

“Pretty good,” Nia said looking at her tablet. “Alex you’re already popular, as usual.” 

Alex smirked. She hadn’t been able to be as close with Kara during the last five years as she finished her medical degree and did her residency. But she’d always come to join Kara during her time off and any and all pictures of them together had been wildly popular. When people found out that Alex was a lesbian they only got more so. Apparently the queer community in Krypton had made Alex into some kind of icon. They’d even invited her to speak at a few events.

“I was a little worried about it but the extra time with Kal actually seems to have gone down well. People like how genuine it was,” Nia said.

Kara laughed.

“Because it was genuine. Do you think you can find some extra time this month for me to see him?” she asked turning to Winn.

Winn smiled at her and made a note.

“Anything for you your highness,” he teased.

She stuck her tongue out and he laughed.

“Hey you’re about to be Queen, there will be no more of that childishness,” Eliza said waggling a finger at Kara. 

Kara pulled a face in response enjoying Eliza’s quiet laugh. She turned and smiled looking out the window at her country. She had missed Krypton.

People in the streets were waving at the cars even though they couldn’t see through the tinting. Kara smiled and waved back.

“Okay princess, your schedule,” Winn said before she could roll down the window to properly wave back at people.

“Today is fairly relaxed as we weren’t sure if there would be delays. When we arrive at the palace there’s the formal greeting with the Queen, and the inspection of the staff, I’ll give you a hint no matter what happens they’re up to standard.”

Kara smiled and nodded.

“Noted.”

“There’s a couple of hours for you to go over the ceremonial standards and then you can relax in your suite until dinner. The Queen has made it a nice family gathering and I do believe there is a clear night ahead so you can also do a little stargazing tonight.”

Kara smiled and looked at Alex who smiled back.

“I bet I can find all five heroes before you do,” she said.

Alex scoffed.

“You’re on, but you can’t get help from Astra.”

Kara chuckled. Astra knew the Kryptonian night sky better than anyone.

When the car pulled up at the front of the palace Astra came out to greet them with a welcoming smile wearing a pale blue traditional Kryptonian dress. 

The moment the car door opened Kara practically flew out of it to hug her Aunt. Astra laughed returning the hug with equal enthusiasm.

“It has been too long little one,” Astra said pressing a traditional kiss to the top of Kara’s head.

Kara beamed back and hugged her again. She had missed her. Astra’s returning embrace said she was not alone in her feelings. They both took a deep breath and separated so Astra could greet everyone else. Alex got a tight hug and a kiss on the head too. Astra argued that if Alex was Kara’s sister she was family and so got all the same privileges. Kara knew Alex was secretly very pleased that she would always get a kiss on the head too.

Eliza was greeted with a warm hug and a kiss on the cheek. The two exchanging a long look that seemed to be a whole conversation that ended with them both laughing. The two older women turned to the group.

“J’onn, a pleasure to see you again,” Astra said inclining her head towards him. “And you as well, Winn, Nia. I am happy to see you all so well.”

J’onn, Winn, and Nia all gave Astra a bow and their thanks. 

“Now, shall we finish the formalities?” Astra asked extending an arm to Kara. 

Kara grinned looping her arm through Astra’s and letting her slowly guide her up the steps and towards the large arched doors of the palace. A bell sounded high above and Kara looked up to see a second banner be dropped to show she had arrived.

The royal palace in Argo was made of a white stone with a faint blueish tinge. In ages past it had been called the fortress of solitude as when the snow came it would be near invisible against the mountains and nigh unreachable. Modern extensions as Argo had become the capital had changed it and now few called it by the old name outside of stories.

“Now, if you listen carefully you can actually hear the staff racing through the hallways to line up for the inspection,” Astra said in a hushed tone making Kara smile. “So we’re going to walk very slowly towards the gardens.”

Kara laughed and looked behind her to see Alex escorting Eliza who was smiling at her. Nia and Winn were busily examining their tablets but seemed happy. Kara turned back to her Aunt and smiled.

“Well, I would like to make a good first impression,” she said. 

Astra laughed softly.

“I think you’re a bit late for most of them. Though there is a new head of staff who I think you’ll be happy to see.”

Kara’s eyes lit up.

“Ooh who is it?” Kara asked excitedly.

Astra laughed at her eagerness.

“I’ll let her tell you.”

Kara grinned she was pretty sure she knew who it would be.

“There’s also a new Commander of the guard. I think you’ve met, Querl Dox.”

“Brainy!” Kara said waving at him as he came around the corner and gave the traditional Kryptonian salute.

“Crown Princess,” he said formally.

Kara almost went to hug him but decided one breach of protocol was enough for today so she smiled at him  as she passed.

“I’ll get a hug from you later,” she promised.

“Of course Crown Princess,” he said not breaking his salute.

Kara smiled and they made the turn to the gardens walking through the hedges. Nia lifted her tablet to film the inspection of the staff. There was the sound of several pairs of running feet nearby and Astra smiled turning to indicate the palace before they stepped properly into the gardens.

“I had the facade touched up recently,” Astra said motioning with her hand to the white tower behind them. 

Kara quickly turned so the inspection wouldn’t officially start as the last of the staff raced into place. She took a step to the side to better see the walls.

“Ah yes, lovely it’s very ni-” 

She slipped and tried to recover but then her heels sank into the soft garden bed and she fell. J’onn reached out to catch her but he was too far behind due to the presence of the royal guard. She put out her hand to catch herself and grabbed the first solid object her hand found. 

Kara breathed a sigh of relief as she gripped a metal pipe in the hedge and Alex caught her other hand effectively stopping her from falling into the garden. She glanced over her shoulder at the flowers she’d almost crushed.

“The gardens are looking fantastic,” she said with a smile.

Astra chuckled and Alex sighed shaking her head as she and J’onn moved to pull Kara back onto the path. Then her hand slipped and she fell with a yell as there was an echoed series of yells and shrieks from the staff as the sprinklers activated.

A figure jumped over her and the sprinklers were shut off. Kara found herself looking up at a familiar smiling face.

“Well if I had any doubt as to who you were that certainly cleared it up,” Vasquez said. She smiled and offered Kara a hand up. “Welcome back your highness.”

Kara sighed and accepted it with an apologetic smile.

“Hi Vasquez, I see you got a promotion.”

Vasquez laughed and nodded.

“Well when Hank heard you were becoming queen he decided it was probably best to put in his resignation and wished me good luck.”

Kara laughed and sighed looked apologetically at the dripping line of household staff. She quickly took her place for the inspection brushing dirt and leaves off herself. She could see several of the staff trying valiantly not to laugh at her. In particular the kitchen staff who all looked familiar. She met the eyes of the head chef Fig-Tix. He started shaking with the effort not to laugh.

“I am terribly sorry about that everyone. I would promise this will likely never happen again but… well I recognise a few faces and so you’d know that’s a lie,” she said.

There was a laugh from the long serving staff and quite a few of the newer ones. 

“So I’ll just say you’ve all passed inspection and… again sorry.”

There was another laugh and Vasquez sighed.

“Fifteen minutes for you all to dry and then get back to your stations,” she said. 

The staff dispersed with much laughter and a few calling welcome to their princess. Kara looked over at her entourage. Alex was laughing silently. Winn face palmed and Astra just sighed and smiled at her shaking her head. Eliza gave her an encouraging thumbs up. 

Her gaze settled on Nia who looked apologetic.

“I’m guessing that was filmed live?” Kara said. 

Nia nodded and Kara laughed. Well her clumsiness was bound to be caught on camera eventually.

 

Lena felt immediate relief as the wheels finally landed on the runway. She released her death grip on the armrest and sent a quick prayer of thanks to Rao. Jess politely didn’t say anything until they had taxied over to the private hangar and the doors were opened.

“Welcome home my lady,” the flight attendant said.

Lena gave her a quick smile and looked out the window. Home. Krypton. She had missed her country. She smiled looking at the distant fields of white flowers and the high mountains beyond. 

She gathered her bag and coat stepping out and breathing deeply as the clean mountain air filled her lungs. She was home again. She smiled as she saw her driver Frank open the car door for her.

“Hello Frank,” she said smiling at him.

“Welcome home my lady. We’ve missed you.”

Lena beamed at him as Jess quickly slid into the back seat of the car.

“My mother been any trouble?” she asked.

Frank widened his eyes slightly.

“Of course not my lady. Your mother is goodness itself,” he said with false sincerity.

Lena laughed and got into the car. Frank joined them a minute later as their luggage was loaded.

“Where to first?” Frank asked.

“My office, I want to pick up a few things. Then to my mother’s for dinner at six.”

“Straight to work, as usual,” he said giving her a gentle smile.

Lena returned it and turned to Jess as the car started moving.

“So your schedule is mostly clear tonight,” Jess said. “You wanted me to remind you about going over the princess articles, also the voting record for Minister Zod.”

“My mother should have those for me when we arrive this evening, did you get a response about my request for the research grant list?”

“Yes, it’s been printed and should be waiting for you at the office. There should also be the final documents from the Luthor foundation for your handover to Sam.”

“Good, please make sure I sign those before we leave for dinner. I want the transfer finalised before tomorrow afternoon.”

“Of course,” Jess said making a note. “You also need to approve the final version of your letter to the editor for the Kryptonian Tribune, draft a response to Lord Wayne about the trade agreement, and you wanted to go over the funding request for an update to the Kith bridge.”

Lena sighed and nodded. 

“Did I get the paper from Dr Olsen yet?”

Jess hesitated for a moment then nodded.

“She sent it through, when we arrive I’ll have it printed out for you.”

“That’s okay, just send it to me now.”

“Of course,” Jess said tapping on her phone.

Lena clicked over to her email and opened the report.

The promise of Virtual Reality in providing mental health services by Dr K. Olsen.

Lena sighed and saw Jess watching her cautiously. She started reading. 

They arrived at her Argo office a half hour later and she got out immediately heading up. She nodded to the couple of staff that were still in the office despite the weekend. They all quickly jumped to their feet greeting her. She smiled but didn’t say anything more than a passing greeting as she made her way to her office.

She walked in and saw someone had printed out all the files she asked for and put them in neat white folders on her desk. A small stack of more general papers waited in her in tray. 

Lena sat and opened the first file. She looked up and saw the picture by her computer. She hesitated her eyes lingering for a moment. The picture showed Lex grinning at the camera one arm around her fifteen year old self in her fencing gear. Her hair was messy and her face covered in sweat as she smiled holding up her National championship trophy. She sighed and forced her eyes away from his smiling face to the reports before her. She had work to do.

 

“Mother have you seen his voting history. He was against providing aid during a flood, a flood for Rao’s sake,” Lena said incredulous.

She highlighted the line with a yellow highlighter shaking her head as she continued reading down the voting history of Minister Zod. 

“Lena dear watch this-”

Lillian moved her phone into Lena’s view. Lena shifted her papers to keep reading.

“No thank you, I want to find out what else this idiot has opposed,” Lena said not even glancing at the screen.

“The crown princess just completely ruined her inspection of the staff,” Lillian said with a laugh. “This is going to be easier than I thought.”

“Mhmm that’s- oh really?” Lena said exasperated. “He objected to the extension of the environmental protection act. He’s a conservative! It was their policy.”

Lillian chuckled and shook her head smiling as she watched the video on her phone. Lena looked at her.

“Mother, why is it that this,” she gestured to the papers spread before her. “Idiot. Is the one who will be announcing my challenge to the throne?’

Lillian looked up from her phone.

“Two reasons. One he is an idiot and thus a useful pawn. And two, as the parliamentary records officer he has access to the official articles on princess’ ascension.”

Lena sighed.

“Speaking of where are they?” she asked looking around at the mess of papers spread around her.

“Where’s what?” Lillian asked her eyes on her phone.

Lena sighed and looked at her.

“The Princess articles I wanted to look over the requirements to make sure I had my arguments ready should they be needed.”

Lillian glanced at Lena again and then at the papers on the table.

“Aren’t they there?” she asked.

Lena sighed.

“No mother they’re not,” she said barely controlling her exasperation. “Is there something in them I’m not going to like?”

Lillian sighed and looked at Lena for a long moment.

“No dear. Why would you think that?”

Lena really wanted to roll her eyes, but the last time she did she’d gotten a two hour dressing down from Lillian and she really didn’t have the time for that.

“Mother this is the third time I’ve asked you for the articles and the third time they’ve not been given to me. So, I’m going to ask again, is there something in them I am not going to like?”

Lillian sighed and raised an eyebrow.

“Really Lena, you think I would hide something from you now? When we’re so very close to achieving the goal of this family for nine generations.”

And so it begun. Lena tuned her out. She knew the key points. Nine generations, denied the throne, dearest wish of her father, all the preparations made before her brother went missing, last hope for a Luthor ruler before they were too distant, and she was an illegitimate daughter born of an indiscretion of her father’s with a farmers daughter. 

“Eve forgot it again didn’t she?” Lena said cutting into the lecture.

Lillian sighed.

“I don’t know why I keep her around,” she muttered. “She’s absolutely useless.”

Lena went back to the voting records. So there was something coming she was not going to like. She forced down another sigh and kept reading Minister Zod’s the voting history. She just hoped whatever was coming wasn’t too ridiculous. Knowing her mother it could be anything from must have a yearly gynaecology exam to must never contradict her mother. 

She met Jess’ eyes across the table and her assistant gave her a sympathetic look. As she kept reading Lena found her attention drifting back. Back to blonde hair streaked with blue and a soft pink mouth upon her own. She stared unseeing at the page in front of her as her head was suddenly full of breathy pleas. 

“Oh Rao Lena don’t stop. Yes there! Yes! Lena, Lena, Lena-”

“Lena!”

She snapped out of her reverie with a start.

“Yes Mother?” she asked rubbing her head.

“What is wrong with you tonight? I swear ever since your little vacation to Austria-”

“Australia.”

“You’ve been so unfocused.”

Lena sighed and turned to her mother.

“Sorry I am simply preoccupied with thoughts about what I’ll do once I take the throne. I think a searchable database for all of these records might need to be first. Seriously why is nothing electronic?”

“Because her majesty managed to get a transparency clause into the updated standards so the records must be publicly available. In response the major parties made it as difficult as possible for anyone to search them. Eve spent a whole three hours copying those for you.”

Lena sighed and grabbed the next stack. Queen Astra had probably fought for a decade to get these records made publicly available, the least she could do was actually read them. Half a page later her eyes were glazing over with thoughts of Kara again. Lena sighed and pushed herself away from the table giving up.

“I’ll finish these tomorrow. Jess can you block me out a couple of hours tomorrow morning to finish these then.”

Jess nodded quickly.

“Did you want me to move your call to the hospital?” she asked.

Lena sighed.

“Give them my apologies and ask Jan to please email through any requests they have. Also inform them I’ve returned and I’ll have PC collected next week, details to be confirmed on Monday. Also double check that Sam is still set to arrive on Sunday and if you can-”

“Your apartments in Argo have already been cleaned and deliveries will be made and put away Sunday morning before she lands,” Jess said with a smile. “I confirmed this morning.”

Lena gave her an apologetic look. 

“Sorry Jess. Clearly I need some sleep.”

Jess smiled back. They both knew it wasn’t sleep she was missing. She’d slept most of the flight. Lena sighed and bid her mother goodnight. Lillian barely looked away from whatever she was going over on her phone to return the farewell. 

Lena headed up to her rooms and closed the door with a sigh. She closed her eyes giving herself a moment. When she opened them again she looked over at the picture sitting on the bookshelf. It was of Lex smiling holding Ignatius’ reins after a particularly long ride together through the Kryptonian mountains. 

“This is all your fault you know,” she told his picture. “Now I have to give up my chance at love and a great sex life to become queen.”

Rhea nodded again giving Kara an odd look. Kara quickly picked up her knife and fork cutting into her potato.

“So I hear you’re going to be driving for the Kryptonian team in the Krypton 100,” Kara said smiling at Mon-el. 

“I am, my third year as the driver. I’m rather looking forwards to it,” he said. “We’ve made a lot of changes to the car which I hope-”

“Oh yes! I heard about this,” Lena said suddenly looking very excited. “There's a rumour the team made a change to the fuel injectors, is this true?”

Mon-el laughed a little.

“Yes, though I confess I’m not the best mechanic so I don’t know what we did to them exactly. You’d need to ask Barbara, Barbara Gordon. She’s the brains behind the change.”

“Why do I know that name?” Jack asked.

“Because she was the one who…”

Kara smiled at Mon-el as Lena launched into an intense engineering discussion with Jack that flew over everyone else’s head. General conversation continued around the table and Kara ate exchanging small talk with Mon-el about his racing career. 

She could see why he was so popular. He was handsome, funny, charming, and very friendly. If she wasn’t hyper aware of Lena three seats away she might have found herself quite taken by him. As it was her eyes would slide over to look at Lena every so often to see Lena's eyes sparkling and her hands moving energetically as she talked with Jack and then Alex. The three of them had a fierce debate about the science in some obscure horror movie that sounded terrifying to Kara.

Mon-el didn’t seem to notice though his focus was on her alone. After the meal was finished everyone left the table to get up and mingle talking casually. Kara gave Mon-el an apologetic look as they stood a little awkwardly together while their mothers talked about them as children. Once they shifted to their teenage years Kara gave Mon-el a worried look.

They moved away walking a little down the beach before they heard anything too embarrassing. They were quiet both trying to think of something to talk about. Then Mon-el’s eyes lit up and he raced off returning with a volleyball. He motioned to the nearby net.

“How do you feel about a game?” he asked.

Kara smiled gratefully at him.

“Thank you. That would be a great,” she said.

Mon-el grinned and gave his best aw shucks look. 

“Shall we get them to play too? I bet we can beat them,” he said in a half whisper pointing at Jack and  Lena. “And if they won’t I’m sure you could almost beat me.”

Kara laughed and then narrowed her eyes playfully at him. He grinned and held out the ball. She grabbed it and started spinning it on one finger.

“Hey Lena!” Kara called.

Lena turned around and made her way towards them Jack happily walking with her. Kara held up the ball.

“You wanna play?” she asked.

“No thank you,” Lena said quickly. “I’ll just watch and… read. Jack will though.”

“I will?” Jack asked surprised.

“Yes,” Lena said giving him a smile. “And uh… shirt off. I need to show everyone just how pretty you are. I shall admire you from the side lines accordingly.” 

Jack laughed and nodded stripping off his shirt and posing for her. Lena looked him over carefully lowering her glasses and adding a small bite of her lip. Jack winked and Lena laughed. Mon-el immediately took off his shirt and Kara gave him a smile walking around him and making appropriately admiring looks. She looked over at Lena and smirked.

“I think mine might be bigger than yours.”

Lena narrowed her eyes.

“Oh we’re going there are we?” she asked.

Kara smiled and looked at Mon-el. He winked at her.

“Yes, I do believe we are,” Kara said.

The boys flexed next to each other engaging in a little friendly competition. Then Kara took off her shirt to reveal the bikini top underneath. Jack looked at Lena who was suddenly staring.

“Come on Lena, play too,” Kara said with a smile knowing exactly what she was doing to Lena as she subtly flexed holding up the ball.

“I am… terrible at sports,” Lena said actively stopping herself from drooling.

“Says the Olympic medallist,” Kara said tossing the ball in the air and offering it to Lena.

Lena sighed stripping off her shirt and got up. Kara’s eyes immediately zeroed in on her chest. Lena had always looked amazing in a bikini but she seemed to look even better all of a sudden. 

“Don’t drool,” Lena reminded her.

Kara sucked in a breath as Lena took the ball from her outstretched hand and walked over to Jack. He grinned at her and held out a hand. In a very queenly gesture she placed her hand in his and let him lead her over to the other side of the net. 

Kara looked at Mon-el. He grinned back and offered his arm. She took it the two of them walking over to their side.

“Shall we crush them?” he asked.

Kara grinned at him.

“Well they are pretenders to the throne.”

Mon-el laughed and they moved into position. Kara’s eyes met Lena’s. Lena quirked an eyebrow and Kara narrowed her eyes. Lena tossed the ball in the air and it begun. 

It took about three seconds for them all to get wildly competitive. After their third very loud debate about whether or not a ball was in or not Astra came forwards shaking her head. She took a seat at the net and was swiftly provided a whistle to help her judge. No one argued with the Queen. 

Kara and Mon-el had the physical advantage but Lena and Jack knew each other well and could communicate with ease. Lena and Jack cruised to victory in the first game as Kara and Mon-el worked out how to stay out of each other’s way. Kara and Mon-el stole the second game smashing through the poor defence Lena and Jack had. The third game however stayed close as both sides now knew what to expect. As they inched closer to 21 it got fiercer. The distant cameras capturing every heated moment of competition. 

It was 19-18 Lena and Jack in the lead when Mon-el got a lucky hit past Lena, much to her annoyance, then Kara hit the ball across court Jack just missing it putting them one away from victory. 

Mon-el served and Jack leapt into the air slamming it down to Kara’s right with a sudden burst of power to tie them up. They were all sweating covered in sand and itching to win. Kara met Lena’s eyes and her lips twitched. Lena smirked as the serve went up and the final battle begun. 

It was vicious. Jack and Lena working to set everything they could sending their opponents running every direction as Mon-el and Kara sank every bit of their power into their attacks trying to push through their defence. 

Jack dived just making the save and sending the ball up into the air near the net. It was coming down over the net no way to tell which side.

“Mine!” Lena and Kara yelled both of them running for the net. 

They both jumped. For a moment they were both hung suspended in the air their arms raised then they both tried to hit the ball. Their heads collided with a loud thunk and they collapsed on their respective sides of the net.

There was a second of stunned silence then Astra started laughing and they both clutched their now aching heads as Alex yelled for ice. Mon-el and Jack raced forwards fussing over them and accepting the quickly provided ice packs applying them to the rapidly growing lumps on their heads. 

Kara and Lena exchanged a look under the net the ball between them as ice was applied and people rushed around. They both started laughing.

 

DAY 4

Lena sighed looking at the morning newspapers. All three major Kryptonian papers made them front page news. The Kryptonian Tribune had apparently managed to get a mid air shot of them bashing heads and splashed it all over their front page with Battle of the Queens. The Krypton Sun had them both lying on the sand right after impact First blood in bitter cat-fight over throne. The Argo Herald had chosen a different tactic. They had made their front page the two of them sitting in the sand looking at each other ice being applied to their heads Jack and Mon-el in focus beside them. The fight for the crown a tie.

Lena sighed putting aside the papers and looked at Lillian who glared at her. She turned to Jack who was sitting at the breakfast table having appeared early that morning looking fresh and bright and immediately filled his plate with fruit and toast. 

“We need to discuss strategy,” Lillian said. 

Lena and Jack exchanged a look.

“Alright, how did you want to change our pre-existing plan mother?” Lena asked.

“They’re going to want to play you off against her. You’re the usurper you need to make sure they can’t spin you as evil so don’t say anything mean about her. Jack I assume you can be romantic.”

Jack looked up from his toast and gave Lillian a smile.

“The most romantic,” he said. “What do you want cloyingly sweet, puppy love, or gentleman seduction?” 

“Try devoted husband,” Lillian said. “You need to woo every woman in Krypton with how you treat her. Mon-el is going to run around being showy and over the top, I know his type. Jack you need to come across as genuine, understated, and whatever you do, straight.”

Jack laughed softly.

“Just like University then?” he asked.

Lena winked.

“Can you still kiss convincingly?” Lena asked.

Jack smiled at Lena over his coffee and winked.

“My mother still thinks I’m straight, so yes. But only as long as there’s no tongue,” he said.

Lena laughed. There had never been any tongue. 

“Take this seriously the pair of you, if there’s the slightest-”

“Mother we convinced you for two years, I think we can convince Krypton,” Lena said extending her hand. 

Jack smiled and looked adoringly at her before kissing her hand.

“How long before there’s fake sex?” he asked.

Lena laughed.

“Two weeks, I’m a lady.”

Lillian made a disgusted noise at them both but otherwise stayed quiet. Sam came in and dropped into the chair opposite Lena. She looked at Jack who was still giving Lena his best adoring look.

“Ah the game begins again?” she said. “When does the fake sex start.”

“Two weeks,” Jack said. “She’s a lady.”

Jack sighed and added a pout. It wasn’t as good as Kara’s but still suitably adorable.

“You’ll need to add the messy hair to really sell it,” Sam said.

Jack growled turning to glare at Sam.

“There will be no messing with my hair.”

“Not for two weeks there won’t be,” Lena said. “Shall I be the top again?”

Sam laughed and Lillian tossed her napkin down and left muttering under her breath. They waited until she was out of the room then turned back to each other exchanging smiles.

“I think she might still be a little mad she fell for the act too,” Jack said.

Sam laughed and Lena allowed herself a smile. 

“Can I catch you making out in a cupboard again?” Sam asked.

“No, I’m supposed to be a future Queen. I can’t be caught necking like a teenager in a cupboard.”

Sam grinned and winked at Jack who smiled back. Jess came in and begun giving Lena her schedule. Lena sighed when she realised how much she had to do.

“Sam can you help Jack, don’t let him use any sky writing, hot air balloons, or just generally anything in the air. Other than that… well you know how to make him look good.”

Sam laughed and nodded. Lena rose and picked up her jacket checking herself in the mirror before turning back to her friends.

“Right time to go convince the media of Krypton how superior I am without actually insulting the love of my life,” Lena said. 

Jack and Sam aw’d. Lena glared at them and adjusted her collar making sure everything sat just right. She examined herself in the red suit and contemplated her makeup. She looked at Sam.

“Should I wear something else for the interviews?” she asked.

Sam considered her for a moment and shook her head.

“Lean into the usurper thing a little. Dark colours, blues, blacks, and greens. Kara already has the vibrant colours and light aesthetic you should contrast. Focus on looking more put together and classy. For all professional things wear suits, for the garden parties, evenings, etc we’ll get you in dresses to highlight your femininity. But during the week no that’s perfect, but ditch red in the future it’s too strongly associated with the house of El. Go charcoal next time.”

Lena nodded and checked herself one last time before giving Jess a look and heading for the door.

“Jack I expect something good tonight, preferably food related because I will be starving.”

Jack saluted.

“Yes darling.”

 

Kara smiled and greeted Mon-el with a tight hug. He gave her a bright smile and offered her a racket.

“You know if we keep this up we could totally be the most sporty royal pair in the world. We could have bingo cards.”

Kara laughed.

“Well I do love getting out and being active,” she said. “Now as this is for charity we need to be all kinds of gracious.”

Mon-el bowed low.

“I shall follow your lead my future Queen.”

“But we also need to destroy them,” she added.

He laughed.

“I shall do my very best. I do need to impress you still.”

Kara smiled and they walked out together. There was applause from the waiting crowd and then they sized up against their opponents. The Genovians smiled. At first the game was competitive. Then it was discovered that both Kara and Mia were klutzy and it became a race to see which one caused injury first. Kara won, technically. For the game point she sent the ball hurtling towards Mia but her momentum carried her forwards and she dropped her racket and tripped over it to end up sprawled on the pitch. The ball however continued hitting the other side where Mia tripped over it and went hurtling into the net with a pained grunt.

Mon-el and Lord Devereaux exchanged a look. Lord Devereaux smiled as he accepted an ice pack and went to take care of his girlfriend as Mon-el did the same.

“You know, maybe we should avoid sports with balls,” Mon-el said with a smile.

Kara laughed and accepted the ice pack. 

“You might be right.”

She exchanged a look with Mia over the net. They shared a moment. They were both able to finish a few minutes later after a snickering Alex declared them both fine. 

After that the game was called in the Kryptonians favour and there was applause as they all left the pitch. 

Mon-el and Kara were quickly ambushed by the Lur-Var of the Kryptonian Tribune. Mon-el handled the opening volley of questions with a smile allowing Kara a moment to consider her bruised knuckles. 

“And how do you feel about your challenger, Lena Luthor?” Lur-Var asked deliberately turning to smile at Kara.

Kara couldn’t help her smile.

“I think she’s lovely,” Kara said immediately.

“She’s trying to steal the throne from you,” Lur-Var said bewildered.

“Well as far as her family is concerned my family stole it from hers so…” Kara shrugged. “She’s also just a wonderful person all around, did you know she was part of the team that drafted the new changes to the environmental protections act and she was involved in the discussions-”

“You sound like you almost admire her,” Lur-Var said cutting her off.

Kara scoffed.

“I can’t think of anyone who wouldn’t admire her. She’s done a tremendous amount of good in her life so far and I’m honoured to call her a friend.”

“A friend?” Lur-Var asked. “You consider Lady Luthor a friend?”

Kara nodded smiling.

“And what about you Lord Daxam, how do you feel about the usurper?” Lur-Var asked.

Mon-el shrugged smiling.

“I’ve only met her once so I can only really comment that she seems to be a well educated engineer and a great competitor. Though if she’s managed to impress Princess Kara she must be pretty amazing.”

Kara beamed at him. He grinned back. They excused themselves heading for the change rooms. 

In the car back to the palace Kara smiled at Mon-el.

“Thank you, for backing me up about Lena,” she said.

He smiled.

“Well it sounds like you know her pretty well, if you say she’s a good person then I’m sure she must be.”

Kara nodded.

“She really is. She’s one of the best people I’ve ever met. The more I learn about her the more I find I like,” she admitted.

Mon-el smiled and nodded.

“You two are… Best friends?”

Kara laughed and nodded.

“That’s probably a good way to put it. She’s my best friend, well besides Alex but she’s my sister so that’s different.”

“So Lena’s like a sister to you?”

Kara started laughing and couldn’t stop which made Mon-el laugh.

“Okay not sisters,” he said. 

Kara shook her head still smiling.

“Mon-el," kara lowered her voice to a more serious tone. "I am sure you’re aware of the situation I am in, and why you were invited here.”

He nodded and sighed.

“You need a husband.”

“For one year,” she said. “I think we could be very good friends in time, and I think we can win the hearts of the people of Krypton before the wedding. I also understand that you are the kind of person who would understand why I must do this. I do not wish to keep you from finding love in the future, and so I only ask for your help, as a friend.”

Mon-el smiled and taking her hand placed a kiss on it.

“And were I to fall in love with you?” he asked.

“Well, then I imagine it would simply make things both easier and harder for you,” she said giving him a bright smile.

He laughed softly.

“And if you were to fall in love with me?”

Kara laughed softly retrieving her hand gently.

“I would be quite surprised. So, can I count on you for this? An engagement, wedding, and a year of marriage.”

He nodded and she smiled gratefully at him.

“And any attempt to win your heart?” he asked playfully.

“You’re welcome to try, but I’m afraid someone else might have already stolen it,” she said quickly looking out the window.

“Oh? And why is he not here then?” he asked.

Kara took a deep breath and sighed.

“They can’t marry me. Which is why I’m asking you to,” she said.

Mon-el sighed and reached over to give her knee a gentle squeeze.

“Don’t worry fair Princess. I shall be your brave knight and take those worries from your heart to bring that smile to your face.”

Kara smiled at him.

“See, you’re already falling for me and forgetting all about him,” he said with a wink.

“I’m afraid that would take a lot more than a few smiles,” she said gently.

“Well I like a challenge,” Mon-el said sitting back.

“Oh do you now?” Kara asked forcing a laugh.

Mon-el nodded.

“Well then, how about tomorrow you join me in the morning for a run through Argo? You can see if you can beat me from the palace to the outlook and back.”

 

Lena returned to the palace late that evening exhausted from all the meetings and found herself standing at the door to her assigned suite where a red bow was tied around the door. A note was attached in Jack’s handwriting. Follow the ribbon.

She took out her phone first taking a picture of the bow and then starting a video as she opened the door and placed her hand on the ribbon. She followed it through the dimly lit lounge around the dining table, then the coffee table and finally out onto the balcony where Jack quickly straightened up and smiled at her as she lifted the camera to capture him.

He’d set them a table on the balcony and lit a half dozen candles to see by. In the darkness they added a warm glow over everything. Jack himself was wearing a pair of black pants with a dark blue dress shirt rolled up to his elbows the top two buttons undone.

She smiled at him and he grinned back.

“My lady fair,” he said bowing. Lena laughed softly. “I thought that after a tiring day you could use a break. So I spent the morning at the markets acquiring only the finest ingredients and made you dinner.” 

Lena smiled at him and he grinned back. She ended the video and they sat. 

“Did you really make me dinner?” she asked.

He nodded looking very proud of himself. She sat and tasted the pasta and sighed happily.

“Best beard ever,” she said. 

He laughed and they ate in comfortable silence for a while. 

Notes for the Chapter:

And so the games begin! Can they keep their secret from everyone? How does Mon-el plan to win over Kara's heart? Will Jack make more beard jokes?
The answers to all these questions and more, next time. Until then, stay safe y'all.

Next time:
Kara and Lena have a fight... in the water!

5. Princess fight?

DAY 6

Kara sighed as another arrow missed the target and buried itself in the grass around the target. She turned to look at Nia sitting on the stone bench.

“Nia distract me, what are people saying about me and Mon-el?”

Mon-el grinned at her from his place by a statue. She returned the smile as best she could.

“Okay so generally speaking you’re both tracking well,” Nia said looking at her tablet.

Kara nodded picking up another arrow. Alex took an exaggerated step backwards. Kara rolled her eyes but Alex smiled and winked at her. She raised the bow aiming for the circle again. 

“So it looks like everyone is trying to make it a big competition but the two of you have nothing bad to say about each other so they’re struggling. Lena said some lovely things about you in her interview for the Tribune by the way.”

Kara let go and the arrow completely missed instead shooting into the nearby hedge. She sighed and drew another.

“How am I tracking?” Mon-el asked from his place on the bench beside Nia.

“Good,” Nia said smiling at him.

Kara aimed quickly and let the arrow fly. It did slightly better but still ended up in the hedge. She drew another arrow. She was twenty in and still failing to get close. It was like the arrows had a mind of their own.

“General commentary suggests people like you as a pair,” Nia continued. “You’re both energetic, friendly and good looking. You’re mostly getting called charming, or boyish. Her beating you on your run this morning is earning a lot of positive attention, especially how you didn’t seem to mind losing.”

Kara fired and the arrow hit the dirt in front of the target. She sighed. She used to be good at this. She and Alex spent a whole summer doing archery in Midvale with the bows Astra sent them for their birthdays. Alex seeing her frustration gave her an encouraging smile.

“Well a lord must always give way to a princess,” Mon-el said with a shrug.

“How about the food festival?” Kara asked turning to accept another handful of arrows from Alex.

“Oh people loved that,” Nia said. “There’s a thousand comments about how much you two ate and how cute it is that you both love food that much. All the vendors seemed to have a picture of you two eating their food and so they’ve put them everywhere with the hashtag royal appetite.”

“Nice,” Mon-el said grinning at Kara.

She returned the smile and fired again. It was almost in.

“What about our competition? The usurpers?” Mon-el asked.

Nia was quiet for a moment then she made a soft hmm sound.

“Okay so they’re definitely winning on the romantic side. Not surprising given their history. They’re used to be a couple and Jack is good at playing the sweet romantic for an audience. Lena has mostly focused on being seen as more competent and professional than Kara and then softening around Jack. She’s been out shaking a lot of hands and showing people how good she’ll be at the job. Combined they’re quite the power couple.”

Mon-el sighed.

“So I need to be more romantic?” he asked.

Kara fired and missed again, by a wide margin. She made a frustrated noise.

“No, I think you’re doing well as you are,” Nia said giving Kara a concerned look. 

“Here you need to relax,” Mon-el said getting up and coming over. 

Mon-el helped Kara position her arms standing close as he helped her draw it back. It was slightly better that time clipping the hoop. He smiled at her handing over another arrow and this time standing directly behind her his hands covering hers as Nia continued her commentary.

“People like you two as the playful new couple. You’re doing well, they just have a different appeal. While you two were making friends with every food vendor in Krypton Lena was at an art gallery helping shine a spotlight on Kryptonian art and culture and then after Jack took her for ice cream and there’s a thousand pictures of them holding hands and-”

Mon-el’s hand touched Kara’s waist and she twitched as she fired. The arrow was wildly off hitting a statue and shooting through a hedge. A surprised yell came from the other side and Kara froze. 

“Sorry!” Kara yelled.

Mon-el stepped back with a sigh and a second later Lena and Jack appeared in the archway.

“You know if you want me dead there are easier ways to do it,” Lena joked.

Kara sighed heavily lowering her bow giving Lena a pained look.

“I got that on camera by the way,” Jack said holding up his phone. “Way to undercut my being romantic. I was halfway through Vona Gal-Ax’s third love sonnet when you made me scream like a little kid,” he said giving Kara a smile.

“Sorry Jack,” she said giving him an apologetic look. 

Lena was watching her concerned.

“You okay?” she asked.

Kara groaned indicating the number of arrows littering the lawn around the target. 

“I need to be able to shoot a flaming arrow through the hoop but I can’t even get close,” she said with another frustrated sigh. “I used to be good at this. I know the technique but I can’t seem to-”

“Hey,” Lena said gently cutting her off. “It’s okay you’ll get it.”

Kara gave her a small smile.

“She’s too tense,” Mon-el said giving Kara an affectionate pat on the shoulder. 

Lena looked at Kara and gave her a small smile coming closer.

“You just need a good luck charm,” Lena said.

She gave Kara a small smile and Nia reached for her Nerf gun as Lena came over and gave Kara’s shoulder a squeeze.

“Here,” she said removing the ribbon around her neck. “This was my mothers. It’s the only thing I have of hers.”

She held up the ribbon so Kara could see the writing on it.

“But importantly for you right now, it’s a sacred ribbon that’s been blessed for good luck.”

She took a step closer and Kara felt herself become hyper aware of how close Lena was as she tied the ribbon around her wrist.

“Now try. It might take a few times for the ribbon to work but well…” Lena gave her a smile. “You’ve always been a quick learner with a little luck.”

Kara blushed a faint shade of pink at the reminder of Lena teaching her poker. They hadn’t bet with chips. She accepted the arrow Lena handed her. She looked at Lena once more and then raised the bow aiming again. She let go and the arrow hit the target just inside the hoop. 

Alex, Nia, Jack, and Mon-el applauded loudly. Kara looked at Lena and gave her a grateful smile.

“Like I said, you just needed a little luck,” Lena said still smiling at her. Kara nodded returning the smile. “You can wear it tomorrow for the ceremony.”

“Thank you.”

Kara hugged Lena tightly. Lena hugged her back the two of them holding each other as tight as they could breathing deeply. They were immediately shot by Nia. Kara sighed and they separated in time to see Alex handing Nia another chocolate.

“Seriously Alex, as a doctor aren’t you supposed to be anti sweets or something?” Kara asked.

“That’s dentists,” Alex said with a smirk. “And this is for your own good. You two are not allowed to hug, or touch, or anything else so…” 

Alex motioned with her hands and they separated further with near identical sighs of exasperation.

“Why aren’t they allowed to touch?” Mon-el asked.

Alex and Nia sighed.

“Because they can’t touch without looking gayer than a rainbow,” Alex said.

Mon-el nodded.

“Yeah it did look a little… intense,” he said making a vague motion with his hands.

Lena laughed and Kara looked at her empty quiver. 

“Well can you blame us,” Lena said her smile disappearing to be replaced with an almost pleading expression. “We’re not allowed to do anything together and we miss each other.”

Alex gave Lena a look then softened a little at Lena’s sad expression.

“You can do stuff together you’re just not allowed to touch,” she said throwing up her hands.

Lena seemed to think for a moment then she looked at Kara with a somewhat hopeful expression.

“Jack and I were going to go to the opera to see the opening of Astran.”

“The slug slayer?” Mon-el asked confused.

“The very one,” Lena said giving him a smile. “Someone made an opera about his story and it’s apparently quite good.”

She turned back to Kara with a smile.

“If you have nothing else planned tonight you could join Jack and I in the royal box.”

“How come you’re going to be in the royal box?” Kara asked.

“Because Lena asked the Queen if we could join her,” Jack said.

“Astra’s going?” Kara asked surprised.

“Well she is now,” Lena said laughing. “I ran into her in the gardens and asked if she thought it would be inappropriate for Jack and I to use the seats and she said it probably would be, but not if she came too. Eliza apparently hasn’t seen Kryptonian opera so she’s coming as well.”

Kara looked at Lena surprised.

“I thought she was going to invite you and Mon-el as well but she might have-”

Astra strode through the archway at that moment followed by Eliza who was carrying a bouquet of flowers.

“Ah lovely you already invited them,” Astra said smiling at them.

“Well she was just informing me you were all going so I guess we are too unless I have another commitment, Winn? Winn!”

Winn rose from behind the bench looking a bit dishevelled from his nap in the grass.

“Yes your highness?” he asked with a yawn.

“Do I have time to go to the opera tonight?” she asked.

He nodded and lay down again.

“You have the whole evening clear,” he said with a yawn.

Kara smiled at Lena. 

“Excellent. Mon-el, Alex do you want to come?”

“I should let you know there’s only six seats in the box,” Astra said. "So you can bring one extra and anyone else will have to take the secondary box which doesn’t have as good a view.”

Kara and Alex exchanged a look.

“Oh no, the box with the slightly less advantageous view,” Alex said sarcastically. “How awful.”

Astra chuckled.

“I uh, I don’t really like the opera,” Mon-el said. “Why don’t you all go and I’ll take the chance to catch up with the lads instead.”

“Sounds good,” Kara said giving him a quick smile. “Alex?”

“Well someone has to sit between you and Lena,” Alex said with a smile. “Unless Nia or Winn want to go.”

“No thank you!” Winn called curling up in the grass again.

Nia shook her head.

“Brainy is taking me to see it on Friday.”

Alex turned to smile at Kara.

“Well what time and what’s the dress code?”

“Fancy and we should leave in an hour,” Lena said. 

They all broke up with a lot of smiles and Kara gave the waiting attendant an apology for the spread of arrows. He just smiled back and suggested she ask Astra how many she’d lost practising.

In the car on the way to the opera Kara discovered it was in excess of a hundred. Astra was apparently terrible at archery. Which made Kara feel better as she handed Lena her mother’s ribbon back.

The opera was very over the top but very pretty. The costumes in particular were stunning. Kara enjoyed it, mostly because she was out with Lena and they could mutter jokes to each other throughout. Much to Alex’s irritation as she was the one positioned between them at all times. 

After the opera they all returned to the palace opening a few bottles of wine and Astra regaled them all with stories of dignitaries making asses of themselves as they sat in the gardens. Alex was still seated between Kara and Lena just in case but they got to talk to each other still. 

Jack eventually took pity on Alex as Kara explained the plot of Disney’s the little mermaid to Lena. He traded places with Alex jumping in with his own additions about the original myth. At midnight, and probably after she realised Kara and Lena were moving closer with every minute despite the supposed obstacle of Jack between them, Alex ordered them all to bed despite protest from Lena and Kara.

“Come on you have a long day of shaking hands and talking about the weather at the party tomorrow. You both need your sleep,” she said ushering them out of their chairs. 

There was much pouting and protest but she was unmoved and they all got up. Astra and Eliza excepted because no one told the Queen when to go to bed and as Eliza was explaining something to her she was excepted too. Or so Astra insisted as she poured herself and the laughing Eliza another glass of wine. Kara sighed and gave Lena a look.

“When I’m queen I totally get to use that excuse to stay up late,” she said.

Lena laughed and pointed at herself. 

“Me too.”

Kara smiled and moved to kiss her but Alex put a hand on her face stepping between them.

“And no more getting drunk by either of you. You can barely control yourselves normally.”

Kara and Lena tried to explain how they hadn’t actually touched so they were doing much better drunk than sober as Alex dragged Kara off to bed and Lena was carried away by a laughing Jack.

 

DAY 7

The day of the party Mon-el was waiting outside Kara's room to escort her. She smiled accepting his help as she descended the stairs trying not to stumble as she thought about opening the party. Mon-el gave her a reassuring hug but her anxiety didn’t ease until she saw Lena standing near the bow. Their eyes met and she saw Lena open her hand to show the ribbon. 

Kara was still nervous as she went to pick up the bow but Lena was there and she felt the ribbon wrap around her wrist and tighten as Lena tied the knot. Kara looked up and Lena winked. The whole action took mere seconds and was conveniently hidden by Jack stepping forwards to give Mon-el a hug in greeting.

Kara smiled and turned away taking her place. Mon-el gave her a kiss on the cheek and she gave him a slightly confused smile.

“For luck,” he said with a wink. 

She nodded smiling and lifted the bow aiming carefully. Her eyes moved to the ribbon around her wrist. She breathed out and fired. The arrow flew perfectly and the circle ignited. There was a round of applause and Mon-el quickly had his arm around her. She smiled up at him then found her eyes shifting to seek out Lena’s. She saw Lena’s smile and there was a small moment then Jack said something pulling Lena away.

“Seriously Kara. Could you please try not to look so lovesick,” Alex whispered.

Kara sighed and nodded. She turned to Mon-el and did her best to focus on him. And she succeeded, right up until ten minutes later when she and Lena ran into each other again at the buffet table. Kara removed the ribbon and handed it back to Lena with a grateful smile. Lena returned the smile as Jack carefully tied the ribbon around Lena’s neck. 

“Rivals,” Alex muttered walking past with a plate of food.

Kara sighed scrunching up her face in frustration. She looked at Lena with a silent plea for help. Lena smiled and grabbed Jack wrapping herself around his side.

“Hey Kara did you know that Jack was working on a cure for cancer?” she said loudly.

“No, I didn’t,” Kara said smiling brightly. She grabbed Mon-el pulling him close. “Did you know Mon-el helped raise 12 million dollars for the research into new Asthma treatments?”

Lena pretended to be surprised.

“No I didn’t,” she said widening her eyes. “Did you know Jack was part of the team who helped design the new trains for the London Underground?”

“No, did you know Mon-el was an ambassador for youth sports in Krypton-”

“No I didn’t. Did you know Jack helped develop a new heart monitor for-”

“Weren’t you involved in that too?” Kara asked sweetly. “And that new smart inhaler.”

Jack laughed as Lena sighed.

“Hey Mon-el,” Jack said still smiling.

“Yes Jack?” Mon-el asked.

“While they play my horse is bigger than yours why don’t we both get a drink?”

Mon-el laughed and nodded.

“Sure man, sounds good.”

“My horse is definitely bigger,” Lena muttered with a small pout.

Jack laughed.

“Yes he is,” Jack said giving her a kiss on the cheek.

Mon-el immediately took a step back to copy the gesture with Kara. Kara smiled at Lena who laughed.

“We’re really bad at being rivals aren’t we,” Lena said.

Kara nodded.

“So-” 

Lena was cut off by the arrival of her mother. 

“Lena dear you simply must speak with the Prime Minister. He’s such a delight and his son is so interested in your work on the environmental protection act.”

Lena gave Kara a look as she was dragged away. Kara sighed watching her go and turned around to see Eliza coming over. Eliza wrapped an arm around Kara’s shoulders.

“You did great,” Eliza said.

Kara smiled.

“Thanks. Just needed a little luck I guess.”

Mon-el returned with two glasses of chapagne and smiled at Eliza as he handed Kara one.

“Shall we circulate before food?” he asked.

Kara smiled and nodded. Eliza gave her shoulder a squeeze then smiled at Mon-el.

Kara didn’t see Lena again until her third circuit of the party as she shook hands with the Prime Minister nodded briefly to his son and pretended she was being signalled by someone to make a hasty retreat across the wooden bridge over the pond. 

She smiled to a couple of people she vaguely recognised asking about their families. Lena was standing nearby checking something on her phone. Kara smiled and excused herself to go find where she’d lost Mon-el. She saw Mxy rapidly approaching and quickly hurried onwards pretending not to have seen him. 

Then she stumbled, then she tripped, and she had one panicked moment to realise which direction she was falling before she crashed into Lena sending them both plunging into the pond below. 

They quickly surfaced gasping and shaking wet hair out of their faces.

“Lena I’m so sorry,” Kara said quickly.

Lena glared at her as she spat out dirty pond water.

“I just slipped and then-”

Lena splashed her. For a moment Kara was stunned then she glared at Lena and splashed her back. They glared at each other for a moment then it was a full on battle. The two of them splashing each other moving steadily closer laughing and shrieking in the freezing cold water.

“Oi!” Alex yelled from the bank. They turned to look at her. “Yes you two, the idiots in the pond. Get out of the water it’s full of fish shit.”

Kara looked at Lena and she nodded. No discussion needed to be had. She knew what they must do.

“Well when you put it like that,” Lena called smiling at Alex.

“She does make an excellent point about the fish,” Kara said as they approached the bank and Alex.

They got to the edge and Kara held a hand out to Alex. 

“Give us a hand will you?” she said as Lena dragged herself out next to Alex.

“So you can pull me in no-”

Lena jumped on Alex and Kara grabbed her the two of them combined dragging Alex into the pond with much yelling. The now three way water fight raged as Alex yelled about her phone only for Kara to jump on her as Lena splashed them both laughing.

“Lena get out of that pond!” Lillian yelled.

Lena pretended not to hear as she helped Kara dunk Alex under the water.

“You know there’s eels in there,” Astra called from the bank smiling at them.

There was a moment of silence then Kara and Lena were racing out of the water as fast as they could while Alex sighed and started wading slowly to the bank. 

Jack and Mon-el had both acquired towels and quickly wrapped them around the now shivering pair. Mon-el held Kara in his arms as Jack held up Lena’s phone. She’d luckily dropped it on the bridge not in the water. Astra sighed looking at them both and offered a towel to Alex as she emerged glaring at them. 

Kara stuck out her tongue and Alex sighed.

“You two,” Alex said pointing a finger. “Are a menace.”

“Real eloquent there Alex,” Lena said smirking.

Kara snickered as Alex glared at Lena. 

“Shut it Luthor,” Alex growled as Astra wrapped the towel around her shoulders.

“Make me,” Lena said.

Kara shrugged off Mon-el’s arms and moved to stand beside Lena the two of them silently daring Alex. She glowered back.

“I will get you two for this,” she muttered.

“Come on then,” they said squaring up. 

“Children,” Astra said smiling at Kara and Lena as she rubbed Alex’s hair with the towel. “Go clean yourselves up and get changed. Alex when you come back you can have cake since you were a victim of their antics.”

Alex looked very smug as she looked at the horrified Kara and Lena.

“She totally started it,” Kara said turning her infamous pout on Astra.

“Yeah, she called us idiots,” Lena added.

Astra looked at the two of them.

“A queen does not rise to such bait,” she said sinking every bit of queenly dignity into her voice. “We must treat other people with respect and dignity at all times. Not dunk them in ponds for petty name calling.”

Kara and Lena exchanged a look. 

“But… it’s Alex,” Kara mumbled. “She’s my sister, mot a person.”

Lena nodded quickly.

“Siblings totally count as an exception to the people rule,” Lena said.

Astra laughed and shook her head at them. 

“Lena I would hope you wouldn’t do such a thing to your brother,” Lillian said glaring at her.

“Of course not mother,” Lena said with false sincerity.

Kara snickered.

“You’d have lured him to the edge screamed eels and shoved him in,” Kara said.

Lena and Jack laughed.

“You know her so well,” Jack said smiling at Kara.

Kara beamed back. Astra sighed looking at them. 

“You two,” she said shaking her head. “Just... go get cleaned up.”

“Can we have cake too?” Kara asked giving Astra her best puppy dog eyes.

Lena quickly added hers.

“Please,” they begged.

Astra looked skyward.

“Rao help me,” she said. “Fine, but please, a little more dignity in future. From both of you.”

“We promise!” they said already racing off towards the palace.

Astra smiled watching them go. 

Notes for the Chapter:

As always let me know what you thought down in the comments and I'll see you next time. Stay safe y'all.

Next time:
Sam, Jack, and Lena wear matching hoodies for sad reasons and there's pretty horses.

6. Horses

Notes for the Chapter:

Because this is a princess story I had to get in a pretty horse or two, it's the rules. So any of you who don't know what a Friesian horse is I recommend you do a quick google search so you know just how big and pretty they are.

TW: References to Lex's death. Lena is going to be reading his suicide note and having feels about it, if that's something that might be hard for you please skip all of day 8. I've tried to make it so you won't miss anythig beyond some extra character bonding and sweetness if you skip those parts.

DAY 8

Lena sat in her rooms in the palace going over the latest reports. She rubbed her eyes and looked at the picture she’d placed at the edge of the desk. It was the one of her and Lex at the Krypton 100. She forced herself to keep trying to read but her eyes kept returning to the picture. Maybe she would be brave enough today.

After ten minutes she sighed and put down her pen reaching for the picture frame. She flipped the picture over and removed the back. Behind the picture was the letter. Lena took a deep breath and removed the paper opening the crinkled pages. 

She knew the first few paragraphs by heart.

Dearest sister, 

I am sorry I cannot stay, but know there is nothing in this world that has made me prouder or happier than being your big brother. Ever since you came to us you have made my world so much brighter.

I wish I could see you take on the world next year, I know you’ll do great. If I can watch from the next life I’ll be there cheering you on. When you hear the crowd know I’m there cheering the loudest, as always.

She tried to read on but her eyes wouldn’t let her. Tears fell from her face to the page and Lena quickly turned away lest she smudge the ink. She pushed the letter away as tears started rolling down her face. She couldn’t stay. She couldn’t bear to look at his final words again. She wasn’t ready. 

She forced herself up and out into the gardens moving quickly her head down as she tried to put as much distance between herself and the pages as she could. 

She walked right into someone.

“I’m sor-”

“Lena?” Kara asked worried.

Lena sniffed and looked up at the worried Kara. She took a deep breath holding tight to Kara using her to pull herself back to the present.

“I was-” She choked back more tears. “Trying to read Lex’s letter.”

Kara hugged her tight and Lena sank into the familiar embrace. They heard a door open nearby and Kara quickly guided Lena away before anyone saw them. Lena wasn’t really aware of where they were going until they returned to her room and Kara was gently guiding her to her chair again before cautiously taking up the pages and folding them again moving to return them to the picture.

Lena caught her hand.

“Please,” she whispered.

Kara froze.

“What do you want me to do?” she asked.

“Read the next bit for me. I- I can’t.”

Kara nodded unfolding the pages again.

“Where did you-”

“Same place as last time,” Lena whispered.

Kara gripped her hand tightly as she took a breath.

“I’m sorry I’ll never see you get your degree, or degrees knowing you. I’m sorry I won’t be there to drag you from your dorm on adventures like I said I would, but I’m sure you’ll make friends who will.”

Kara gripped Lena’s hand tighter as she sobbed against Kara’s shoulder. Kara held her tighter with one arm.

“I’m sorry I won’t be there to watch you fall in love for the first time. It’ll probably be a disaster and you will inevitably have your heart broken. But I promise one day you’re going to fall in love with someone and they’re going to fall in love right back and you’re going to get married-”

“Stop,” Lena begged. “Stop please. I can’t.”

She clung to Kara as she cried. She couldn’t hear her brother tell her how happy he would be at her wedding. Not now. Not yet. Kara seemed to understand as she wrapped her arms around Lena holding her as she cried the two of them sinking to the floor by her desk.

Lena wasn’t sure how long it was before the door opened and Sam’s voice reached her ears.

“What-”

“Lex,” Kara said in explanation. 

Then Sam was there holding her too. 

“It’s okay sweetheart. We’re here.”

Lena remained on the floor clinging to Kara as Sam held her and then sometime later Jack was there too. She heard him pick up the paper and put it back in the frame. The final click telling her the picture was back in place. Then he carefully removed her fingers from Kara’s shirt and wiped her face with a handkerchief.

“Hey there darling,” he said softly. 

She gave him a small smile. 

“We’re here, it’s okay. How far did you get this time?” he asked.

“Wedding,” Lena whispered.

Jack smiled at her.

“So all these tears are because you think he’d hate me that much? I’m not that ugly am I? At least I have hair.”

Lena found herself smiling then laughing.

“Kara back me up here, I’m not that awful am I?” Jack asked.

Kara chuckled.

“I don’t know Jack, you do hate Gilbert and Sullivan. That’s a pretty big-”

“Hey!” Jack protested. “You sing I am the very model of a modern Major General two hundred times and see how fond of them you are.”

Lena, Kara, and Sam all laughed and the door opened as Alex stuck her head in.

“Hey have you seen- Oh.”

She looked at the four of them cuddled together on the floor.

“What uh…”

Jack grinned.

“Welcome we’re just about to start the orgy, the more the merrier,” Jack declared.

Alex’s horrified face sent the four of them into a fit of giggles. Alex sighed stepping into the room and closing her eyes.

“I’m just going to erase that image from my memory, going, going, gone.”

She sighed in relief and looked at them her eyes sweeping over Lena then Kara’s tear stained shirt. She gave Lena an apologetic look.

“I’m sorry to break up this up but Kara’s apparently due at a press guild ball tonight and Winn’s turning the palace upside down trying to find her because Cat Grant will murder him if she’s late."

Kara sighed heavily and looked at Lena. Lena gave her a small smile.

“I’ll be okay,” she said. “Jack and Sam will look after me.”

Kara still hesitated. 

“Don’t worry Princess,” Sam said gently. “We’ve got her.”

Kara sighed and pressed a soft kiss to Lena’s head then she looked at Jack.

“Call me if…”

Jack smiled and nodded.

“I promise.”

Kara looked at Lena again then got up with a final squeeze of her shoulder. Jack took her place. Lena watched Kara leave and Alex gave her an apologetic look. Lena looked at her two best friends.

“I vote we all put on something comfy but stylish and watch trash,” Jack said.

“Can there be alcohol too?” Lena asked.

“Yes but no more than two glasses because we have to be up bright and early tomorrow to attend a breakfast, also with Cat Grant.”

Lena groaned.

“Sam?”

“Don’t look at me for help. I’m a single parent. I haven’t gotten drunk without a week of preplanning for years. Hangovers with a child are torture.”

Lena let them pull her up off the floor and then Sam was digging through her clothes and then the three of them were curled in her bed wearing their matching Argo University hoodies and watching some competitive cooking show. Lena sighed letting her friends comfort her. She still felt something was missing. She couldn’t work out what until Kara stuck her head in to wish her goodnight and smiled. 

“You three look amazingly comfy,” Kara said smiling at them. “Can I take a picture?”

They nodded and Kara stood at the end of the bed for a moment with her phone. Lena’s phone chimed as Kara lowered hers.

“I sent you the pictures.”

Lena smiled.

“Thank you,” she said. 

They both knew it was about more than the pictures. Kara gave her a small smile.

“Anytime.”

For a moment they just looked at each other then Kara sighed.

“Goodnight Lena.”

“Goodnight Kara.”

Kara left.

“Goodnight Kara!” Jack and Sam called.

Kara stuck her head back in.

“Goodnight Jack. Goodnight Sam.”

They waved exuberantly at her. Kara smiled back and disappeared closing the door behind her.

Lena snuggled down with her friends and let her eyes drift lower. They’d all be complaining about their backs tomorrow but she felt safe and warm and loved. She knew they’d stay all night with her. They always did. She heard Jack scoff at something on the show and Sam voice her agreement as her eyes closed. 

As Lena drifted off some part of her whispered how Kara had stayed with her too when she’d read the letter to her.

 

DAY 10
