Lena scoffed looking down at her phone. The Kryptonian Tribune still had a picture of them fighting in the water as their main story with the words Queen fight! underneath. The Argo Post wasn’t much better having gone with the headline Battle of the tiaras. Lena muttered to herself closing the news app and almost walked right into Mon-el as he exited the stables.

“Oh sorry,” he said giving her a quick smile.

She smiled back and looked around spotting Kara off in the small exercise yard.

“No problem, what’s Kara doing?” she asked watching her ride in a slow figure eight.

“She’s practising for the inspection tomorrow afternoon,” Mon-el said with a shrug. “Not sure why, she’s only got to ride in a straight line for a minute but I guess that’s Kara for you. She can never do things the easy way.”

Lena laughed softly and nodded.

“How long has she been practising?”

“Over an hour,” Mon-el said with a shrug. “We were chatting but there’s only so many times a guy can watch a horse circle a paddock talking about musicals before he goes crazy. Anyway I’m gonna go find some food. Talk to you later.”

“Later,” Lena echoed her eyes on Kara. 

She cautiously looked around them and saw a few photographers by the far fence but they appeared to be packing up. Lena smiled and put her phone away making her way over to the fence and Kara. 

“So is this your horse,” Lena said watching Kara gently turn in another figure eight.

Kara smiled and nodded watching Lena climb up on the fence.

“Technically she’s Astra's as she was the one who bought her for me,” Kara said with a shrug. “She was born in Midvale to one of our neighbours when Astra was visiting and since I really liked her Astra decided to buy her for me. I was very excited at the time and Astra foolishly said I got to name her. Being fourteen I named her after my favourite singer.”

“So it’s NSYNC the horse?”

“Singer, not group.” Kara gave Lena a look. “It’s Britney.”

Lena laughed softly.

“Well what’s your horse called?” Kara asked pouting. 

Lena coughed and felt a blush creeping up her cheeks. Kara waited and Lena mumbled something.

“Oh come on, it can’t be that bad,” Kara said smiling at Lena as she passed.

Lena sighed.

“PC.”

“As in a computer?” Kara asked.

“As in Prince Charming,” Lena admitted.

Kara started laughing.

“Oh Rao, really?”

“Shut up!” Lena’s face was quickly turning pink. “I was ten. Lex was teasing me that I’d never get to kiss a Prince Charming like in the stories so I named my horse Prince Charming. I called him Prince for a bit but by thirteen he was PC. Unless Lex was talking about him, he would always call him by his full name just to tease me.”

Kara was still laughing.

“I’m feeling very judged right now,” Lena said trying to sound annoyed.

Kara looked at her with a smile.

“Is PC here?”

Lena sighed and smiled at Kara.

“He’s due to arrive early tomorrow morning. Your aunt was kind enough to let him be stabled here for the next month.”

“Good. I remember you saying you miss him when you’re away,” Kara said turning a little to smile properly at Lena.

“I do, he’s a great listener,” Lena said smiling back.

They fell silent for a minute, Lena leaning on the fence watching Kara as she did circuits with Britney.

There was a flash of distant lightning and Britney tossed her head pulling the reins from Kara’s hand.

“Easy girl, easy,” Kara said reaching forwards to soothe her as she continued to turn skittering to the side as the rumble of thunder filled the air.

“Hey Lena can you?” Kara asked.

Lena jumped the fence and approached her hands out. Combined they calmed Britney enough that Lena could get ahold of the reins and lead her towards the fence so Kara could get down. There was another flash of lightning and a loud clap of thunder but Lena kept a firm grip on the reins as Britney tossed her head.

Kara got down and spent a minute calming Britney with Lena beside her.

“Thanks for that,” Kara said. “She’s a bit flighty but fast and I don’t have a lot of control riding side saddle. Especially since she seems to take most direction less as instruction and more as a request at the best of times.”

Lena laughed softly giving Britney a pat.

“That’s okay. I remember when I would practice side saddle with PC. Thankfully he’s as solid as a rock and very obedient so I never had to have great control.”

Kara laughed softly and Lena handed her the reins. For a moment they looked at each other both wishing they could kiss. Kara glanced over and noticing one of the photographers was still there sighed. They had their camera pointed at them. 

“I should probably let you finish up before Nia comes along and shoots us for being too close,” Lena said giving Kara a sad smile.

Kara laughed softly and sighed.

“Yeah, she does seem to love doing that. I blame Alex and her chocolate bribery.”

Lena laughed looking at Kara for a long moment the urge to kiss her almost overpowering. She sighed taking a step backwards.

“Goodnight Kara.”

“Goodnight Lena, sweet dreams.”

Lena gave her one last smile and left. Kara watched her go with a sigh. Britney nudged her gently and Kara turned her attention back to her horse.

“You know you’re a terrible nuisance right?” she said scratching her shoulder.

Britney did not seem to agree as she happily let Kara return her to the stable and brush her down.

 

DAY 11

“We can get a farrier out but she’s in a bit of a mood your highness,” the groom said apologetically as Kara looked at Britney.

She was indeed in a bit of a mood. Kara shook her head at her horse. Of course the morning of her troop inspection Britney, who had been as happy as can be all week, decided to throw a tantrum.

“You are such a flighty girl no matter how well you’re treated,” Kara said shaking her head. “Is this payback for your name? I was fourteen you can’t hold it against me still.” 

“Hey Kara, what’s happening?” Lena asked.

Kara turned towards Lena and tried not to drool as Lena approached in her riding gear. Her eyes drifted lower for a moment to admire the way the riding pants clung to Lena’s legs. There was a small cough from Lena and Kara corrected her wandering eyes. She smiled at Lena in greeting then noticed Jack not far behind her looking decidedly English in tweed riding gear. She quickly gave Jack a smile too. He waved back.

“Britney decided she doesn’t want to have her day in the sun and threw a shoe,” Kara said gesturing. “And now she’s throwing a tantrum, aren’t you sweetheart?” 

Britney huffed and turned away stomping around her stall. Lena laughed softly as Kara pouted.

“If you need a replacement horse you can use PC. He arrived this morning and I just took him out for a short ride so he’s in a great mood,” Lena said. “He’s comfortable with a sidesaddle and like I said last night he’s as steady as a rock. Considering the only people who ride him except me are children I can say with certainty that he’ll take direction well.”

Kara gave Britney one last look and sighed.

“Sure, may as well give him a shot. He won’t have a problem with a ridiculously heavy dress will he? I’m wearing something like twenty kilos of fabric.”

Lena laughed loudly and Jack snorted.

“PC will have no problem with you, or your dress,” Lena said smiling.

Kara gave her a look and the groom nearby smiled.

“Lead on,” Kara said with a wave of her hand. “Let’s go meet this Prince of yours” 

Jack chuckled and Lena rolled her eyes leading Kara back out of the stables to where two horses stood by a water trough. One was a pretty thoroughbred the other a very large and magnificent looking friesian. Kara sighed. She had no doubt which was Lena’s horse.

“Where’s my sweet boy?” Lena cooed. 

The friesian lifted his head and tried to go towards her but was prevented by his lead rope attached to the water trough. Lena went over and untied him bringing the beautiful giant over. Kara looked from PC to the grinning Lena and back again shaking her head.

“This is your Prince Charming?” Kara asked looking at PC as he towered above Lena.

“When we were kids Lex teased me for riding a pony. So I wanted a horse that would be taller than his so I could win my horse is bigger than your horse,” Lena said stroking PC’s nose. “Dad got me PC. Lex was very jealous. Though he always pointed out Ignatius was faster.”

“And what was your pony was called?” Kara asked moving closer to give PC a friendly pat.

“He was a family horse, and he was called Pumpkin.”

“That is adorable,” Kara said grinning at her.

Jack chuckled as Lena glared at Kara.

“Is not.”

“Is too.”

Lena stuck her tongue out at Kara who just laughed.

“So, would you like to give him a test ride?” Lena asked. 

Kara nodded smiling. His saddle was quickly changed and a step was brought so Kara could seat herself while Lena continued to lavish affection on PC who soaked it up with evident delight. 

Five minutes in the saddle Kara knew PC would be perfect. She brought him to a halt at the edge of the practice field.

“He’s perfect, thank you,” Kara said.

Lena gave her a smile.

“I’m glad to hear it. I’ll leave you to it. I’m going to go shower and change. I’ve got some schmoozing to do before the inspection.” She gave Kara one more smile. “I’ll see you both later.”

Kara nodded and smiled back. Lena turned and started walking out. PC tried to follow her but Lena shook her head giving him a gentle push.

“Later PC, I’ll give you a sugar cube after you show everyone what a good boy you are and escort Kara to see the troops.” 

Lena ducked under the fence and with a final wave headed off to join Jack who was waiting by the stables. Kara easily turned PC’s head away to keep practising. 

 

Kara was assisted into the saddle by two attendants. Help she was grateful for in the heavy dress. Whoever decided the inspection of the troops must always be done in full royal regalia was an idiot as far as she was concerned. The corset she’d quickly realised was indispensable as it let her sit comfortably even if she couldn’t actually bend over in it.

PC didn’t even blink at the extra weight. The attendants finished arranging her dress around her and she got comfortable in the saddle. She was handed PC’s reins and everyone did their final checks. J’onn gave her a nod to signal they were ready. Kara clicked her tongue and PC started walking. She smiled imagining a young Lena riding him. He moved with such a sure and steady tread Kara couldn’t help but feel charmed by the gentle giant.

“Nothing has ever stopped you has it,” she said giving him a gentle pat. 

They made the turn onto the path for the inspection. The order was given by Brainy and the Kryptonian troops came to attention in their red and gold dress uniforms to her right. Kara straightened a little in the saddle smiling at the troops as she passed and glanced over to see the gathered citizens who’d taken the walk across the fields to watch from behind the fence to her left. She looked back at the troops then at the crowd of dignitaries ahead. They included Lena seated between Jack and Alex. As she got closer Kara gave Lena a small smile and got one in return. 

Jack said something in Lena’s ear making her laugh. Lena turned to him saying something in return. Kara wished she could hear what they were saying but she had an inspection to do so she focused on the troops opposite. Then as though acting on her wishes PC’s ears perked up and he turned his head towards Lena. Kara quickly tried to turn him away but he ignored her as he walked determinedly towards Lena.

“No PC,” Kara said trying to turn him towards the middle of the path again. “We have to inspect the troops first.”

She was however unable to prevent her smile at his obvious excitement at locating Lena. There was a chuckles from several people watching and J’onn came forward to try and turn his head for her but PC pushed right through J’onn and her efforts to dissuade him as the crowd laughed louder. PC stopped in front of Lena who was smiling and trying to look apologetic as she covered her face pretending she couldn’t see PC while Jack and Alex laughed beside her.

PC huffed and nudged Lena with his head.

“Lena,” Kara said trying not to laugh herself. “Do something about your horse.”

Lena gently pushed PC away he turned in a circle then went right back to her causing the crowd to laugh again. PC stomped his feet and Kara kept trying to gently coax him away with one hand. Lena sighed heavily and got up. She climbed over the short fence taking hold of PC’s bridle and led him back onto the path then tried to leave but he turned again following her.

Kara was too busy laughing at this point to try and stop him as Lena turned once more leading him back to his spot. She took two steps back before PC followed her again.

“I think PC might be trying to make a point,” Kara said. 

Lena looked up at her with an apologetic look. 

“I’m so sorry,” she said.

Kara smiled shaking her head.

“Just lead him,” she said smiling as she tossed the reins to Lena.

Lena sighed giving PC a pat before taking hold of the reins and leading him the rest of the way past the troops. She and Kara were trying to behave but they kept looking at each other and laughing. Kara could see the various soldiers all trying to keep their faces straight as the inspection continued.

They made it to the end of the line and the call was made for the troops to be dismissed. Lena held PC in place giving him a sugar cube so Kara could get down.

A reporter Kara recognised as Lur-Var from the Argo Post came over quickly followed by a camera. Kara and Lena exchanged a look and then they were both laughing and Lena was rubbing PC’s forehead.

“Impossible horse,” she said. “I told her you were such a good boy and then you went and did that.”

“That was a bit of an unusual inspection we must say,” Lur-Var said grinning at them both. 

“I swear he’s normally much better behaved,” Lena said quickly. “Whenever I’m not in the country he’s stabled at the Luthor Children’s Hospital and used as a therapy horse.”

“He just missed you,” Kara said giving Lena a smile.

Lena sighed and PC sniffed at her pocket.

“That’s all you get when you misbehave,” Lena said glaring at her horse. 

“So just to be clear this is your horse?” Lur-Var asked.

“Yes,” Lena admitted with a sigh. “Her highness was set to ride her own horse but she threw a shoe this morning and I know PC’s comfortable with a sidesaddle rider. Again I swear he’s normally a very good boy.”

Lena gently stroked PC's face giving Kara another apologetic smile. PC bent down and nudged at Lena’s pocket searching again for more sugar cubes.

“I’m sorry baby I don’t have any more,” Lena cooed holding out her empty hands for PC to sniff.

“Well I do,” Kara said pulling one out and offering it to PC.

PC nibbled it gently and swung his head towards Kara clearly more interested in her now.

“Ha, he likes me more now,” Kara said giving him a pat. “I guess he can be my Prince Charming now.”

Lena looked at her and scoffed.

“Well at least I didn’t name him Britney,” she said.

Kara gasped in mock outrage a hand over her heart. Their soon to be fiancees arrived. Mon-el slinging an arm around Kara’s waist with a smile. Jack took Lena’s hand then gave PC a fond pat. Kara turned to Mon-el.

“I do believe she just insulted my horse,” she said.

Mon-el nodded.

“Well I guess we need to duel, pistols at dawn Luthor,” he said with mock severity.

Lena scoffed and turned to Jack.

“I will of course be your second,” Jack said quickly.

Everyone laughed.

“Real brave dude,” Mon-el said cuddling closer to Kara.

“She’s a better shot,” Jack said smiling back.

“Really?” Kara asked.

“She was the reigning queen of the Argo University paintball club three years running,” Jack said.

Lena smirked and Mon-el laughed.

“Reigning king of paintball five years running,” he said pointing at himself.

“I’ll see you at dawn then,” Lena said smirking.

Mon-el stopped smiling as Lena turned and walked off with Jack in tow. She whistled and PC followed her. Kara laughed and watched them leave for a moment shaking her head. She turned to Mon-el with a bright smile.

“Well I hope you’re ready to get up early to defend my horse’s honour,” she said.

Mon-el looked at her in surprise.

“She won’t actually-”

Kara gave him a look. He froze looking a tiny bit nervous.

“Are you actually a paintball king?” Kara asked.

He hesitated then nodded quickly.

“Yeah totally.”

“Great!” Kara kissed his cheek. “I guess we’re getting up early tomorrow then.”

Mon-el nodded and Kara smiled at him trying not to laugh. He noticed something over her shoulder and gave her an apologetic look.

“Sorry babe I’ll be right back.”

He backed away and Kara turned smiling to Lur-Var.

“She’s going to crush him. But it’s really sweet that he’s going to try anyway,” she said.

Lur-Var laughed and Astra appeared calling Kara away.

Notes for the Chapter:

Well I hope you all enjoyed PC's efforts to bring our favourite heroines together. If you did let me know down in the comments and until next time, stay safe.

Next time:
There's a duel between Lena and Mon-el. Also they get to attend the Krypton 100.

7. Paintball and Cars

DAY 12

They were all up early the next morning Jack and Lena having gone all out and put on what looked to be pirate costumes with some of Lena's swords. Both wore a tricorn hat with a puffy white shirt and tight black pants. Jack had a brown coat with an abundance of buttons and Lena had chosen to wear a black velvet cape. Both had chosen to wear a black mask. They looked both ridiculous and very pretty at the same time. Kara was dearly wishing she’d thought to wear a costume too. While her plain red dress was simple enough that it could be considered costume-ish Mon-el’s t-shirt and jeans certainly weren't.

Jack carried the two paint ball guns in a polished wooden box and Winn was ready with the camera. They made a big production out of their preparations. Kara and Jack put on swords (Lena giving Kara hers) and the traditional Kryptonian red dueling capes Nia had brought with her. Kara was pleased at least one of her entourage had considered costuming.

Mon-el examined his paintball gun with mock seriousness as he looked over at Lena doing the same thing. Jack removed her cape with a flourish and stepped back.

“Not too late to back out Luthor,” Mon-el called. 

Lena’s lips twitched in a smile as she looked at Mon-el raising her chin in defiance.

“Never! A Luthor does not back down,” Lena declared.

“Don’t hit him in the face!” Kara yelled as she took her place beside Jack.

Lena nodded her agreement. Mon-el looked at her and Kara gave him a look back. He just shook his head smiling.

“Can I hit her in the face?” he asked.

“General rules of paintball say no face shots,” Lena reminded him.

Mon-el nodded his agreement and they turned standing back to back. Kara couldn’t help but admire the image the two of them in standing in the early dawn light Lena in costume and Mon-el standing tall. They pointed their guns skyward.

“One!” Nia called starting the count. 

They both took a step moving in the traditional Kryptonian spiral step until the count reached nine then turning and firing. Mon-el didn’t even get his gun around properly before Lena shot him. As Kara requested, not in the face but the chest knocking the wind out of him and causing him to drop his gun.

He fell down with a yell. Kara rushed forwards crying about how Lena had killed him as she bent over Mon-el pretending to weep over his body as Mon-el groaned in pain. Jack strode forwards and held out Lena’s cape for her. Lena looked over at Kara and started to laugh evilly as Kara continued to feign heartbreak.

“Oh wow that was good,” Kara said breaking character. “Where’d you learn your evil laugh?”

“Free lessons are given to all challengers for the throne,” Lena said with a shrug. “We practice every second Tuesday.”

Lena glanced at the camera Winn held still recording.

“Well I shall avenge my poor slain love,” Kara said dramatically drawing her sword.

Lena turned and drew Jack’s with a smile. He laughed and accepted the paintball gun she threw him. Lena held the sword in her left hand, she was a fencing champion it would be rude not to. Kara changed hands to her left as well and they faced off. Kara smiled.

“You seem a decent lady, I hate to kill you,” Kara said.

Lena laughed.

“You seem a decent lady, I hate to die.”

Kara grinned and they took their places trading a few hits slowly circling each other both grinning. 

“You’re using Bennetti’s defence against me,” Kara said.

“It seemed only fitting considering the… flat terrain,” Lena said with a smile.

They traded strikes for a minute Lena easily blocking and returning the attacks at a slow enough pace for Kara to block.

“I admit you are better than I am,” Kara said.

“Then why are you smiling?” Lena asked smiling pretty widely herself.

Kara tossed the sword to her right hand.

“I am not left handed.”

Lena backed up a little blocking Kara’s strikes before ducking behind her and swapping over to her right hand.

“I am not left handed either,” and to add to the drama she easily disarmed Kara sending her sword off into the nearby grass.

Kara looked at Lena then her sword.

“Really?” she asked. 

Lena laughed and shrugged lifting the tip of her sword.

“Get used to disappointment,” she said.

Kara moved to retrieve her sword but Lena stopped her by placing her sword at Kara’s throat. 

“Uh uh, I am not Westley,” Lena said.

Kara tried not to smile as she looked at Lena. Her hair had come partially loose in the fight and small wisps were flying around her face in the breeze. With the Kryptonian mountains and sunrise behind her she looked absolutely breathtaking. The loose white shirt only added to her appeal. 

Kara let out a breath as she smiled at Lena. Lena smiled back biting her lip clearly noticing Kara’s changing attention. Jack coughed and Lena straightened up pointing her sword at Kara as Winn moved closer with the camera.

“This is the end Princess. I have slain your love and now I shall slay you and uh- line?”

“Take the throne back,” Jack yelled.

“Yes I shall take the throne back and erase all knowledge of your family from the records. I- line”

“Will burn the records and salt the lands of your family.” 

“Yes that, and I shall execute all who dare whisper your name and uh…”

“Kill your men and take all your women for myself,” Jack said.

“Yes, that too. All the evil usurper things,” Lena said with a wave of her hand.

Kara did her best determined Queenly look.

“The house of El shall rise again,” she declared.

“Oh yeah?” Lena asked with a smirk. “You got a secret baby or something who’s gonna avenge you?” 

Kara looked at Winn and by extension the camera. She made a face silently shaking her head.

“Uh… oh wait I know how to get out of this,” Kara said excitedly.

Lena scoffed.

“This should be good,” she said smirking at the camera.

“Please, please I need to live,” Kara said softly her eyes looking up into Lena’s.

“Oh? And what’s so important for you here?” Lena asked.

“True love.”

Lena hesitated her eyes on Kara’s and she couldn’t help her smile as Kara looked at her. Lena opened her mouth and then closed it. She nodded and took her sword away.

“Well, that’s a pretty good reason,” Lena said.

She stepped away and looked over at Mon-el who had propped himself up on his elbows to watch their duel. 

“Oh and it looks like your true love is only mostly dead.”

Mon-el frowned confused.

“To Miracle Alex we go!” Kara yelled.

She ran over and whispered to Mon-el to play dead and then started trying to drag him but he was too heavy.

“Hey uh, a little help here,” she said.

So Lena, Jack, Kara, and Jess -still being filmed by Winn- carried Mon-el to the palace and someone was sent to get Alex. Alex came out looking very unimpressed in her pyjamas barely covered by a robe as she put a chocolate in Mon-el’s mouth to bring him back. He sat up smiling which prompted several cheers from the early morning group. 

“I live, do I get a kiss?” Mon-el asked.

“Yes Farm boy,” Kara declared kissing him quickly. 

The others applauded and cheered.

“Farm boy?” Mon-el asked frowning.

Kara looked at him shocked.

“You haven’t seen the Princess Bride have you?” Jack asked laughing.

Mon-el shook his head.

“Well you’re watching it now,” Kara said firmly. “It’s one of the best movies like… ever.”

Lena looked at Jack then at the table already set for their breakfast.

“Farm boy, pass me that pitcher,” she said.

Jack smiled and picked up an empty jug and bringing it to her.

“As you wish,” he said.

Lena smiled at him and they kissed. It was not deep but they made it look it. Jess and Nia aw’d and Kara sighed. She looked back at a grinning Mon-el. She smiled at him and kissed his cheek before moving further down the table to where the food was waiting. 

Mon-el followed her with a sigh. He was quiet through most of their early breakfast but Kara put it down to him just being tired and sore. He was given an ice pack for his chest but a large bruise was already forming. 

After breakfast Lena and Jack excused themselves apparently they had some work to do at Lena’s office in Argo before they attended a luncheon of some kind. Kara smiled and waved them off before turning to Mon-el.

“Shall we be lazy and watch the Princess Bride this morning before we attend the Argo guilds meeting?” she asked.

He sighed and nodded his eyes on the retreating pair.

“You okay?” Kara asked. “If you’re tired you can just go back to bed it’s no big-”

“Why’d she have to kill me?” he asked.

Kara laughed giving him a gentle pat.

“It was a duel. It’s kind of the point,” she said.

Mon-el huffed.

“Yeah but why did I have to be dead why couldn’t she just shoot me in the leg or something?”

“Maybe because she didn’t want to seriously hurt you before the race tomorrow,” Kara said. “If she hit your arm or leg you might not be able to race as well. Your chest hurts but it won’t hamper you as badly if it swells.”

Mon-el seemed to think about it and conceded with a nod and some muttering.

“Mon-el,” Kara said her tone changing to something more serious. “That whole thing was really good. It was funny, and people are going to love it. She even let you play the hero so you can’t really complain she was undermining your image. And well… you did go up against Lena knowing she’s very good at paintball. You had to know she would win.”

Mon-el looked at her then shrugged picking up some toast.

“It just- I didn’t know the movie so I couldn’t really join in,” he finished. “I felt left out.”

He gave her a sad look and Kara smiled giving him a kiss on the cheek.

“Well the next time we do a movie homage I promise it can be one you know.”

Mon-el smiled at her and gave her hand a small squeeze. 

“Thanks.”

Kara beamed back returning to her breakfast. 

“You’re still gonna have to watch the Princess Bride with me though.”

He laughed and nodded his usual good mood seemingly returning.

“We can do it right after breakfast,” he promised.

 

DAY 13

The day of the Krypton 100 was bright, clear, and hot. Perfect race weather apparently. Kara sent Mon-el a good luck text before she got up and showered. Nia had already picked a pale yellow sundress for her to wear. Apparently she wanted people to start making the connection between Kara and marriage hence the yellow. 

Kara dressed and headed down to breakfast after which she was dragged away almost immediately by Winn to go over the preparations for her coronation and wedding. She finally escaped after three hours to get a quick bite to eat before she was hurried into a car with J’onn to get to the race. 

Her arrival was covered by a dozen photographers and two cameras as she made her way onto the track and to the Kryptonian team. She shook hands with several grinning team members before greeting Mon-el with a hug and a quick kiss. She was getting more comfortable with kissing him even if it did feel a little awkward at first.

He gave her a team hat and she smiled putting it on. Nia would probably be shaking her head about it not really matching but Kara didn’t mind. She gave Mon-el a final kiss for luck before she was led away to find her seat. Thankfully it wasn’t a far walk. 

As crown princess she was sitting at the finish line with the other dignitaries. She had glanced at one of the large screen showing the seating at the finish line and saw Rhea sitting in a place of honour beside Astra with Eliza on her other side.

Kara smiled as she arrived and saw Lean beside the only empty seat. Lena was wearing a white sundress with a black belt and very bold red lipstick. Lena turned at Kara’s arrival her mouth turning up in a bright smile as she moved a program off the empty seat beside her. Kara beamed as she sat down. Lena offered her the program.

“Apparently Alex thinks she’ll just save herself the torment and let us sit next to each other so the pictures aren’t of us talking around her,” Lena said.

Kara laughed accepting the program. 

“I take it there’s a lot of cameras currently pointed at us right now,” Kara said.

“Ten at least, and that’s not counting all the ones for the finish line,” Astra said leaning forwards to momentarily separate them. 

She turned towards Kara greeting her with a kiss on the head before sitting back to resume her conversation with Rhea and Eliza. Kara gave Lena a smug look.

“Hey I totally got a kiss too,” Lena said.

“Really?” Kara asked surprised.

“Yup, from Jack,” Lena said smirking.

Jack upon hearing his name turned and smiled.

“What did I do?” he asked.

“Greeted me with a kiss.”

Jack nodded quickly and turned away again his eyes on the cars moving up to the starting line. He lifted his binoculars.

“Lena have you seen the engine on 33? It’s a complete disaster,” he said. “It doesn’t look like it’ll make it even halfway.”

Lena scoffed and looked at Kara.

“Yeah but he’s your boyfriend so it’s not that special. I got a kiss from the Queen,” Kara said with a smirk.

Lena scoffed.

“Yeah but that’s because you’re family. She has to greet you like that,” Lena said returning the smirk as Kara feigned offense

“I still win," Kara muttered pouting.

Lena glared conceding with a sigh. Kara winked at her. Lena’s glare became a smile and then Kara was laughing.

“You’re ridiculous,” Lena said.

“Yeah, but so are you.”

Lena laughed again and conceded with a nod. For a moment they just smiled at each other.

“I’ve missed you,” Kara said with a sigh.

“Me too.”

Kara took a risk and gave Lena’s hand a squeeze. For a moment they looked at each other then Astra leaned forwards.

“Just to remind you both again, there is no less than a dozen cameras and at least three microphones pointed at you so please, try not to make the front page.”

They exchanged a look and then both of them sighed. Astra smiled giving their joined hands a squeeze then turned to Lena.

“Lena dear, I didn’t see you there,” she said.

Astra reached forwards gently taking Lena’s face in her hands before pressing a soft kiss to Lena’s head. The sudden click of cameras was audible even from their vantage point. Lena smirked at Kara as she gaped at her Aunt.

“I thought I was your favourite,” Kara said.

Astra laughed.

“I am Queen I don’t have favourites.”

They both gave her a disbelieving looks. 

“But if I had to choose I’d pick Alex. She’s a doctor.”

Astra sat back laughing as Kara and Lena exchanged a horrified look. Alex leaned back from her place beside Kara to smile at Astra.

“Love you too your majesty,” she said.

Astra beamed at Alex.

“I could have become a doctor if I wanted,” Kara muttered.

Lena nodded.

“You’d have been an amazing doctor,” she said.

“If only I didn’t hate blood,” Kara whispered.

“And vomit.”

“And… the other gross body fluids.” Kara made a face. “Okay so maybe I couldn’t be a doctor.”

Lena snickered and gave her hand a final squeeze before letting go.

"Just get a doctorate, it's what I plan to do," she said.

The race official appeared and smiled at Kara.

“Princess, if you would be so kind as to open the race,” he indicated the red switch sitting on an elevated podium.

“Of course,” she said smiling brightly and handing Lena her program. 

There was applause as she rose and with a smile flicked the switch. The first light changed then there was a moment but the next light didn’t move. There was laughter and Kara tried the switch again but it didn’t work. The race coordinators begun to panic slightly as Kara smiled and looked at Lena with a shrug. 

“We’ll have someone-” 

Lena got up and opened the podium. She fixed something with a laugh.

“Loose connection,” she explained. “Try again.”

Kara scoffed shaking her head as she flicked the switch again. The lights worked this time. The coordinator apologised again and Kara gave him a smile as the cars all sped off in a roar of engines.

“It’s fine,” she said.

Kara and Lena went back in their chairs to watch the race on the screen. They smiled at each other and  tried to focus on the race.

They mostly succeeded at least in appearance. In reality Kara found herself looking at the screen but her whole body attuned to Lena as she talked about the cars and the race and the differences. By the halfway point when car 33 stalled and Jack let out a triumphant “I told you so!” they were both a bit too distracted to do more than smile and nod as Lena explained how the Kryptonian engines were different to the other teams. 

As they came to the final stretch Alex gave them both a heads up and they turned to cheer as Mon-el raced Maxwell Lord for first place. They all rose standing at the balcony and yelling encouragement as Mon-el edged out Lord for the lead. Then they applauded loudly as he beat him across the line by a length.

Kara hurried down with the others to congratulate Mon-el on his win in the pit station. She raced a little ahead of the others as he got out of the car getting tackled enthusiastically by the Kryptonian team.

His smile brightened as he saw her and he ran over sweeping her off her feet with a laugh as she approached.

“Congratulations!” she yelled.

He beamed back.

“Well I had some very good motivation,” he said. 

He put her down with a smile and got down on one knee pulling a box out of his pocket. Kara’s heart caught in her chest as she realised what was happening, and apparently everyone else did too if the sudden quiet was any indication.

“Princess Kara of house El, will you do me the honour of marrying me?” he asked.

Kara found herself a little overwhelmed as she looked at Mon-el on his knee smiling at her his hair messed from the helmet and the crowd around them.

“Yes! Of course!” she said grinning at him. 

He grinned back sliding the ring onto her finger and then sweeping her off her feet again as he spun her around. She found herself leaning forwards and kissing him. He kissed her back enthusiastically.

 

Lena felt her heart seize a little as she watched Kara kiss Mon-el and heard everyone cheer. She recognised thae feeling as it coiled in the pit of her stomach as she watched everyone congratulate them. She’d known it was coming but… She hadn’t expected Kara to look so happy when it did though. A part of her started to wonder if Kara was actually going to fall for Mon-el. 

Then Jack was there putting an arm around her. 

“Let’s go,” he said gently.

“We need to offer congratulations,” she said watching Eliza embrace Mon-el and smile at him.

He shook his head. Then Sam was on her other side.

“Hey Sam can you tell Kara and Mon-el congratulations from us we’re going to head off,” Jack said.

Sam looked at Jack then Lena and nodded.

“I’ll tell them,” she promised.

Lena let Jack lead her away. No one really noticed thankfully as they made their way to Jack’s car. Lena sighed as they left.

“I’m fine,” she said.

“Right which is why you got all pale and looked like someone just kicked your puppy,” Jack said.

“It was just a moment,” she muttered. “I was just a little jealous that’s all.”

Jack reached over and squeezed her hand.

“You’re allowed to have feelings Lena. And you don’t have to stay and watch everyone fawn over them and pretend to be happy the girl you’re in love with is gonna marry him.”

“I’m not in love-”

Jack scoffed.

“So the last two hours of you both talking to each other exclusively and smiling non stop was just pretending. This is me Lena,” he said giving her a look. “I know you better.”

Lena sighed.

“I forgot this part of relationships,” she muttered. “The not so fun feelings.”

Back at the palace Jack guided her to get changed and they headed for the stables. Lena relaxed a little as PC greeted her. She smiled hugging him gently.

“Hello sweet boy,” she said.

Jack had PC and his mare Trinity saddled and they headed out on one of the nearby trails. Lena felt her feeling ease as she rode PC. She always felt safe and happy while riding him. Her own Prince Charming. She sighed her jealousy forgotten as she enjoyed the ride and Jack’s company. PC helped calm her as she guided him over a few small jumps. 

They stopped an hour later at a nice overlook. Lena dismounted smiling as she tied PC to a nearby tree. Jack held out his hand and led her over to look down at the view of Argo.

“So I know I didn’t win anything big today but I was hoping you’d be ever so agreeable as to marry me and then later divorce me,” Jack said. 

Lena turned and saw he had a ring. She smiled and hugged him.

“Thank you Jack.”

He smiled and slid the ring on her finger.

“I got Kara’s opinion and she suggested a ruby so you’d know she wanted it to be from her.”

Lena smiled wider looking at the silver band with the dark red ruby.

“Once everyone gets over the royal engagement we’ll let slip about ours,” Jack said.

Lena sighed and nodded.

“You know I love you right?” she said. “If we weren’t both exceedingly gay I’d really marry you.”

Jack smiled and pressed a kiss to her head. 

“Love you too.”

 

Lena was brushing PC down when Kara arrived leaning into the stall gate with a bright smile.

“Hey you,” she said.

Lena smiled at her.

“Congratulations Princess,” Lena said nodding at the ring adorning her finger.

Kara sighed.

“Yeah… I kinda knew it was coming but- hey are you wearing it?” Kara asked excitedly looking at Lena’s hand. 

Lena smiled and nodded holding up her hand so Kara could better see the engagement ring. Kara grinned.

“I hear you had a hand in choosing it,” Lena said.

Kara nodded then suddenly she looked shy. 

“I uh… I wanted you to know that well, even if I wasn’t able to propose I still… well you know.”

Lena smiled at Kara. 

“Thank you, it’s beautiful.”

Kara beamed. 

“I have a feeling you weren’t involved in this one,” she said holding up her hand to show the large chunk of diamond adoring her hand. 

“No, I would have recommended something flatter because I am sure that rock will catch on a door at some point and go flying off.”

Kara laughed and nodded.

“Good thing I only need to wear it for a month. The bracelet will be much easier.”

Lena nodded and they both heard a footsteps approaching and Mon-el appeared wrapping an arm around Kara.

“Hello future wife,” he said grinning at her.

“Hey Mon-el,” Kara said smiling back. 

Mon-el looked at Lena giving her a smile.

“Congratulations Lord Daxum. I’m sure you’ll be a very good couple for the year.”

“Who said it would just be one year?” he asked playfully. “Maybe she’ll fall madly in love with me and we’ll be together forever.”

Lena smiled politely.

“Well I’ve heard of stranger things I suppose.”

Kara winked at her and Lena smiled. PC shifted and Lena turned back to him. She continued brushing him and smiled as he sniffed at her pockets.

“There’s no sugar there,” she said gently pushing his head away so she could do his neck. 

Kara laughed as PC tried to sniff Lena’s pockets again.

“Oh fine I lied,” Lena said taking one out and offering it to him.

“Shall we go to dinner?” Mon-el asked.

“Soon,” Kara said. “I want to check in on Britney, she’s been a bit sad.”

“Well then let’s go see her,” Mon-el said stepping back and tugging Kara’s hand to lead her away. “Then we can go up and my mother can tell us how cute we are for the thousandth time.”

Kara sighed.

“Do we have to?” she asked giving him a pleading look.

Mon-el laughed.

“Come on, you better get used to it now.”

Kara sighed and looked back at Lena.

“Goodnight Lena. I’ll see you tomorrow.”

Lena smiled and nodded.

“Goodnight Kara.”

She listened as Mon-el dragged Kara away then the two of them talking as they headed to Britney’s stall. Lena sighed and looked at the ring on her finger the ruby winking up at her.

PC nudged her and she smiled turning to gently stroke his face. Jack had a dinner with some old friends that night and while Sam had promised they could do dinner together she had yet to appear and drag Lena out of the stables. 

Lena sighed looking at PC. She spent a minute just enjoying his attention and the soft brown of his eyes. She took a deep breath and decided to stop moping and go find Sam.

“Who’s my favourite?” she asked smiling at him.

He turned his head as Kara and Mon-el walked past. Lena laughed softly.

“You don’t have to call me out like that,” she muttered giving him a final pat.

Notes for the Chapter:

Well I hope y'all enjoyed my little homage to the Princess Bride. Let me know down in the comments what you're thinking about the story. Are you worried about Kara falling for Mon-el? How long do you think before Kara manages to lose her engagement ring? Can the universe find even more ways to involve Lena in Kara's princess duties? You'll have to wait until next time to see if these questions and many more are answered.
Stay safe y'all.

Next time:
There's a parade! And something small and cute and... streaky.

8. The parade and Streaky

Summary for the Chapter:

TW: Lena remembering Lex, but happy memories.

DAY 14

The closing of the Krypton 100 was a parade along the route. A choice Lena was sure had been made by the organisers to make the pack up easier over the years. As she was technically a member of the royal family until Kara’s coronation Lena was to be part of the parade though she was to go after the current royal family. Kara had asked if they could go together but Winn had shut that down quickly. They still needed to maintain the illusion of being competitors.

As her method of transport in the parade was left up to her Lena decided to get out Lex’s racer. The car was a vintage Krypton 100 winner from the seventies. The most famous Kryptonian car outside a museum and Lex’s most prized possession. Lena knew people would be excited to see it. 

She smiled as she drove the short distance from her private garage to the palace. Instead of trying to manoeuvre it through the other vehicles she parked it out the front of the palace. Getting out she nodded to the nearby guards silently drawing their attention to the car. No one would try to steal it in front of the royal guard.

Mon-el was returning from his run when she'd arrived and stopped mid step when he saw the car.

“Is that…”

Lena laughed at his shocked face and nodded.

“Yup, number 45, five time Krypton 100 winner. I thought it might be nice to let Krypton see it again.”

She looked over the car with a proud smile. Several people walking past had stopped and taken out their phones to get pictures. Lena looked over and saw Mon-el was one of them.

“This car is amazing,” he said cautiously reaching out to touch it. Lena laughed again.

“I really should put her in a museum or something but I can’t quite bring myself to part with her,” she said running a hand lovingly over the car.

She had so many happy memories with this car. All of them involving Lex. She could still remember the day Lex brought it home. 

“Lena wake up! Wake up!” Lex yelled racing into her room.

Lena groaned covering her face as Lex jumped on the bed smiling at her.

“You’ll never believe what I got,” he said jumping up and down.

“Sleep,” Lena mumbled rolling over.

Lex laughed throwing the blankets back as he grabbed her hand and dragged her out of her room and to the front steps of the mansion despite her protests. Then he showed her the car.

“Number 45!” he enthused.

Lena blinked at the car confused and then laughed as she recognised it and smiled at her brother.

“Okay, okay. That’s pretty cool,” she conceded.

“I know! Now hurry up and get dressed so we can take it for a drive,” Lex said grinning at her as he hugged the car.

Mon-el looked at the car in awe as he put his phone away. Lena smiled at him letting the memory fade.

“After the parade if you want you can take it for a spin,” she promised.

He looked at her suddenly very excited. 

“Really?”

She nodded smiling.

“Sure, take Kara she’d love it.”

“Hey thanks,” he said.

Lena gave him a smile and got one in return.

“You know, for a usurper you’re alright,” Mon-el said throwing an arm around her shoulders.

Lena laughed.

“For a spoilt lord you’re not so bad either,” she said.

Mon-el laughed good naturedly dropping his arm as they walked into the palace together. 

“See you later,” Mon-el said cheerfully.

Lena nodded forcing herself to smile.

“See you.”

She walked towards the gardens her mind was still lost in the past.

“Lena come on let’s go for a drive!” Lex said racing into her bedroom.

“I can’t Mum will murder me if I’m late,” Lena said.

“Late for what?” Lex asked.

Lena rolled her eyes and picked up her earrings.

“The Luthor Foundation gala. You know the event she’s been going on about for weeks. The one you told her you’d also attend.”

Lex made a disgusted noise and threw himself on her bed. He jumped up almost immediately.

“Okay new plan. We’ll drive there in 45. Put in an appearance to make mother dearest happy and then we can sneak out early. There’s always a window open at these things. I’ll even let you drive us back. You’ve got your license right?”

“Lex I’m fifteen I can’t legally drive. You know this,” Lena said laughing.

Lex made a disappointed noise.

“I did wonder why you were so short.”

“Hey!”

Kara appeared in front of her. Lena quickly blinked away the memory. 

“You okay?” Kara asked looking at Lena very carefully.

“Lex,” she whispered. “Happy memories.”

Kara looked around and seeing none of their entourages swept Lena into a tight hug. Lena clung to her for a moment breathing in the familiar smell of sugar and sunshine. Lena took a deep breath and forced herself to let go. Kara gave her a small smile and Lena returned it.

“Tell me about them sometime?”

Lena nodded. Kara smiled at her very gently and reached out to wipe a tear away. They carefully parted ways Kara heading back to her suite and Lena continuing deeper into the gardens. 

Three hours later they all assembled for the parade. Lena and Jack smiled at Kara and Mon-el as they approached their transport. Mon-el was riding with Astra and Kara in a horse drawn carriage. Lena and Jack would be right behind them in 45. Jack smiled when he saw the car and gave her hand a squeeze as they approached it. 

Lena sighed as Jack opened the door for her and held out his hand. Lena reached out and froze listening hard. She thought she’d heard something but it was hard to distinguish what with all the people moving around them. She looked around but there was nothing. Just to be sure she checked under the car and shook her head.

“You okay?” Jack asked reaching out to touch her shoulder.

“Just thought I heard something,” she said with a sigh.

Jack gave her hand another squeeze.

“Did you want me to drive?” he asked gently.

She nodded handing him the keys. Jack kissed her cheek and led her around to the passenger side seating her with flourish that made her smile. He closed her door and jogged back around the car to get in the driver seat. 

He smiled at her once more and started the engine as the call was made for them to start moving. Lena met Kara’s gaze as they did. She knew Kara was checking on her. Lena gave her a small smile and inclined her head towards Jack silently telling Kara he’d look after her. She got a nod in return. Mon-el said something to draw Kara's attention and Lena smiled watching Kara laugh. Jack reached over and held her hand.

“You okay?” he asked.

“Just one of those days I guess,” she said with a sigh.

He smiled and nodded. They exited the palace gates and Lena put on a smile to wave at the people gathered along the parade route. There was a lot of cheers as they realised just which car she and Jack were in. Lex would have loved it. She felt tears prick at the corners of her eyes as her mind supplied images of Lex hanging out the side window and waving enthusiastically to people.

Jack’s hand found hers again. Lena took a deep breath and carefully packed her feelings for her brother into a box to deal with later and smiled more naturally at Jack. He grinned back the two of them enjoying the parade as they soaked up the sunshine and the happy people. Music drifted to them from further ahead and behind. Lena smiled watching Kara smiling and waving to the people and throwing sweets to the children as she passed.

Then Lena heard it again, so faint. Then again. Alarmed Lena turned to Jack.

“Cut the engine,” she ordered.

Jack didn’t hesitate slamming on the brakes and turning off the engine as Lena jumped out racing to the front of the car and pulling up the hood. She heard it again, a soft meow.

Jack came racing around to see what was wrong.

“What-”

She shushed him pulling out her phone and using it as a flashlight trying to locate the sound. Jack fell silent listening then there was another tiny meow. He looked at her alarmed then there was the sound of racing footsteps and a murmur of the crowd as Kara appeared beside Lena.

“What’s wrong?” she asked breathless.

Mon-el appeared close behind her looking confused.

“It sounds like there’s a cat in the engine,” Jack said.

J’onn and several of the royal guard came rushing over following Kara.

“We need a set of tools, there’s a kitten in the engine,” Kara ordered the moment they appeared.

J’onn put a hand to his ear piece putting in the call as Lena and Kara begun stripping off their jackets and tossing them to their respective fiances. 

Lena rolled up her sleeves and Kara took Lena’s phone trying to locate the cat. Lena started undoing what she could to try and get into the inner workings of the engine. The one drawback to the racer was the size. There was a thousand tiny pockets of space in which the kitten could be hiding.

A member of the royal guard appeared with a bag of mechanical tools. Jack accepted them with a smile and handed them to Kara who started spreading them out for use. 

“Kara,” Lena said not looking away from the engine. “Wrench.”

She held out her hand and the correct wrench was immediately supplied.

“Tell them to continue the parade around us,” Kara said rolling up her sleeves.

“You need any help babe?” Mon-el asked.

“No, it’s okay. We got this,” Kara said grabbing a spanner. “Not the first engine we’ve taken apart.”

Lena laughed softly at the reminder. They’d taken a rental car to do the great ocean road and one hour in had ended up stranded on some country back road trying to diagnose the problem while they waited for a tow truck. They’d found the problem -not they could fix it with their tools- then made good use of the backseat of the car in the five hours it took the tow truck to arrive. 

Jack smiled at Mon-el giving him a pat on the shoulder.

“Trust me, we do not want to get in between these two and saving a kitten,” Jack said.

Mon-el sighed and winced as he watched Lena toss aside part of the car trying to get deeper to where the cat seemed to be.

“They’re tearing apart a piece of art though,” he said.

Jack laughed and the two men fell silent.

“I think it crawled up from underneath we might need to separate the engine completely to get at it,” Lena said. 

“Did you want me to check underneath?” Kara asked.

“No point, it had to crawl through the undercarriage to get here. I checked everything over just last week in preparation for today,” Lena said.

The parade continued with a marching band splitting in two to go around the stopped car as they worked. A half dozen royal guards stood in a circle protecting Kara and by extension the car. Jack headed over to the crowd to explain the situation and word quickly spread. Lena could hear a helicopter overhead and when she looked up from the engine there was now a news camera focused on her and Kara as they continued disassembling the car. 

“I see it,” Kara said excitedly. 

Lena moved the battery handing it to Jack and dived back in looking with Kara.

“I got the reflection of it’s eyes,” Kara said pointing down behind the engine.

Lena nodded and looking at the engine made a decision. She climbed onto the car and put her hand in among the parts feeling blindly for the kitten ignoring the cuts and scrapes from the various parts as she tried to reach it.

Her fingers brushed fur and she grinned forcing her arm deeper, her feet leaving the ground as her whole arm disappeared into the car.  She felt a brush of fur again and carefully gripped the kitten. 

“Got it.” She tried to wriggle backwards and found herself caught on something. “Uh Kara can you?”

“On it,” Kara said wrapping her arms around her and pulling. Bit by bit Lena was moved backwards her arm coming out with the kitten held firmly in her right hand as it wriggled and meowed protest.

“You’re almost free baby,” Lena said trying to reassure it.

Kara’s hands wrapped around her again and she pulled again Lena’s arm coming free. Lena smiled as her feet touched the ground again and she stumbled back into Kara. Kara steaded her easily as Lena proudly held up the kitten. She was now covered in grease her white shirt completely ruined and had oil all over her arms but she had the kitten and Kara was holding her close. 

There was immediate applause from the crowd as they saw the retrieved kitten. Lena turned to Kara the two of them smiling at each other. Lena felt the near overwhelming urge to kiss Kara. She quickly looked at the kitten and smiled as it squirmed in her hands fighting her grip.

“Aw she’s all streaky,” Lena said smiling at the filthy kitten. “Here you go princess.”

Lena held the kitten out to Kara.

“Aw she is streaky,” Kara said smiling as she accepted the mewling kitten. 

She turned and waved to the crowd causing another round of applause as she held up the kitten. They all turned back to each other, then the car parts all around them.

“And now to put the car back together,” Jack said with a smile.

Mon-el sighed shuffling his feet. Kara looked at the still mewling kitten concerned.

“You guys take the kitten to get checked over, we’ll handle the car,” Lena said nodding to Jack.

Kara seemed torn but Lena smiled and gave her hand a reassuring squeeze. Kara nodded smiling at Mon-el. He bowed and led her to the armoured car waiting nearby. The two of them left waving to the people again.

“You know they’re gonna get all the cute cat pics now,” Jack said with a sigh.

Lena nodded watching as Jack removed his jacket putting it in the car with hers. 

“But we get to be the couple who fixes things,” Lena said handing him the wrench.

Jack smiled rolling up his sleeves. They set to work putting the car back in working order. It took longer to put it back together than it had to take it apart but instead of making them move the police simply moved the cordon around them as the parade ended and many of the spectators chose to stay and watch as they finished putting the car back together. The news camera even stayed which amused Lena. 

Finally she closed the hood and nodded to Jack who turned the key and grinned at her as the car immediately roared to life resulting in another cheer and much applause from the assembled crowd.

The smiling police removed the cordon and with a final wave they got in the car and carefully drove away heading back to the palace. 

 

Kara cradled the kitten close as it sniffed and mewled angrily. She, J’onn, Winn, and Mon-el had made their way to a nearby veterinary practice.

“You are such a loud kitten,” she said trying to wipe more of the grease off as they walked from the car. 

A vet tech showed them through immediately and the vet quickly appeared smiling widely at them all. Winn took out his phone and asked if the vet minded him recording this for the people of Krypton.

“Of course not,” the vet said smiling quickly.

Kara watched as he carefully checked the kitten over.

“Well your highness, it looks like, amazingly, she sustained no injury from her time in the car. I’d say she’s about six weeks, underweight, has fleas, probably worms, and an ear infection but otherwise she’s okay. She’ll need a special diet and some antibiotics for a few weeks but she should be fine.”

Kara sighed in relief and the vet continued talking. 

“I feel very confident saying she’s a stray based on all this and the lack of microchip. So I need to ask if you’re intending to have her put up for adoption or-”

“No I think I’ll keep her,” Kara said with a smile. “She was afterall a gift from Lena, technically speaking, well unless Lena wants to keep her. Actually hang on, I’ll ask her.”

She handed the kitten to Mon-el who made kissy noises at it as she sent the message. The vet went on to explain some more of the care for the kitten as Kara waited for a reply. Eventually her phone buzzed and she laughed.

“So I’m definitely the one taking her. Apparently Jack is allergic to cats,” she said grinning at Winn then the vet.

Mon-el laughed.

“Well I’m not so…”

He gave her a bright smiled which Kara returned. 

“What are you going to call her?” the vet asked.

Kara considered the kitten carefully as she stood on the examination table. She was light grey with black stripes and bright green eyes. The green reminded her of Lena.

“Streaky,” she said smiling.

“Aw cute,” Mon-el said.

He bent down to look at the kitten on the table.

“Do you like your new name Streaky?” he asked. Streaky meowed loudly. “I think she does,” he said giving her a very careful pat.

They were taken through to the back by one of the vet techs and shown how to wash her. They carefully cleaned all the grease and oil from her fur then gave her the flea wash before they dried her. Streaky did not appreciate their diligent care and attempted to escape five times resulting in a lot of laughter from everyone.

Then it was shopping time. Kara had a great hour going through a pet store with Mon-el and Winn buying Streaky her first baby collar with a bell. 

“Red so everyone knows she’s a princess from the house of El,” Mon-el said carefully slipping it in place. 

As well as a couple of soft beds and at least twenty toys all of which Winn held up for Streaky to pick declaring only those she touched were worthy of being added to the trolley.

The delay meant they arrived back at the palace at the same time as Lena and Jack. Both of whom looked a little tired but happy. They were also both filthy. 

“Lena, Jack!” Kara said greeting them. “Look, now Streaky’s all clean.”

Lena did an exaggerated bow.

“Our humble greetings to Princess Streaky may she reign forever,” she said.

Kara laughed and pressed a kiss to the sleepy kitten. She looked apologetically at Jack.

“Sorry about the allergies meaning no kitten for you,” she said.

Jack beamed at her. 

“If Lena had really wanted her I’d just take antihistamines everyday for the next decade,” he said.

“Aw, you are the sweetest man in the whole world,” Kara said smiling at him.

Lena gave him a quick kiss on the cheek as she smiled at him.

“I’ll have you know I’m just practical. I know she’d pick the cat over me in her bed.”

Lena laughed and nodded.

“If we find a dog though it’s ours,” Lena said

Kara nodded then she smiling as she looked at Lena. She loved seeing Lena like that her hair falling out of her bun a smile on her face everything about her looked so… soft. She wanted to pull her close and kiss her over and over until the last of her hair fell free and the happy smile turned seductive.

“What?” Lena asked smiling back as she fiddled with her stained cuff. 

“You’re just all messy,” Kara said still smiling at her. “Miss perfectly put together covered in grease. I kinda love it.”

Lena winked as Jack laughed. Lillian appeared. 

“Lena, Jack. Good you’re back. Hurry up and get changed we have dinner with the Police Chief and his wife tonight.”

Lena sighed giving Kara a final smile as she headed inside pulling Jack along behind her. Kara watched her go with a sigh then looked down at Streaky in her arms. She smiled at the sleeping kitten and allowed Mon-el to guide her into the palace and towards her rooms so they could set everything up for Streaky.

 

DAY 15

It didn’t take long for someone to notice the ring on Lena’s hand as she handed Kara the kitten. Then more pictures were checked and it became clear she had gotten engaged on the same day as Kara. 

Lena woke to a half dozen pictures in the Kryptonian tabloids all circling her left hand. They conveniently had an interview set with Cat Grant for Catco that morning. Considering the popularity of the morning show it was excellent timing.

Lena and Jack sat together in front of the camera as the final checks were made as the makeup people did the last few touch ups before they started. Cat turned to Jack and Lena as they carefully arranged themselves on the couch. Jack wore a navy blue pinstripe shirt with the sleeves carefully rolled up to show his forearms. Lena was in a charcoal skirt and white silk blouse that accented her figure nicely. Jack put an arm around the back of the couch as Lena shifted a little leaning into him. They both checked their pose on the screens and Cat raised an eyebrow.

“You two are very concerned with how you’ll look,” she said.

“My beard is a thing of beauty and must be properly appreciated,” Jack said lifting his chin and turning his head from side to side.

Lena smiled and gave him a look right as the light changed and Cat launched into her introduction. Lena and Jack focused smiling politely as Cat turned to them.

“So first of all congratulations on your engagement,” she said.

“Thank you,” they chorused smiling back.

“Which begs the question how do you feel about the crown princess’ engagement to Lord Mon-el of house Daxam?”

Jack and Lena exchanged a confused look and gave Cat bright smiles.

“We’re very happy for them,” Jack said.

“Mon-el’s a great guy and I know he and Kara are both really excited to have found each other and to be getting married,” Lena added.

Cat gave them a look.

“You were at the Krypton 100 when he proposed but you weren’t seen congratulating them. In fact you left right after he won the race,” Cat said giving Lena a particularly intense look.

Lena didn’t waver.

“It was a big moment for them and we wanted to make sure the focus was on them and their engagement. Us getting in the middle of that we felt might have made it more about the throne than the two of them taking the next step in their relationship, so we decided to leave. We congratulated them in private that evening after they returned to the palace.”

Cat nodded adjusting her notes as she carefully considered them.

“It looks like she will be crowned after all considering the timing of her wedding, how does that make you feel?” Cat asked.

“I am happy for her, but I do worry a little about what it could mean for Krypton,” Lena said with a small sigh. “Unlike most monarchy’s around the world a Kryptonian Queen needs to do politics and I worry Kara will struggle, and as charming, popular, and fun as Mon-el is, he doesn’t have the political mind she really needs to help her in the role.”

“And if she asked for your help with all that after she is crowned, would you?” Cat asked giving Lena a stern look.

“Of course I would help her,” Lena said glaring at Cat. “No matter how I would feel losing the throne I will always put my people’s needs above my own and it is hubris to think otherwise.” 

Cat raised an eyebrow and smiled at Lena as though she had just passed some test. Lena sighed relaxing her face into a smile again.

“You and the princess seem to get along quite well,” Cate said. “You were seen talking quite closely all through the Krypton 100 and seemed quite comfortable working together to take apart the car in the parade. Not to mention your antics in the eel pond.”

Lena smiled and Jack gave her hand a subtle squeeze as he chuckled.

“Yes, we actually met while on holiday before we knew who each other was and had became good friends. So while my challenge for the throne did cause some trouble between us initially Kara and I… Well we have far too much in common and get along far too well for any real animosity,” Lena said with a shrug. “I dare say we are quite good friends despite the circumstances. She understands why I’m challenging and I understand why she’s not backing down.”

“Speaking of your challenge, what compelled you to challenge for the throne?”

Lena sighed and thought about it for a moment. She looked back at Cat who was waiting patiently.

“I could go into the history of my family’s claim, my father and grandfather’s attempts and the reasons why it’s such a strong claim but, well the reason I’m challenging is quite simple. I’ve actually got the skills and experience to be queen. I’ve been preparing for this pretty much my entire life. Originally the expectation was that Lex would become King and I would advise him but after…” Jack squeezed her hand again. “Well after, I was the last Luthor able to challenge. So I pivoted away from my sporting career and begun seriously studying and working to gain the experience, connections, and training to take the position.”

Cat gave her a long practised look of cynicism.

“Princess Kara also has a degree and spent years preparing for the role. What makes you so much more qualified than her?” Cat asked.

Lena smiled.

“Princess Kara has studied internationally. She got her degree at Oxford and her training and experience has been focused on building her connections externally. While she might be able to handle the UN or foreign dignitaries she is not familiar with Krypton. You cannot rule for the people if you do not know the people, and the systems they live under. I’ve lived in Krypton my entire life. I went to primary and secondary school in the Fire Falls region and I got my degree at Argo University.”

She turned to smile at Jack.

“Where I met this handsome man.”

He chuckled and winked giving her a small hug.

“I studied our laws and then I worked with and for the public service here. I’ve been officially involved in the political scene in Krypton for five years and was raised practically swimming in it. I know all our major players. I know our laws, our public services, our major industries and organisations. As I said before Krypton is not like England or Japan or any of those other countries where their monarch is just a figurehead. To rule Krypton as monarch is more than that. It’s a political position for life, a job. And it’s not an easy one.”

Lena took a deep breath here came the hard bit. How to balance it perfectly.

“Kara, as wonderful as she is, and I mean that sincerely, she is a wonderful, kind, intelligent…” sexy, beautiful, loving, “Brilliant woman, but she doesn’t know Kryptonian politics. She has no experience handling the processes and challenges with making policy within Krypton. Managing that process requires practice and experience and while Astra has done what she could with the time she’s had the point remains Kara was not raised for the role. She doesn’t know the noble families and has been strongly influenced by the American upbringing and education she did have. At times this is great as it gives her a fresh perspective and she’s not as set as others in upholding the way things are, but the problem remains that she needs much more help managing and navigating Kryptonian politics as a result.”

“And you don’t need help?” Cat asked with a smirk.

Lena laughed holding up her hands.

“I openly admit even with all my training and experience I do still need help at times, that’s what advisers and Parliament are for. But I have been trained for the position all my life. I am a Lady, and I was raised with the full understanding and expectation that I would be involved in the politics of this nation at it’s most senior levels. I am ready to take the position and help lead my people onwards.”

Cat nodded considering Lena for a moment.

“Have you heard that apparently there are people out there who think the best royal couple would be you and the princess?” Cat asked smiling.

Lena and Jack immediately started laughing.

“Look, I agree that would certainly be the easiest solution to this whole thing. But then again…” Lena turned to smile at Jack. “I am very happy to have Jack back with me and… the rekindling of an old flame.” 

She gave him a slow smile and he smiled back and kissed her hand with unmistakable affection.

“So even though the Princess is set to marry and take the throne you are still going to go through with your marriage?” Cat asked seeming surprised.

“Oh yes,” Jack said pulling Lena a little closer. “We have always gone well together and well… I think we both kind of needed this push to remember our personal lives are just as important as our professional. And there are certainly advantages to matrimony…” 

Lena subtly bit her lip giving him a look as her eyes moved over his body.

“Oh so there has definitely been a rekindling has there?” Cat asked grinning at them both as the crowd laughed.

Lena smiled and resisted the urge to hide her face a little as Cat gave her a very knowing look.

“Maybe,” Lena said feigning a blush. 

Cat laughed and Jack kissed Lena’s cheek.

“I have always loved watching her with cars. There’s just something so… alluring about a woman elbow deep in an engine,” he said with a grin towards Cat.

Lena and Cat laughed.

“You and your love of me with cars,” Lena muttered turning to smile at him.

“You’re always so put together I love seeing you just a little messy,” Jack said twirling a piece of her hair free.

Lena’s heart seized for a moment and Jack smiled and gave her shoulder a gentle squeeze. 

“You’ve seen me messy plenty of times,” she said.

Jack waggled his eyebrows and Lena laughed shaking her head at him. 

“Just for that you can sleep on the couch tonight.”

“I think there’s another bed at the palace,” Jack said with a laugh.

“Unfortunately all 104 beds are taken,” Lena said smugly.

“Really?” Jack asked feigning shock. “With who?”

“Ghosts.”

Jack laughed.

“That sounds like something Kara would say.”

Lena smiled and gave him a look.

“She did.”

Jack laughed louder holding Lena tighter. Cat looked at them confused but didn’t interrupt the moment.

“Okay so I guess I need to convince you to let me share with you,” Jack said.

“I am very susceptible to bribery in this case,” Lena said smiling up at him.

Jack smiled back pretending to think.

“I’ll bring you chocolate covered strawberries and give you a massage.”

“Oh deal,” she said.

Cat grinned at them both and they smiled at the camera then Jack took the moment to capture her lips in a very soft kiss. 

Cat ended the interview a few minutes later and thanked them for coming as she gave her congratulations once more. Once the cameras were off she turned to look at them.

“You two are very good at this,” she said. 

They smiled back. 

“And Jack you are right,” Cat smiled at Lena and held her gaze. “You do have a very pretty beard.”

Lena opened then closed her mouth as Cat winked and turned to accept a coffee from her nervous assistant that she immediately declared was too cold. Lena looked at Jack who smiled back. 

“I think she knows,” he whispered very quietly.

Lena laughed and they got up meeting Jess at the door. 

 

Kara smiled watching the interview of Jack and Lena. If she didn’t know the truth she would believe it herself. She was touched by Jack using her words as a tease for Lena. It felt almost like a very subtle nod to her and she appreciated it. The more she learnt about Jack the more she liked him. 

After seeing his performance in the interview she was starting to understand how he had helped Lena convince her mother she was straight all through university. She hoped she could put in half as good a performance. Though she was sure Cat wasn’t going to fault her and Mon-el for not having the same depth in their relationship. She and Mon-el had an interview set with Cat in the days before their wedding to add to the excitement in the lead up.

Streaky meowed at her feet and Kara smiled reaching down and putting Streaky in her lap. Streaky nudged her hand with her head and Kara smiled petting her until she purred. Mon-el appeared and dropped into a chair beside her. 

“And how is the littlest princess of Krypton?” he asked wriggling his fingers for Streaky.

She immediately batted at his fingers trying to bite them as he smiled and teased her holding them just out of reach.

“Be careful she doesn’t start seeing your fingers as food,” Kara warned smiling at Mon-el.

He lured Streaky to jump onto the table with his wriggling fingers. Then registering her warning he laughed and reached for a nearby cat toy.

“Your highness makes a very good point,” he said waving the ribbon for Streaky to chase. “She won’t be the littlest princess forever.”

He glanced over at the tablet seeing the paused interview.

“How did they go? Did Cat grill them?” he asked.

Kara smiled shaking her head.

“No, Cat asked a few questions but the interview was more to get them to confirm the engagement and for Lena to make a case for her as Queen. Apparently Cat and Lillian were high school friends so she’s known Lena since she was a kid. It was a very sweet interview actually. See for yourself.”

She slid her headphones across the table for him. She took over wriggling the ribbon for Streaky to chase as Mon-el put on her headphones. 

He removed them with a sigh a few minutes later. Kara glanced over and saw him scrolling through the comments.

“What are people saying?” she asked.

He shrugged.

“The usual,” he said sounding tired. “They’re a very real love story. It’s so great to see two old flames who came back together to burn hotter. Jack is the most romantic of men. They’re a power couple.”

He pushed the tablet away with a frustrated noise and looked at her with a thoughtful expression.

“We need to up our sex appeal,” he said.

Kara scoffed.

“Considering how many pictures there are of us in tight athletic gear, and the various comments we get I don’t think sex appeal is an area we’re behind in,” she said smiling at him.

Mon-el laughed softly.

“What I meant is everyone knows they’re sleeping together so we need to like…”

He waved his hands and Kara scoffed.

“What?” she asked with a laugh.

“Suggest we’re doing it,” he said. “Or like… we could actually do it. If you were interested. We are getting married soon so…”

He gave her a look and Kara smiled back shaking her head. 

“Sorry Mon-el I’m not really interested in doing or implying that. I kinda have this whole good girl reputation. Saving it until marriage fits with that,” she said turning back to Streaky who was chewing determinedly on the ribbon.

“It’s cool,” he said smiling brightly at her. Then he frowned seeming concerned. “Um… are you a uh… a virgin?”

Kara looked at him for a moment seeing his very serious expression. She started laughing and didn’t really stop for a minute. Mon-el relaxed a little and laughed as well.

“I’m gonna take that as a no,” he said watching her wiping tears of laughter from her eyes.

“Yeah that’s a no,” she said still chuckling. “I’m gonna tell Lena you thought I was a virgin she’ll think that’s hilarious.”

Mon-el gave her a confused look as she reached for her phone typing the message and sending it quickly.

“Why will she find that hilarious?” he asked.

Kara hesitated. She hadn’t exactly hidden her past with Lena from him but at the same time something told her not to let him know the extent of their relationship. She turned to him with a very bright smile.

“Let’s just say she knows the exact number and a lot of details from a few drunken nights together.”

He laughed and nodded.

“And what is the number?” he asked leaning towards her waggling his eyebrows.

Kara feigned shock gasping and putting a hand over her heart. 

“A princess never tells,” she said.

He sighed shaking his head with a smile and looked at Streaky who was still rolling around with the ribbon.

“You know if you want we can totally do a video about how Streaky stole the other half of my bed,” she suggested. 

His face lit up excitedly.

“Ooh we could totally do something about me sleeping on the cat bed because she stole my spot. What do you think Nia?”

Mon-el turned to Nia who was lying on the couch absorbed in something on her phone. She looked up hearing her name and gave them a confused look. Mon-el repeated his idea. Nia smiled brightly sitting up.

“Yeah that could be really cute. A video showing you on the floor in the cat bed then panning over to Streaky curled up on Kara’s bed…”

Kara’s phone buzzed and she smiled seeing Lena’s name. 

I spat wine all over the table when I read that. Mother was furious. Then Jack read the message and slid to the floor laughing hysterically in the middle of this sports lunch drawing everyone’s attention. So mother stole my phone from him to see what it was and you won’t believe this but she actually smiled! It was brief but I saw it. Then she handed the phone back to me and just walked away.

Kara grinned and turned to Nia.

“Hey Nia do you know what lunch Lena and Jack are at right now and if there’s cameras. Apparently Lillian Luthor smiled at a message I sent Lena, and I kinda want proof.”

Nia laughed and nodded.

“Sure, I’ll message Jess.”

She typed on her phone for a minute then stopped and looked at Kara.

“What did you send her?”

Kara handed over her phone. Nia read the message looked at Mon-el and started laughing then didn’t stop. Mon-el gave Kara a look. She gave him an apologetic smile. He smiled back.

“Message received, your good girl reputation isn’t an accurate representation of your experience level.”

Notes for the Chapter:

The number of cute animal sidekicks is now at two, well three if we include Britney. Before anyone asks no there will not be Krypto the super dog in this. The cute animal quota for the princess story has been met.

Next time:
They go to the great temple of Argo to announce their weddings, and Kara has a talk with Astra under the stars.

9. The Church

Notes for the Chapter:

Sorry this is a bit of a setup chapter but I promise more shenanigans next time.

DAY 18

Lena smiled as she and Jack joined Alex in the waiting car.

“Greetings Usurpers,” Alex said with a smile.

Jack smiled waving to her as he listened to his mother on the phone. 

“Hey Alex,” Lena said smiling back. “Shouldn’t you be with Kara?”

Alex sighed and nodded. 

“I slept in. I’ve been trying to get up to date on all the Kryptonian medical stuff before the coronation. I spent six hours last night reading about burns treatments for the wedding. Since you’re going to the same place I thought I’d just catch a ride with you even though it’s like eight blocks but-.”

“Yes of course,” Jack said exasperated. “No, I am being very agreeable and charming. I promise. I have not completely- uh huh.”

He sighed and begun making mhmm noises. Alex gave Lena a look she smiled.

“His mother,” Lena whispered. 

Alex smothered a laugh. Jack looked at her then Lena who was smiling back far too smugly.
