“Hey Mum did you want to talk to Lena? She’s right here,” Jack said with a bright smile.

Lena looked at him in horror.

“I can’t I’m very busy,” she said quickly taking out her own phone and pretending to work.

“No, of course she’s not too busy for you,” Jack said grinning at Lena. 

Alex snickered as she watched the silent fight between the pair that ended the moment Jack managed to press the phone to Lena’s ear.

“Anne, so lovely to hear from you,” Lena said with a falsely bright voice.

Alex watched as Lena smacked Jack repeatedly while talking to his mother. 

“Of course, no everything between us is great. Yes, he’s been quite the gentleman.”

Jack smirked and Lena hit him again.

“What do you- oh well… I wouldn’t say he’s been inattentive…”

Jack’s head snapped up and Lena gave him her best evil grin. The silent fight begun in reverse as Jack tried to get the phone back.

“He’s been very sweet I just… sometimes I wonder if he really means it you know. I would hate for him to just be pretending for some reason.”

Jack managed to wrestle the phone back.

“Sorry Mum we have to go we just arrived and there’s lot’s of important people to speak with,” he said quickly. “We’ll call you again when we get a chance. Bye.”

“Bye Anne!” Lena called.

Jack glared at Lena.

“Seriously?” he whined. “She’s going to give me such a- ugh.”

Lena smirked as Jack fell back in his seat dramatically covering his eyes.

“You shouldn’t have put her on the phone with me,” Lena said.

Alex looked between them. 

“Here I was thinking only Kara could make you act like a child. But now I’m starting to think that you’re secretly incredibly immature and you just hide it under a mask of refinement,” Alex said.

Jack laughed.

“Oh the refinement’s a mask alright. Lena is actually an idiot she’s just really good at hiding it.”

Lena rolled her eyes.

“Says the man who started a campus wide water fight.”

Jack smiled.

“Says the woman who helped me.”

“I think you mean the woman who won,” Lena corrected with a smile.

Alex sighed.

“You really are the perfect woman for her,” she said.

Lena turned to Alex with an oddly shy smile.

“Thanks,” she whispered.

The car came to a stop and they all got out. The great temple of Argo was a thing of beauty. Made of the same local stone as the palace it was a large central tower with dozens of triangular wings coming off at points to make a symbolic sun. All over it were yellow and white banners and ribbons. 

They started the trek up the many stairs in front of the main entrance. Jack was looking around at a few of the groups standing on the steps with the priests. 

“Wait, I thought you said the church was against same sex unions,” he said frowning. 

Lena turned following his gaze and smiled. Jack was staring at a pair of men on the steps in neat suits with bright yellow shirts and flowers. A group of friends were nearby and what looked like one of the men’s mothers was weeping as she watched the two men reciting vows before two priests. One stood silent his back to the pair as the other recited the vows.

“It’s not legally binding it’s just symbolic,” Lena explained with a sigh. “People do it for renewing their vows and teenagers in the seventies used to do it as some kind of we’ll be together for real one day thing. So in the eighties the queer community started doing it as well and they just haven’t really stopped.”

Jack gave her a confused look. Lena nodded at the two men. 

“You can stand on the steps so you’re technically at the temple and say the vows but the second priest just immediately annuls it all when they turn around and touch the pair. But allowing them to say the vows means that people can do the whole ceremony, well the old ceremony. The church isn’t exactly happy about queer people doing it and not all priests will agree to do the ceremony but enough will for it to be a common practice. So…”

She shrugged and Jack smiled putting an arm around her as they watched the two men coat their hands in oil then the smiling priest tied their bracelets with a ribbon before they thrust their hands into a lit brazier as one priest waved his hands saying the prayers. The men were both smiling as they removed their hands.

The three of them joined the applause as the men kissed and then the second priest turned and tapped the two men. The men didn’t seem to notice too busy looking at each other. One grabbed the other’s hand and they ran from the steps amid cheers and whistles from their friends as they got into a waiting car. The car sped off almost immediately as the men kissed again.

“That uh-”

Jack turned to look at Lena. She winked at him but offered no explanation.

“It’s the old elopement ceremony,” Alex said smiling. “You gotta consummate the marriage so there’s no take backsies.” 

Jack and Lena looked at Alex surprised. She noticed and crossed her arms.

“I’m medical adviser to the crown princess. I gotta know the cultural stuff. That oil for example is made from the blood bloom plant and stops their hands from being burnt when they put them in the flames.”

Jack quickly looked at Lena a little worried. Apparently he hadn’t attended any Kryptonian weddings. Lena patted his cheek. 

“It’s okay we’ll use the oil. It’s technically optional but I would kind of like to not have a burnt hand for the wedding night.” She smiled at him. “It might impact my ability to eat leftover wedding cake.”

Jack laughed and they were joined by Mon-el and Kara.

“Hey Alex,” Kara said throwing her arm around her sister’s shoulders. She looked at Lena and Jack narrowing her eyes playfully. “Usurpers.”

Jack narrowed his eyes right back.

“Your time shall come false princess,” he teased wrapping his arms around Lena.

Kara grinned at him.

“Hey now, no fighting in the temple,” a priest said coming towards them with a giant grin. “That’s a sin.”

“Kal!” Kara cried flinging her arms around him.

He hugged her back laughing happily as he lifted her off her feet momentarily. Kara squealed in surprise and playfully smacked him. Kal put her down still smiling then looked at Lena.

“Hello again Lena, I’m not sure-”

“I remember you Kal,” she said moving forwards with a smile. “Hard to forget the man who had to catch me when I fell off a fence.”

Kal chuckled and Kara let go of him turning to Lena surprised.

“You’ve met-”

“Kal and Lex were friends in high school. Kal was dragged with me into a few of Lex’s various schemes. Usually ones involving breaking out and going racing.”

Kal chuckled and moved forwards to greet Lena with a warm hug. She allowed herself to sink into the reassuring embrace with a smile. He pulled back a little giving her a very gentle look.

“I sent-”

Lena nodded and smiled.

“I got them. Thank you. Lex would have loved the colours.”

Kal nodded sadly then he turned to shake hands with Mon-el and Jack. He gave Alex a tight hug lifting her off her feet too despite her protest. Greetings done he smiled at them all.

“I take it you’re all here to give notice of your impending weddings,” Kal said.

They nodded.

“Mine’ll be on the steps,” Alex said. 

Kal smiled at her.

“Well when you do it, I hope to at least be invited.”

Alex laughed.

“You’ll be the priest. I’ll need you to tackle the other one so me and my bride can run off and make it official.”

Kal laughed.

“Sorry still not legal then,” he said.

Alex shrugged.

“What are we talking about?” Mon-el asked putting his phone away.

“The step bindings,” Kal said with a smile.

Mon-el frowned then looked over at a pair of teenagers with their friends cheering them on as they recited their vows.

“Oh right, Eve and Trinia,” Mon-el said laughing.

“Who?” Jack asked confused.

“Eve and Trinia are an infamous Kryptonian lesbian couple from the eighties,” Lena explained. “They did the step binding then one of their friends punched the second priest in the face starting a brawl while they escaped. It was a whole thing at the time.”

J’onn appeared at Kara’s side.

“Princess if we could continue this inside,” he said subtly indicating the growing crowd nearby.

They all turned continuing up the steps to the temple entrance as a team of the royal guard surrounded them.

“Essentially it resulted in a legal battle because the ceremony on the steps is technically an elopement binding,” Lena explained sliding her arm in Jack’s. “They were done by couples whose families disagreed with the match, or who couldn’t afford to do it in the temple, they could still marry with a priest who was willing to do it for a private fee, or for no charge if they were really poor. They were technically at the temple on the steps but not actually inside it which would suggest approval. Then they would run off and consummate their marriage. That meant that even should the families or church still disagree and seek to annul their marriage any children of their union would technically be legitimate and the woman’s reputation wouldn’t be lowered by a lack of virtue. Normally the possibility of children was enough to ensure the marriages were upheld the next day but not always. It’s actually how the house of El started.”

Jack laughed and looked at Kara who nodded.

“My several greats, grandfather Kon-el was born after the crown prince fell in love with a lower noblewoman and did an elopement binding. It was dissolved the next day but as luck would have it she was pregnant. Kon-el was made a Duke and given land and the House of El was born.”

Lena scoffed.

“It was his son who stole the throne from the Luthors,” Lena added.

Jack laughed and then suddenly fell silent as they entered the temple. Lena smiled. Inside was a great circular room with a large pillared hallway leading off into the various wings. But the true spectacle was the main tower itself which was empty all the way to the roof. From the outside the tower looked solid but was actually made with thousands of small windows to let in the sun’s light which was then concentrated with thousands of matching mirrors to a giant crystal suspended over their heads. The crystal illuminated the floor below in an intricate sun design that moved across the floor over the day as the light from the windows shifted to illuminate different sides of the crystal.

“You lived in Argo for five years and you’ve never been inside the great temple,” Lena said smiling at Jack as he continued to gape at the crystal. 

“The church and I weren’t exactly on good terms at the time,” Jack whispered his eyes on the design.

“It was made for me,” Kara said with a wink.

Jack looked at her and laughed.

“Well my namesake,” Kara admitted. “According to legend she was a woman of great beauty who always obeyed her parents etc etc, but importantly she could not look towards the sun for Rao’s brilliance was too bright for her. She was wanted by many as a bride but she refused them all for she felt if she could not see Rao then her union could not be truly blessed by him. So the architect hired by the church for the temple hearing this made her promise she would marry him if he could let her see the face of Rao. She agreed. It took him fifty years but he did it using the crystal. And so she married him that very day.”

Jack whistled looking at the sun design sparkling on the floor.

“Man was crazy,” Mon-el muttered. “According to the legend he died that night. He worked for fifty years for one night.”

Kara turned to Mon-el and smacked him lightly.

“It’s romantic,” she said.

Mon-el laughed.

“I’m just saying no woman is that good especially not at like seventy.”

Lena and Alex snickered as Kara hit Mon-el again. 

“Lena back me up here,” Kara said.

“It is very romantic,” Lena agreed quickly.

“Aw come on Lena you wouldn’t wait fifty years for one night would you?” Mon-el asked.

Lena looked over at Mon-el. She gave him a smile.

“Mon-el, you’d be surprised what I’d be willing to do for love.”

Mon-el looked at her and made a face as Kara aw’d and Alex scoffed.

“That almost sounded like a threat,” Mon-el said.

Lena laughed and Jack wrapped his arms around her.

“Princess how lovely of you to come.” 

One of the high priests came racing forwards as fast as his heavy white robes would allow. 

“Thank you, I am delighted to be here,” Kara said quickly turning and greeting him. 

Kal moved to stand beside Jack and Lena as the high priest, who had now been joined by a half dozen other priests, spoke with Kara and Mon-el.

“So do you two have an appointment?” Kal asked.

“No, we were going to do a private announcement,” Lena said watching as Kara and Mon-el were led away by the priests.

“Well if you have no objections I would be honoured to assist you,” Kal said.

Lena smiled up at him. 

“Thank you Kal, we’d be delighted.”

Kal led them away to one of the private prayer rooms and returned a few minutes later with a scroll.

“Jack I know you’re new to this so I’ll explain it all first then you’ll know what’s happening. First Lena is going to pray to Rao for his blessing with your union. Then she will write her name on the banner and the date of the wedding. Then I will sign it and take it out to announce to anyone listening and put it up for display until your wedding day.”

“Cool so what do I do today?” Jack asked.

Kal smiled and shrugged.

“Sit there and look pretty darling,” Lena said. “The only important name for the wedding is the bride’s.” She winked at Jack. “Just in case you don’t show up and I have to marry the best man. It’s why the groom’s name is always left blank.”

Jack laughed and nodded.

“I will sprinkle you with holy water at the end of the prayers to encourage you to turn up or drown,” Kal added with a smile.

Jack gave Kal a concerned look.

“Does that happen often?” he asked.

Lena smiled at him.

“There’s a fable about a man who was running from his wedding because he didn’t want to marry the woman he had tricked and gotten pregnant. As punishment for running away from his responsibilities, and for generally being a bad person never willing to own up to his responsibilities, Rao blinded the groom as he was riding over a bridge and he fell into the river. He drowned and his best man married the woman and they were very happy together, the baby grew up to be a great hero.”

Jack nodded in understanding.

“Don’t worry no one’s drowned a runaway groom since the thirties,” Lena added. 

Jack looked at her suddenly very concerned. Lena gave him her best Luthor smile. He turned to Kal. Kal gave him a reassuring smile. 

“Shall we begin?” Kal asked.

 

Kara was relieved when it was finally over. She thanked the priests politely and escaped to a private prayer room with Alex so she could breathe for a few minutes.

“How are you holding up?” Alex asked giving her a look.

Kara sighed and collapsed onto the pile of cushions on the floor. 

“Exhausted,” Kara said dramatically covering her face with an arm. “I can’t feel my face from all the smiling.”

Alex chuckled watching Kara massage her cheeks. 

“It did seem a bit long for just an announcement,” Alex said lying down beside her.

“Four sets of prayers Alex. Four! You’d think they’d want to make it quick but no. We had to do the whole thing because tradition. I swear I’ll be hearing that chant in my head all day. I don’t know how Kal stands it. I only needed to say it once and sign a banner but noo I’m the crown princess so we had to march around the temple with every set of prayers so all the priests could bless us.”

“Mon-el seemed to have fun,” Alex said sitting beside her.

Kara sighed.

“Yeah he did,” she agreed.

Mon-el had been beaming through the whole thing clearly having a great time as the priests threw holy water on him while she recited the prayers.

“Even if he was dripping all over the floor at the end,” Alex added.

Kara smiled. After four trips around the temple Mon-el had what was probably a couple of litres of holy water thrown on him.

“I’m pretty sure he’ll show up though,” Kara joked. 

Alex laughed.

“If he doesn’t I’ll drown him,” Alex said. “We don’t have time to find you a replacement groom.”

Kara sighed.

“Well there’s always the best man,” she said.

Alex sighed and gave Kara an apologetic look. Kara covered her face again waving a hand for Alex to tell her.

“He made Mxy his best man. Apparently they go way back.” Kara groaned. “Yeah I was gonna warn you. Once I found out it was too late to get him to change it.”

Kara huffed out a breath. 

“Fine, if he doesn’t show Lena can be queen.”

Alex chuckled and there was a polite knock on the door.

“Come in,” Kara said.

The door opened and J’onn smiled at her. 

“I have been informed that if you hurry you can join Kal, Lena, and Jack for lunch at the palace before you have to leave with her majesty for the Guild meeting.”

Kara sighed and got up.

“Can you get Mon-el and sneak us out the back so I don’t have to smile any more?” she asked.

J’onn smiled at her and nodded.

 

Kara was smiling again the moment she saw the picnic set up in the rose gardens with their lunch. She let go of Mon-el’s hand and skipped over dropping dramatically beside Kal.

“Please tell me-”

Lena handed her a plate of potstickers.

“We heard you had to do the traditional ceremony so I thought you’d want some comfort food,” Lena said.

“You are the best usurper ever,” Kara said selecting a potsticker.

Kara moaned as she chewed. Mon-el dropped beside her and reached for a potsticker only for her to growl and move them out of his reach. Mon-el laughed and held up his hands.

“I’ll wait for the next ones.”

Kara smiled at him and he leaned in for a kiss. She presented her cheek.

“Aw no fair,” he said pouting.

“You tried to take her potstickers dude. I did warn you she doesn’t share,” Alex said.

Mon-el sighed and looked at Jack.

“Does Lena share food?” he asked.

Jack smiled.

“I’m not foolish enough to try,” he said kissing Lena on the cheek.

Lena smiled at Jack and picked up a sandwich.

“So Kal,” Kara said smiling at him. “Any chance you’ll bump off Father Zod and be high priest in time for my wedding?”

Kal laughed.

“Sorry Kara, that would be desecration of the temple.”

Kara scoffed.

“So is ejaculating in the holy water but that’s never stopped people.”

Mon-el started choking on his apple. He looked at Kara wide eyed as everyone else laughed. Kara grinned at him.

“Did you just-”

“Yes,” Kara said turning pink. “Queens are allowed to say ejaculating.”

“But they can’t say fuck?” Lena asked smiling.

“Fuck yes we can,” Astra answered.

Everyone turned and Lena laughed. Kal, Alex, and Kara all received a kiss on the head from Astra as she passed.

“Lena dear pass me a sandwich,” Astra said dropping onto the blanket with a sigh. “I just had to sit through a call with the British prime minister over that damn trade agreement. The man is an idiot.”

Lena handed one over as Astra looked around.

“Where’s everyone else?” she asked.

“Winn has the afternoon off to celebrate his mum’s birthday and Nia has some big important date with Brainy,” Kara said with a sigh.

Astra looked at Lena.

“Sam and Jess are in the parliament archives digging up everything they can on coronation elections and copying it.”

Astra sighed.

“They’d have more luck searching the church. They have the majority.”

Lena lay back with a groan.

“That’s good isn’t it?” Mon-el asked. “That way you don’t have to win over the politicians-”

“No,” Astra said cutting him off with a sigh. “The church hates me and, I suspect by extension, Kara. Have done ever since I successfully changed their tax status.”

“Huh?” Mon-el asked looking around.

Lena rolled over to look at Mon-el.

“Okay so you know how every parish has a priest,” she said selecting another sandwich. Mon-el nodded. “Well if we go to election for the monarch the highest priest in each parish gets to vote as the representative of the people in that parish. Which considering the number of parishes makes them the majority. So because they hate Astra they won’t vote for Kara.”

Mon-el nodded slowly.

“Why do they hate Astra for their tax status?”

“Back in 1997 her majesty used her queenly powers to change the church’s tax exemption status-”

“And they haven’t stopped complaining about it ever since,” Astra said.

Mon-el looked between Astra and Lena confused.

“It was a very clever bit of politics your majesty,” Lena said.

Astra smiled at her.

“I still consider it my greatest achievement as Queen.”

Mon-el continued to look confused. He looked at Jack who shrugged indicating he had no idea either.

“The change to their status Mon-el,” Lena said “Means that every year every church must submit their records to the tax office to show they aren’t run for profit, and so any profit they do make is subject to general taxation laws which means the big churches like the great temple in Argo are often hit with a sixty percent tax rate on their year’s profits.”

Mon-el whistled.

“Now that tax money, because it was technically donations, is then given directly to the various regional councils for use in providing local health care initiatives. Since 1997 taxation of the church has been the single biggest revenue source for all regional health care in Krypton. We’re talking close to a couple of billion dollars in the last decade alone and they’re mad about it.”

Mon-el looked over at Astra his eyes wide.

“Okay so Queen by election is bad for Kara,” he said.

Astra smiled and nodded. Kara turned to Kal.

“You’ll vote for me right?”

Kal laughed.

“Sorry Father Zod is highest in Argo so I don’t get a vote. If I could though, I would.”

Kara pouted.

“Zod?” Lena asked. “Isn’t he a minister?”

“They’re brothers,” Astra said with a sigh. “Eldest son to parliament, second to the church and their sister is a member of the foreign office, though she is far more pleasant to deal with. She’s actually our ambassador to China.”

Lena gave Kara a look.

“Well I guess we know who Argo would vote for,” Lena said.

Kara groaned and ate another potsticker to comfort herself. 

“Thankfully it won’t get to that,” Astra said turning to smile at Mon-el and Jack. “Thanks to these fine gentleman you’ll marry and a few days later we shall have a new Queen. Speaking of-” She turned to Kara. “We need to go get ready for the meeting with the Guilds.”

Kara sighed and nodded getting up. Astra held out a hand and Kara helped her to her feet.

“I’ll see you all later,” Kara said smiling.

They all waved and Mon-el immediately went to grab the last potsticker only to see it already stolen by a grinning Kal.

 

DAY 19

Kara sighed looking up at the stars. Alex had been with her for a while the two of them trying to name all the constellations like they’d done as teenagers. But Alex had started yawning and after some gentle prodding left to get some sleep leaving Kara alone on the grass looking up at the cloudy night sky. She shivered but didn’t go in. She needed the comfort of the night sky to try and work through her thoughts. 

Mon-el had gone off to spend the night with the boys and Kara found herself grateful for his absence. They were becoming good friends and she did enjoy his company but his continuing campaign to try and win her heart simply made her feel awkward. His penchant for making these attempts in public only added to her discomfort as she had to play along. 

She heard someone approach and then a familiar red blanket was wrapped around her shoulders. Kara smiled and looked up at her aunt. 

“What has you out here all alone little one?” Astra asked gently.

Kara sighed again. Astra nodded and sat down pulling Kara into her arms like she had when Kara was a teenager.

“What’s on your mind?” she asked tucking Kara’s head under her chin. 

Kara wasn’t really sure how to begin so she sighed again snuggling into her aunt’s familiar embrace. Astra’s arms tightened for a moment and she felt a soft kiss on her head.

“Take your time little one. I’ll be here,” Astra whispered.

Kara contemplated how best to answer.

“It’s about Mon-el,” she finally whispered.

“What has he done?” Astra asked.

Astra’s fingers combed through Kara’s hair causing her to relax a little more. It was a soothing gesture Eliza did whenever Alex or Kara got upset but wouldn’t talk about why. More than one confession had been pulled out of them by the gentle feel of Eliza’s hand in their hair.

“Nothing really,” Kara said playing with the edge of the blanket. “He’s been great really. Everyone is really into our relationship and they all believe it. The church is letting us marry and it’s all going as planned. I just… I wish he’d believe me when I say I’m not going to fall in love with him. He’s a really great guy but he’s not… not the person I love and all I feel for him are normal friend emotions. No matter how many flowers he gives me or times we kiss for the cameras.”

Astra nodded silently her hand continuing it’s soothing work.

“And no matter how nice his body is I’m not interested in doing anything more with him. And I’m… I’m a bit worried he’s going to get resentful of that,” she admitted.

“Is he being pushy?” Astra asked her voice very calm.

“No, he just… he keeps suggesting we do more stuff like Lena and Jack. And I can’t tell him they’re acting and there’s nothing between them because I don’t know if I can really trust him to not try and use that in some way to convince me to do something similar. Like I’m fine with hugging and kissing him for the cameras but I don’t really want to share a bed or have him carry me around.” She looked up at Astra and saw her aunt’s thoughtful expression. “Is it wrong that I’d let Jack do that but not Mon-el?”

Astra smiled down at her and shook her head.

“Jack is a very charming and sweet man, and you know you’re safe with him. It makes sense you would feel more comfortable with him because you know if Jack was in your bed, even if you were both very naked, he would respect your boundaries. It sounds a bit like Mon-el has been pushing against yours.”

Kara sighed and nodded.

“When I tell him to stop he does and then he apologises but it’s just frustrating that he sees winning my love as some challenge as though he just has to earn enough romantic points and he’ll be on top, but that’s not how love works.”

Astra nodded holding her closer.

“Would you like me or J’onn to have a word with him, or maybe even Eliza?”

“No,” Kara said with a sigh. “I don’t think it’ll do anything but make him feel embarrassed. I’ll talk to him about it when we get some time alone. But yeah, it’s just been upsetting me. And…”

She played with the blanket trying to pull the words out.

“I miss Lena. I know why we can’t spend a lot of time together, and we’re both really busy with everything but I- I really miss having her by my side. I have Mon-el there in a way even if it’s pretend but… he just seems to make me miss her more by contrast.”

Astra was quiet for a minute just holding her. 

“When you are Queen there will be a lot of pressure on you to look and behave a certain way. There will be a lot of hard decisions that will weigh you down. But that doesn’t mean you’ll be alone on that throne-”

“I know Mon-el will help and it will make us stronger,” Kara said pulling away a little. “Rhea was telling me-”

“No that is not what I meant,” Astra said pulling her back. “And you can ignore almost everything that comes out of Rhea’s mouth she knows nothing about being queen no matter what she thinks. You will have me, Alex, and Eliza standing around you. Winn, Nia, and J’onn will be between you and any threat. And from what I’ve seen, you'll also have Lena. I have no doubt she’ll be holding your hand through everything.”

Kara turned to look at her Aunt a little surprised. 

“And yes Mon-el will be with you as well. He may currently be your future husband but he will only be the Prince-consort, you will be Queen of Krypton. You will be the one crowned, you will have the power. And if he causes you any problems then it would be very easy to… mitigate him once you are Queen.”

Astra waved her hand in a vague gesture that could be interpreted several ways. Kara looked more seriously at her aunt.

“Mitigate him?” she asked.

Astra smiled nodding.

“If you never want him in your bed than he won’t be. What happens in the private rooms of the King and Queen, so long as it is legal, cannot be discussed in public without your consent. It is law.”

“Seriously?” Kara asked turning properly to look at Astra.

“Yes,” Astra said laughing. “The law was mostly used by kings to get away with having mistresses but for you it can mean that Mon-el is never permitted in your bedroom, and he will never be able to speak about it in public. You could even take Lena instead and he won’t be able to say anything.”

Kara smiled and looked at her Aunt.

“What’s the punishment if he does?” she asked.

“It used to be death,” Astra said with a small smile. “It’s technically treason so anything from 5-30 years in prison. And any outlet the publishes the news gets in a lot of trouble.”

Kara laughed.

“Really?” she asked.

Astra nodded.

“You can thank your great grandfather for that. He was a very private man, and so to guarantee no one discussed what went on between him, his queen, and his mistress he made it treason to discuss what happened in his bedchamber as part of Kryptonian personal privacy laws.”

“Then how do you know what he did?” Kara asked.

“Because the law about the royal bed doesn’t extend to the palace gardens, and they were being photographed one day owing to some particularly fine weather. It just so happened he, the queen, and their lover, decided to enjoy the weather too.”

Kara laughed hiding her head in Astra’s shoulder.

“Oh no,” Kara mumbled.

“It was a bit of a scandal at the time even if the queen reportedly found it hilarious. She had the picture framed and hung in her office.”

Kara smiled. Astra sighed and kissed her forehead.

“You will be a wonderful queen little one, and I promise, you will always be safe here with your family. We will always protect you.”

Astra rose then and left. Kara sat wrapped in the blanket looking at the sky. She wasn’t sure how long it had been when she saw Lena walking across the gardens towards her confused. Lena saw her and smiled. Kara opened the blanket and Lena looked around to check for any witnesses, or more likely Nia and her Nerf gun, then joined her. 

They sat together under the blanket looking at the stars. Eventually Kara let her head fall to Lena’s shoulder as she let herself relax. Considering what Astra had said perhaps she could still have Lena once she was Queen. She sighed and Lena carefully took her hand. Kara snuggled closer letting her eyes close for a little bit. Taking just a moment in the middle of everything to be with Lena.

Lena turned her head and pressed a soft kiss to her head. Kara fought the urge to tilt her head up higher to get a proper kiss.

“I miss you too,” Lena whispered.

Kara smiled.

“I didn’t say anything,” Kara whispered.

“Yes you did. It just wasn’t out loud.”

A combined hens night.

10. The hens night

DAY 20

In the interest of saving the palace staff and royal guard stress and with the short time frame they decided to just combine their hens nights. Though Kara liked to say it was so they could have twice the fun. The boys decided to have a combined stag do on the same night. Brainy had to call up reinforcements for the palace guard as Astra requested they make it a blackout event to prevent any “unsavoury pictures.” Which Kara and Lena easily translated as “in case the two of you get drunk and try to make out in a cupboard.” It was a precaution they didn’t begrudge her. Alex also decided she and Sam would stay sober to keep an eye on the two of them. 

They were all given the rundown on the security plans and blackout protocols by Brainy with a powerpoint presentation he’d clearly gotten Nia’s help on. Kara having been through several blackout events before only half listened as she went over the planning for the night itself. No footage taken of the royal family, all guest devices searched, the guard controlling all images out, and the phone jammer activated. Nothing seemed to have changed from the last time. The last time being Astra’s birthday and possibly the wildest party Kara and Alex had ever attended. 

Astra had a reputation as a composed and decisive queen with a sharp wit and strong ties to the military after the five years she spent serving. Kara had always known her as the steady, supportive, and wise woman of her teenage years. So seeing her aunt’s behaviour at the party had been a bit of a surprise at the time. At one point she’d seen Astra standing on a table in tight leather pants, her shirt ripped open revealing her bra, and drunkenly belting out We will rock you with an equally drunk Cat Grant as several high ranking members of the Kryptonian army thumped the beat on the table. 

She didn’t even remember most of the night having taken part in a drinking game with Alex and Winn after they watched Eliza cheat in an arm wrestling competition with what looked like a Kryptonian air force officer. Kara vaguely remembered singing a duet with Barry Allen and some kind of race with Oliver Queen after Alex disappeared with a Captain Lance, but it was all a bit hazy. 

She’d had woken the next morning on one of the downstairs couches wearing an army flak jacket instead of her shirt and covered in glitter snuggling a stone dragon she discovered had been attached to one of the palace fountains until she’d somehow ripped it free in a display of strength. 

Winn had been asleep next to her, technically. He’d fallen asleep under a coffee table with his feet on the couch beside Kara. He was shirtless with his tie around his head and a dozen lipstick marks on just his arms. His back was covered in grass stains and they never did find his shoes.

Major Lane, whose jacket Kara had apparently won in a footrace, was found the next morning in a garden bed without her pants but still with her boots and cuddling a matching stone dragon. She still sent Kara pictures of the adventures Mushu had with the army. The last email had him being used as a relay rod by new recruits during some kind of army vs air force games.

Kara smiled and refocused as Brainy explained how all recordings from the night would be copied onto a separate drive. To maintain visual coverage they would treble the number of guards in the unmonitored spaces and keep a log. Brainy finished his presentation with a list of all staff with exemptions to the no personal devices rule. Everyone thanked him and Kara looked over to see Mon-el on his phone playing some kind of game. She decided to let it go. 

After the briefing Kara, Mon-el, Lena, and Jack got to sit around the table for another couple of hours finalising the plans for the night. 

Kara had nominated a slumber party theme early so they wouldn’t be forced to wear something ridiculously uncomfortable. Lena had no objections as long as there was some very good scotch available all night for her. With the main theme and entertainment quickly sorted came the hard part, the guest list. Winn and Jess had a list of potential guests drawn up and then came the elimination process and it was indeed a process, one that felt like it took hours.

Each guest, apart from a few obvious ones had to be carefully considered against the others. Kara having little exposure to ninety percent of the possible guests turned to Lena for help as they pared it down in stages. 

Jack smiled as Lena handed Kara a sheet of names.

“Everyone in red has a problem with someone else in red so invite one or the other. I’ve given them matching symbols,” Lena said. “My general recommendations are marked on the right to help you make your choices.

Mon-el laughed glancing over at the list that was almost completely covered in red.

“Hey y’all I’m just going to do a video,” Jack said reaching for his phone.

They all gave their permission as Lena picked up the drafted invitations.

“All animosity is set aside when it comes to party planning,” Jack said smiling as he held his phone up to focus on the two women then slowly panned over to Mon-el who was considering a list of Kryptonian beers.

“We have our priorities straight,” Mon-el said looking up to smile at the camera. “And that priority is a party.”

Lena glanced at Jack at the word straight and smiled. He winked back focusing the camera on her and Kara again.

“Are you fine with the invitations as is or did you want to make changes?” Lena asked holding them out to Kara.

Kara glanced at it and nodded.

“They’re good, they’ve spelt your name wrong though.”

Lena sighed as she saw they had indeed spelt her name wrong. She marked the correction and highlighted it as Jack chuckled.

“Here’s the guest list, do you-”

Lena immediately put a cross through three names.

“You’ll thank me in the future,” she said.

Mon-el slid a sheet of paper towards Jack as he chuckled.

“Okay so here’s a list of possible activities us guys can do,” Mon-el said.

Jack looked down.

“Are there strippers on this list?” he asked warily.

“No strippers,” Kara and Lena said in unison.

“Agreed, but I will note strippers are traditional,” Mon-l said with a sigh.

“What about male strippers?” Jack asked smiling. Lena gave him a look and he sighed. “It was worth a try.” 

Lena rolled her eyes.

“That means no strippers for you too,” Mon-el said.

Kara and Lena exchanged a look.

“Well we weren’t planning on having any so that’s easy enough,” Lena said.

Jack put his phone down and set about quickly editing the video and drafting a post. He slid it across for Lena to approve.

Everyone embodying the motto of the house of El in the name of a good party. El Mayarah.

Lena scoffed and nodded her approval before showing it to Kara who added her nod of approval as she circled potstickers on the proposed menu and added several hearts and exclamation points.

 

DAY 22

The hens night was almost like it’s own parade with all the guests turning up in the most out there pyjamas they thought they could get away with. Several of the ladies had apparently commissioned onesies in their family colours and various animal designs. 

Kara and Lena stood together greeting everyone by name with Nia and Jess whispering who everyone was before they went through. After security did their checks they were escorted through to the ballroom set aside for the event. Alex and Sam were inside helping make sure everyone had a cocktail and got settled around the various board games set out to amuse them.

As the last of the guests were welcomed Kara and Lena finally made their way inside and took their places on a couch situated in the middle of the room. They were handed a glass each. Scotch for Lena and a cocktail for Kara. 

“Alright,” Andrea said getting up on a chair to address the sixty other women. “Now we all know what this night is about,” she said with a wave of her hand.

Lena covered her face. Andrea getting on a soapbox never went well for her. Kara noticed and Lena saw the moment Kara realised who Andrea was.

“You invited your ex to your hens night?” Kara whispered to Lena.

Lena sighed.

“Two of them technically,” she muttered.

Kara turned to look around and then stood up.

“Which one of you is Roulette?” she asked loudly. She gave Andrea an apologetic look. “Sorry Andrea.”

One of the women already at a poker table rose. Kara looked at her.

“That would be me,” the woman said frowning at Kara in confusion.

Kara looked at her then at Lena and turned back to smile brightly at Roulette.

“Thank you. I was just curious who you were,” Kara said sitting down again. 

Andrea looked from Kara to the faintly blushing Lena and started laughing.

“Oh Lena,” Andrea purred. “Did you-” 

Lena jumped to her feet to interrupt Andrea.

“I do believe the point of this night is to get the two of us drunk in various creative ways,” Lena said quickly. “So I suggest you get to it.”

She drained her glass somehow managing not to cough. There was a cheer from the guests and a fresh scotch was provided to Lena as Sam called out that they should start the night with a few games. Lena sat down again and looked at Kara.

“I must say, she’s not who I imagined,” Kara said with a smirk looking over at Roulette.

“She had braces at the time,” Lena muttered defensively. “Once they came off she got very bitchy so…”

Kara snickered and Andrea appeared with a chess set. A sip for every lost piece. She placed it between them and Lena smiled at Kara.

“Shall we?” she asked.

Kara smiled.

“Only if I can keep my clothes on this time,” she whispered.

Lena laughed Kara moved her first piece.

 

Four hours later Kara knew she was a bit drunk. Okay very drunk. The chess match had gotten her tipsy then she’d been pulled into a chaotic game of scrabble. Now she as playing Jenga with Nia and Sam. She had to take a shot for each loss. She’d looked over and felt a pang of jealousy as she saw Lena playing poker with Andrea and Roulette. Then Lena had looked up and smiled at her and she felt warm all over. 

That warm feeling had of course led her to fumble and bring down the Jenga tower causing a cheer and Alex forced her to take a shot of tequila.

Kara decided to return to the lounge at that point and watch everything. Lena joined her soon after with a handful of poker chips. 

“I see someone won,” Kara said.

Lena grinned.

“Then I deliberately lost to Andrea so she’d stop threatening to bring up the year I tried to be a goth.”

Kara laughed.

“I remember you telling me about that. I’m still sad there was never evidence of the nose ring.”

“Well considering how long I actually wore it that’s not surprising,” Lena said with a sigh

“Lillian took it out?” Kara asked.

“Lex actually, and completely by accident. It got caught on my blanket when he flung it off me taking the nose ring with it. He did help stop the bleeding though and never told mother dearest. He spun some story about accidentally cutting my nose with his watch so mother never found out.”

Kara smiled and leaned closer.

“Everything you tell me about Lex makes him sound like the best big brother ever.”

Lena smiled.

“He was. When he was able to get out of bed.”

Lena sighed her eyes turning sad. Kara leaned forwards and held her hand.

“It wasn’t your fault,” she said gently.

“I know,” Lena said meeting her eyes. “My psychologist managed to convince me of that. But I think a part of me will always wonder if I could have saved him somehow.”

Kara nodded and squeezed her hand gently.

“Have you finished reading it yet?”

Lena shook her head. 

“Not all of it,” she said. “I can read what I’ve heard but not beyond.

Kara nodded and they looked at each other for a long moment. Lena drew in a deep shuddering breath.

“If you need help, I’m here,” Kara said.

Lena nodded giving her a soft smile one hand coming up to tuck some of Kara’s hair behind her ear.

“Hey why are there no strippers?” Andrea asked loudly.

Kara and Lena turned. Lena quickly wiped away the  lone tear that had managed to escape her eyes.

“Because if the guys aren’t allowed strippers neither are we,” Kara said smiling.

“Well then Lena, care to fill the role?” Andrea asked.

“Oh no!” Lena said quickly putting down her glass. “Absolutely not. I have left those rebellious days behind.”

“Burlesque is hardly rebellious,” Andrea protested.

There was sudden interest from every one of the guests.

“Still no,” Lena said firmly.

“Come on…” Andrea said pleading. “You just need a couple of fans…”

“Nuh uh…” Lena said crossing her arms unmoved.

“Aw come on Lena…” Kara said fluttering her eyelashes a few times.

 

Lena found her resolve melting against Kara’s rapidly appearing pout.

“That is-” Lena sighed. “If it ever gets out there goes my reputation. So no. Unless…”

There were several looks exchanged by the guests as Kara and Lena had a silent conversation with their eyes. Lena took a deep breath and nodded.

“If someone has fans then fine, but you need to be in the chair otherwise I’m the only one risking something. And every phone is going in a bucket because there will be no record of this,” Lena said.

There was a cheer and Andrea ran off to go get the fans she’d apparently had packed in her car. Lena sighed and looked at Sam as a half dozen guards went around gathering the guests phones.

“I think I just got played,” Lena said.

Sam laughed and nodded.

“Andrea always did know how to drag you into trouble.”

Lena considered that a serious understatement. She had a feeling this was going to go as well for her as all previous schemes of Andrea’s.  

Andrea returned with a set of pink feathery fans and Lena sighed letting Andrea and Kara drag her up from the couch. Lena warmed up a little and called out the song to Nia who had decided to be DJ for the night.

“I can’t believe I keep being made to do this,” Lena said glaring at Andrea.

“Bitch please,” Andrea said loudly. “You’re fucking proud of it.”

Lena knew she would normally argue but she was too drunk to remember her usual response so settled for a classic raised eyebrow that had no effect. Kara happily took her place in the chair and turned to wink at the assembled guests behind her.

Lena sighed and took her position. She glanced behind herself out the large window at the hedge lined garden beyond. She gave Kara a look then looked at the windows.

“It’s a closed private terrace,” Kara said with a shrug.

Lena was going to suggest they close the curtains when the music changed to a familiar beat and she found herself automatically moving the fans into position as Kara gripped the chair. 

Lena moved forwards the fans moving exactly in time with the beat as the crowd of drunken women screamed their approval. She was still wearing a t-shirt and shorts but that didn’t seem to matter to the crowd as she teased glimpses beneath the fans. Then her eyes fell on Kara and didn’t move.

She remembered the last time she’d done this dance. Just her and Kara. She could see Kara remembering. The fans had been red then not pink and she hadn’t been wearing anything underneath, unless you counted body glitter.

Lena couldn’t take her eyes off Kara her body moving through the familiar movements as the sound seemed to dim losing the screams and whistles of the other women as she saw Kara there. Kara who was gripping the chair with knuckles turned white as she stared at Lena with undisguised longing. 

Lena moved closer and closer to that face. To those lips she missed so very much. She moved into the final set of moves and instead of the final reveal she moved closer to Kara and as the final few beats came through the speakers she hid them both from view behind the fans.

“Just one,” she whispered and did what she’d been longing to do all night, she kissed Kara. 

Kara’s hands came up immediately holding her in place for a moment extending the kiss just for a second longer and Lena swallowed a moan of longing before she pulled back and moved away leaving Kara breathless as she took her bow before the screaming women and folded the fans subtly wiping her lips of any evidence. 

She returned to her place on the couch and immediately drained what was left in her glass. Maybe if she got drunk enough she wouldn’t be able to taste Kara anymore. Kara seemed to have the same idea the two of them proceeding to not look at each other as they got drunker. Eventually not even the thumping music and women all talking over each other could keep Lena from falling asleep.

At one point she found herself almost completely pulled from sleep then she felt a familiar warmth wrap around her from behind surrounding her with the smell of sugar and sunshine. She sighed snuggling deeper into Kara’s arms with a smile. There also seemed to be purring coming from somewhere. 

Notes for the Chapter:

I know I know, hardly original making Roulette and Andrea Lena's exes but it's practically fandom canon at this point. Also I think this fic is making me feel even more cheated by the CW's mediocre use of Astra. She had so much badass potential. Anyway I hope y'all had fun with this chapter and I'll see you for the next one. Stay safe.

11. The wake up

Notes for the Chapter:

Minor TW, for Mon-el being a manipulative dick.

DAY 23
Kara felt so warm and comfortable she never wanted to wake, then she shivered as cold air rushed up her legs. She snuggled deeper into the warmth. The smell of floral shampoo filled her nose and she smiled. Lena. She sighed holding her tighter and felt Lena snuggle closer with a small noise.

Then the sudden sound of horns cut through the peace and warmth she felt and ignited a splitting headache. Kara groaned and blinked her eyes open. She looked down and saw Lena snuggled in her arms looking just as confused as she while the Kryptonian national anthem continued somewhere nearby.

Kara lifted her head and groaned again seeing a dozen people, including her sister and Nia, gathered around her bed looking at them. Lena looked up and muttered a curse before closing her eyes.

“Good morning!” the intruders chorused. 

Kara did a quick inventory. She and Lena were still in their pyjamas from the night before and considering Lena's expression as the music continued they had both had far too much to drink to have done anything.

Kara sighed hiding her face in the pillow as Lena sat up and looked around before crawling down the bed to get the blankets pulling them back up and cocooning herself in them.

“Hungover, come back with aspirin and breakfast or don’t come back at all,” Lena said.

Kara made a noise of protest beside her. Everything was too loud, and now she was cold.

“You can’t just go back to sleep now. You’re in Kara’s bed,” Andrea said laughing.

“Watch me,” Lena said closing her eyes and snuggling deeper into the blankets. 

Kara rolled over and tugged at the blankets.

“Lena share,” she said. 

“Get your own,” Lena mumbled back.

“Lena,” Kara whined. “Hungover, be nice.”

“Fine.”

Lena lifted the blanket so Kara could get under too.

“Your Princess thanks you,” Kara mumbled snuggling in closer. 

Lena chuckled already half asleep surrounded by thick blankets and her face buried in Kara’s shoulder blades. Kara wriggled a little closer with a sigh and Lena held her closer for a minute. Then there was rapid footsteps as someone ran into the room.

“Lena Kieran Luthor you get out of that bed this instant!” Lillian yelled.

Lena groaned and Kara made a pained noise. Kara sat up and looked at everyone. She waved a hand.

“Royal decree, everybody out!” she winced at the sound of her own voice and Lena poked her in protest. “Except Lena because she too is in pain.” 

There was some hesitation and a lot of protestation from the various guests. 

“Guards!” she yelled.

There was a sudden rush as three royal guards appeared.

“Please escort the guests from my bedchamber. Then send someone back in a half hour with aspirin-”

“And coffee,” Lena said from the blanket cocoon.

“And coffee,” Kara repeated.

She fell back onto the bed with a pained groan rolling back into the blanket cocoon with Lena and burying her face in Lena’s neck with a wave of relief as the guards quickly removed the interlopers. Kara took the chance and snuggled into Lena. Just for five minutes. Lena made a little humming noise of approval as Kara wrapped her in her arms. Once she was sure they were alone she kissed Lena’s neck earning her a happy sigh.

There was a soft meow and Streaky leapt up to stand on top of them. She seemed to consider them for a moment before curling up and beginning to purr quietly. Kara sighed happily and let herself drift off to sleep once more.

 

Lena emerged first still in her pyjamas and holding coffee she went to the main breakfast table and was greeted by a smiling Jack.

“And how are you this morning?” he asked as she kissed his cheek.

“Very hungover,” Lena said.

Jack smiled and kissed her hand offering her a seat before adding sausages and eggs to her plate with an excess of toast.

“So I hear someone woke in a new place this morning,” he said.

Lillian put down her papers and glared at Lena. She slid a tablet violently across the table a video already open ready to play. Lena checked the caption.  In the interest of assisting with the rift between their houses…

Andrea had posted it. Lena sighed and clicked play. At least it wouldn’t be the fan dance. 

She saw a somewhat shaky video of her unconscious body being carried bridal style by Sam and Kara hung limply over Alex’s shoulder. There appeared to be quite a few women involved in whatever this was. Including a grinning Roulette and Jess.

“This is a fantastic idea,” Sam half whispered.

“They’ll certainly love it,” Alex said turning to smile at the camera. 

The camera dropped a little capturing video of Streaky jumping around Alex’s feet as they went through Kara’s suite heading for the bed. One of the maids had pulled the blankets down on the bed. The camera moved off Streaky as Alex carefully lay Kara in the bed. Kara stirred mumbling something but Alex quickly stroked her hair.

“Shh Kara go back to sleep,” Alex said. “We’re just tucking you and Lena in together.”

Kara smiled and mumbled something that made Alex chuckle. Once they were sure Kara was still asleep the giggling women motioned to Sam. Alex helped Sam put Lena carefully in the bed next to Kara. Lena rolled over and sighed as she reached Kara snuggling against her.

There was a lot of smothered giggles as Kara wrapped her arm around Lena holding her closer. The two of them didn’t stir after both evidently falling deep asleep.

“In the interest of assisting with the rift between their houses…” Andrea said from behind the camera. “We’re just going to leave these two to rest together because they’re clearly very tired.”

Sam and Alex nodded pulling the blankets up and tucking them in. Then they all froze falling silent as Lena mumbled something smiling as she nuzzled into Kara’s neck. Kara sighed her leg shifting under the blankets to cover Lena’s. There was more smothered laughter.

“Is it safe to leave them alone after everything they drank?” a concerned woman asked from somewhere behind the camera.

Alex smiled and pointed at herself.

“I’m sober and as her medical adviser I’ll be keeping a watch on them tonight to make sure they’re okay.”

Streaky scrambled onto the bed and cautiously padded up to look at Kara. She placed a paw on Lena’s back and meowed softly to Kara then turned to look at Lena. She curled up beside Lena and begun purring prompting several aw’s from the watching women.

The video ended and Lena smiled putting it down.

“That was kind of cute, especially with Streaky,” Lena said.

“That’s not all,” Lillian snarled making Lena wince. “This morning she made a second post.”

Lena sighed exiting the video and scrolling up past several pictures and videos of the hens night that, according to Andrea’s posting, went all night without them. The most recent post was another video. This one ominously captioned Waking royalty in style… Lena clicked play and double tapped for fullscreen.

The video was of Kara’s bed again clearly now in the morning. Streaky had moved to the end of the bed and was looking at the new arrivals curiously as the women entered talking in hushed whispers. A very tired looking Alex sat in a chair by the window. Lena was still snuggled against Kara their bodies hidden by the blankets but clearly still close.

“Aw they look kinda cute,” Queen Mia whispered giving them a soft look.

Streaky wandered over and Mia carefully picked her up moving her off the bed.

“Okay,” Alex said getting up. 

There was some smothered giggles then Sam and Alex wrenched the blinds open flooding the room with light. There was a moment of silence as they all waited but the only movement from the bed was Lena burying her face deeper into Kara’s neck.

“Covers,” Andrea whispered.

Two women moved forwards pulling the blankets off them to the end of the bed. There was a lot of smothered giggles as they looked at them. Kara had her hand halfway up Lena’s shirt holding her close the other on Lena’s butt holding her in place half on top of her. Lena has a hand fisted in Kara’s shirt and was snuggled tight against her. Her face almost in Kara’s boobs and her legs tangled in Kara’s.

Lena shivered nuzzling closer. Kara reacted by pulling her closer and breathing deeper for a moment. The camera shifted to show Alex who was looking at the bed with an exasperated look.

“I swear, every fucking time.” 

She turned and signalled through the window which prompted the start of the national anthem. Lena smiled watching the rest of the morning wake up take place the video ending when Andrea was ushered out by a guard.

Lena put down the tablet and sighed turning to Jack. He smiled back at her and waggled his eyebrows earning a smile.

“What were you thinking?” Lillian demanded prompting another wince from Lena.

“Ow head ow, mmm warm soft place,” Lena said.

Jack laughed softly. Lillian did not she continued glaring at Lena.

“I remind you that the throne is on the line,” she said. “If people work out what is really-”

“And I remind you,” Lena said calmly cutting her mother off. “I passed out on a couch. It was others who put me in bed with Kara.”

She bit into the toast with a sigh.

“And yet you stayed in bed with her,” Lillian said.

“I repeat, ow head ow, mmm warm soft place.”

Jack smiled at her and Lena sighed looking at her sausages. She didn’t know if she had the energy to cut them. Jack noticed and leant over to do it for her. Lena looked at him.

“And this is why I’m going to marry you,” she said stabbing a piece with her fork.

Jack laughed.

“Well considering someone did a fan dance last night…”

Lillian’s paper was down again in an instant as she glared at Lena. Lena for her part looked at Jack shocked.

“How do you know-”

He pulled out his phone and opened a video for her.

“Since we’re playing what happened last night, I thought you might want to see this before I delete it.”

Lena leaned over resting her aching head on Jack’s shoulder as he hit play.

The video was quite dark but it was clearly Mon-el, Maxwell Lord, Winn, and James hiding against a low hedge. The camera turned to capture Mxy and Lord Devereaux on Jack’s other side as well as a couple of others she couldn't make out in the dark.

“Ready lads?” Mon-el asked grinning. 

Jack turned the camera back to Mon-el.

“I for the record want it noted I am against crashing the hens night,” Jack said. “If they catch us they really will make us sleep on the most uncomfortable couches they can find.

“Why are you here then?” Mon-el asked grinning at Jack in the darkness.

“To see you all get in trouble.”

Mon-el scoffed and they crawled across the lawn and hid up against another hedge. Then they heard the music change to a song Lena was very familiar with.

“Hey they got strippers,” Mon-el said looking over the hedge.

The camera shifted as Jack moved then dropped back down refocusing the camera on Mon-el who was glaring over the leaves.

“That’s not a stripper,” Jack said laughing. “That’s Lena.”

“Wait what?” Mon-el asked lifting his head a little higher to see better.

There was a rush of movement as the men all shifted to look over the hedge.

“And I do believe that’s Kara in the chair she’s performing for,” Jack added with a laugh.

There was a long moment of silence as they all stayed still looking over the hedge. Mon-el turned from the hedge to look at Jack. He looked furious.

“Is there something I should know about your fiance?” he growled. 

Jack was quiet for a second then he laughed again.

“Mon-el, dude… chill. They’re just having some fun. I’ve seen Lena do that fan dance a dozen times, it’s basically her party trick at this point. And she’s not even stripping.”

Mon-el was looking at Jack very seriously. 

“You don’t seem bothered by this at all,” Mon-el said.

Jack laughed softly.

“No, it’s burlesque. Why are you?” he asked.

“Because I’m marrying Kara,” Mon-el said firmly.

There was laughter from Winn, Jack, and James.

“Yeah so she can be queen,” Winn said. “Chill dude. Now let’s get out of here before they find out we were here and seek revenge. I do not want to know what Lena will do if she ever hears about us seeing that.”

Jack grinned at the camera and winked before ending the video.

Lena groaned and reached for her toast again.

“You’re all sleeping on the couch,” she muttered.

“Even me?” Jack asked.

Lena smiled and patted his cheek as Sam came in grinning at Lena.

“You can sleep in Sam’s bed,” Lena said.

Jack laughed and Sam looked confused.

 

Kara emerged from her room showered, dressed, and feeling at least partially recovered. She smiled seeing Mon-el waiting for her the hallway.

“Hey Mon-el. How was your stag?” she asked with a yawn,

Mon-el jerked his head up looking… angry.

“What the fuck were you doing with Lena?” he hissed.

Kara blinked surprised at the sudden anger.

“What are you talking about?” she asked frowning at him.

“I’m talking about that fan dance striptease she did for you last night,” he growled moving closer. 

Kara chuckled then saw his increased anger.

“Relax she didn’t strip it was just the dance. It was Andrea’s idea because we didn’t have strippers.”

He still looked furious.

“What’s your issue? It was just a bit of fun, you know, the whole point of a hens night,” she said glaring back at him.

“My issue,” Mon-el said through gritted teeth. “Is the woman I’m marrying is letting the woman trying to steal our throne rub all over her and I saw the video of your morning snuggle. What were you thinking?”

Kara looked at him very seriously.

“Firstly, it’s my throne,” she said glaring back at him. “Secondly it was a hens night, a fairly tame one. If you saw the footage of this morning then I’m sure you also saw the video of them putting us in the bed together-”

“You still went back to sleep with her there-”

“Yes,” she said letting her irritation show. “Because I was hungover and so was she. Believe it or not I’ve platonically shared my bed with a lot of people before. Would you be getting angry like this if it was Alex or Nia or even Sam who had been curled up with me?”

Kara glared at him not backing down. Mon-el sighed and rubbed his face.

“I’m sorry,” he said with a sigh. “I really shouldn’t have said all that. You’re right and I’m an asshole.”

“Yes, you are,” Kara said still glaring. “Mon-el this is an arranged marriage.”

“I know, and I just…” He made a frustrated noise. “I’m sorry I have some personal baggage and it’s- I’m taking it out on you and I shouldn’t and I’m sorry. I… My ex, Imra. She cheated on me with a woman and so I’m a bit… sensitive about it happening again and I know this is arranged and you tell me you’re still in love with someone else and all that, but I really like you Kara and I… I do want to see what could exist between us and I’m really really sorry I was being such a prick. You didn’t deserve that.”

Kara looked at him for a long moment but his repentant expression didn’t change. She sighed. She was too hungover for this.

“Okay, apology accepted. But don’t ever pull that kind of thing with me again. I am not your ex and this is not the same. I am Kara Zor-el, Crown Princess of Krypton and I will not tolerate that from anyone, certainly not my fiance.”

Mon-el nodded his head bowed in apology. He looked rather like a kicked puppy and Kara sighed again rubbing her aching head.

“Lena is my friend Mon-el, I’m allowed to share my private space with my friends.”

He nodded again.

“You’re right. And again, I’m an ass.”

She gave him a small smile and nodded. 

“Yes you are.”

He grinned back. 

“I think… hey can we do something today, just us. I know you’ve got the rest of the morning with your aunt but maybe we could go for a ride this afternoon and have a picnic lunch or something. I’d love to get to talk to you some more, just us. I think there’s some stuff I should tell you about me.”

Kara thought about it and nodded.

“Sure. How about you meet me at the stables at one, if you can get it all ready and have them saddle Britney for me we can head out of the trails and I’ll show you one of my favourite spots.”

Mon-el smiled wider.

“Thanks Kara, that would be great.”

Kara nodded again and walked away.

“And Kara,” he called.

She turned and he gave her another apologetic look.

“I really am sorry I… yeah.”

She nodded and left. 

 

At one Kara made her way down to the stables and smiled in greeting as she saw Mon-el waiting with the horses and a couple of picnic baskets. She smiled at him and he beamed back then his smile faltered a little and he looked kind of nervous.

“Are you still… like do you want to come out for a ride with me or are you still mad?” he asked.

Kara gave him a look.

“I know I’ve kinda got a strike against me for being an ass and I- yeah just let’s go. I’m sorry I shouldn’t have mentioned it. Again I’m an ass and you deserved better.”

He quickly mounted up and she followed giving Britney a fond pat as they headed out.

The ride was mostly pleasant. Mon-el was determined to be good company telling her jokes and showing off until they reached the outlook and brought their horses to a stop. He insisted on setting everything up for their picnic and bowed to her once he was finished. Kara smiled and dropped onto the blanket reaching for a bread roll. He dropped next to her. Kara waited but Mon-el didn’t start talking. He fidgeted with his hands looking nervous.

“Take your time,” she said giving him a small smile.

Mon-el sighed heavily looking out over the view for a moment.

“I really do love racing,” he said. “It’s always made me happy. My mother wanted me to be a politician like my Dad but I just… I never liked it. I was the eternal disappointment. The only son who was never smart enough, never ambitious enough. But they were wrong. I may not have been good at academic learning or following all the political stuff but I am smart, I am ambitious.”

He gave her a smile.

“My parents were furious when I went off and became a driver. Then I started winning and well…” Mon-el gave her a look. “The unofficial motto of house Daxam is win at any cost.”

Kara raised an eyebrow.

“That sounds a bit intense.”

He nodded and sighed heavily again.

“Yeah… But I adapted and in the end I do always win. Maybe not in the field my parents wanted but I always win. Whatever I have to do. Which has made me a few enemies I guess. I know early on in my career I was so busy trying to show my parents I could do it that I made mistakes. I used their tactics. And I won. I won a lot and it cost me relationships, and people, and I didn’t care. But then I won the race. I won the Krypton 100 last year and I…” He sighed deeply. “I realised I didn’t have someone special to share it with. I realised I’d been a terrible person. Selfish, reckless, impulsive. People loved me as Mon-el the racer but no one really loved the guy behind the title, the real me. I didn't even like me. Mxy will tell you I was a massive jackass a year ago. Probably why we got along so well now I think about it.”

He gave her another look trying for a self deprecating smile but she didn’t smile back. He rubbed his neck frowning.

“So I decided to change. I started working on actually maintaining friendships. I stopped treating women as disposable and got a serious girlfriend, Imra. I fell for her, hard.”

He sighed deeply and looked out over the view for a minute then back at Kara. She gave him an encouraging look.

“So you fell in love, did she love you back?”

He sighed shaking his head.

“No, but I thought she did. She played me so well. I even got engaged to her. Like an idiot. But it was all…” He shook his head again and turned away. “Sorry you probably don’t wanna hear about how stupid I was. Kinda wrecks the whole cool guy thing.”

Kara sighed and reached out squeezing his hand.

“If you wanna talk about it I’m here to listen. If not that’s okay too,” she said.

He looked at her for a long minute and nodded turning back to her.

“I should tell you. It’s important for you to know since we’re getting married and all and…”

He glanced at her and Kara gave him an encouraging smile.

“Promise you won’t think I’m a pathetic loser?” he asked.

Kara rolled her eyes.

“Mon-el I’m not going to think you’re a pathetic loser because someone broke up with you.”

He chuckled.

“Right yeah. Sorry I guess I’m just used to being with the boys and my family. We uh… yeah sorry too much emotions and I’m sorry you probably don’t wanna hear about this just forget I said anything an we can go back to talking about how great I am at racing-”

Kara put a hand on his shoulder and gave him a look.

“I think you want to tell me. So take a few breaths and take your time. Like I said I’m here to listen if you want. That’s what friends do.”

He smiled at her and gave her hand a kiss.

“You’re too good to me.”

Kara laughed and shook her head removing her hand. Mon-el took a few deep breaths visibly hyping himself up to tell her. Kara spent the time picking grapes to eat.

“We dated, then as I said I fell hard and so we were engaged and she’d moved in with me. I wasn’t even charging her rent.”

He looked at her again and Kara nodded for him to continue. Her hangover was mostly gone but she still felt tired and hungry and dealing with Mon-el's reluctance to share his personal baggage was making her even moreso. She forced herself to pay attention giving him another encouraging smile.

“So I uh… I got back from a trip a day early and I was super excited because I had this whole night planned out. I had bought dessert and I was going to cook her a dinner and do this whole thing with a ribbon for her to follow and there’d be rose petals all over the bed and I had a bottle of champagne, like the whole romantic thing…”

He sighed rubbing his face. Kara watched as he cleared his throat looking out at the view. 

“I heard her come back and I was in the bathroom. I was going to call out to let her know I was there, but I’d just gotten out of the shower and I’d forgotten a towel, again like an idiot. So I was going to ask her for one when I heard her talking with someone else and thinking it might be awkward if I just came out in… well in nothing. I thought they’d only be in the bedroom for a minute and then I could duck out dry off and put on some clothes then surprise her…”

He looked away pulling at the grass. 

“Long story short I caught her between her best friend’s legs and overheard enough to know the truth about her feelings. She was just after my money. She was planning to marry me, fake it for a while then divorce me. She’s an American so it wouldn’t have taken a year. Her plan was six months, we got- we were going to marry in Vegas. She said it was going to be this big spur of the moment secret thing so we could…”

He rubbed his face again and glanced at her nervously. 

“I was an idiot is the short version. I believed her and that’s why the whole thing with you and Lena made me act like an asshole. I was so scared you were doing the same thing and like I know this is an arranged marriage. I know you’re not the kind of girl who just jumps into things quickly. I just I saw the girl I was going to marry in bed with her best friend and it was just that moment all over again. I was hiding naked in my bathroom dripping on the floor as I listened to the person I loved break my heart.”

Kara reached out and squeezed his hand ignoring the sudden lead feeling in her stomach at the implications of his words. He gave her a hopeful smile then turned away wrapping his arms around his legs.

“That doesn’t excuse it I know. I’m a jackass. I was wrong to yell at you. It’s- I just really hope one day you can forgive me for treating you like that because I’m not that guy and I wanna show you I’m not that guy. That’s why I’m here baring my soul to you because I can’t- I can’t let you think I’m that guy.”

Kara sighed quietly and moved closer hugging him.

“I know you’re a good guy Mon-el and I can see you’re sorry. I won’t pretend what you did was okay and if you do it again I’ll kick your ass and then Alex will.”

He chuckled softly.

“I sure hope so. I’d hate to hurt you. I never want to hurt you Kara. I’d… I’d cut my own hand off before hurting you I hope you know that.”

Kara smiled and he sighed running a hand through his hair seeming frustrated with himself.

“There’s something I wanna say but I just… I’m not a words kinda guy. I’m an actions guy and…”

He gave her another self deprecating smile. She smiled back and gave him a quick hug.

“Take your time,” she said.

He blew out a breath. Kara took the moment to add slices of ham and cheese to her roll.

“You’re really great you know that? Like you’re brilliant and… and… so beautiful and sweet and nice and kind and you’re gonna be a wonderful queen.”

Kara smiled at him.

“Thanks.”

He turned to look at her more fully taking her hands in his.

“Look I’m gonna say something and I want you to know there’s no pressure.” 

Kara felt a sinking in her stomach as he looked at her but she nodded. Best get it over with.

“I know this is all just an arranged marriage. I know you just drew my name out of a hat or something-”

“I picked you out of a slideshow,” she said giving him a small smile. “Or rather Nia did.”

He laughed softly.

“Well then I owe Nia a lot because I- I wasn’t in a great place before. The whole Imra thing was pretty recent. I’m not sure if you knew. But I like to think Rao does bring us opportunities when we’re ready. He did it with my racing and I think he did it with you. I know you’ve still got feelings for someone else. I understand it was recent too and I get it. I really do I know that despite everything if Imra stood before me now and begged me to take her back that it was all some terrible mistake there’s a part of me that would be really tempted.”

Kara gave his hand a reassuring squeeze as his voice faltered.

“I understand you’re not really in a place for anything more but… Kara you’re my kryptonite.” She gave him a confused look and he quickly laughed. “That is to say… I think I’m in love with you.”

Kara felt that sinking feeling growing heavier. Mon-el rushed on.

“And again I’m not expecting you to reciprocate. I know you’re not looking for that and you warned me that it was unlikely but I also know the other person isn’t stepping up but I’m here and I am willing to do whatever it takes to help you become Queen. I’m here and I want to be your King, eventually. The thing is I just want to know if you can see even the tiniest piece of you that- I just want to know I’m not seeing things. You do care about me and… I just want to know if you’d be willing to try us for real.”

Kara sighed and gently took her hands back. Her stomach felt like it was full of lead and something in her was looking at this man who was sitting before her confessing his deepest fears and feelings and she could see the desperation… but she couldn’t hurt him like that. She couldn’t give him false hope.

“Mon-el we’re friends, and I do genuinely care about you. I have been having a lot of fun with you and there’s a reason why everyone is so happy for us. We are a good pair in a lot of ways…”

His eyes were lighting up and Kara sighed. She was messing this up.

“In another life maybe. If I hadn’t met someone else and fallen in love with them then I can see how I would have fallen very hard for you. You’re handsome, sweet, funny, and charming. I do like you Mon-el and I am really glad I met you and been able to become friends.”

He deflated.

“I’m sensing a but,” he said pulling away and wiping at his face.

Kara sighed and nodded.

“I’m in love with someone else and I’m sorry I can’t really explain it to you, it’s not really my secret to share in a way and there’s a lot at stake. But there’s a reason I had to ask you to marry me for a year. Because through no fault of theirs the person I love… they can’t. If they could- many things would be easier if they could.”

Mon-el nodded wiping at his face again and clearing his throat.

“I’m sorry but I don’t feel anything more for you than friendship and I don’t think I ever will. I need a husband Mon-el, for a year. For my people. For our people. I’m sure you’ll find your perfect person someday, a woman who will love you back the way you deserve to be loved but I’m afraid it’s not me.”

Mon-el nodded.

“But if they weren’t… a thing. I’d have a chance?” he asked giving her a small smile.

She gave him a small smile.

“Like I said, if I hadn’t met them then yes you certainly would have stood a strong chance. You’re a great guy Mon-el, just not the person for me I’m afraid.”

He nodded sighing.

“Uh I know this is kinda… is it okay if I go off by myself for a bit. Just so I can clear my head. I-”

“Of course. I’ll pack this up and take it back. Take all the time you need.”

He smiled at her getting up quickly and heading for the horses.

“You’re still gonna marry me though right?” he asked with a more playful tone. “I haven’t completely screwed up everything right.”

Kara laughed.

“Yes, we’re still getting married. It’s going to be a very over the top ceremony with dozens of cameras and more pomp than substance and we’ll still get to run around being the most athletic royal couple for a year so they don’t try to take my throne away from me,” she said.

Mon-el smiled and sucked in a deep breath. He looked around and gave her an apologetic look.

“I’ll see you later.”

She smiled back and he rode off. Kara sighed and looked at the picnic. She started packing it up and attaching it to Britney in between bites of her roll. The picnic was afterall supposed to be her lunch. 

She returned to the palace the lead feeling in her stomach still present but now there was something slimy and unpleasant mixing with it that she couldn't quite place. Alex frowned when she saw her.

“What happened?” she asked.

“Oh Mon-el just told me some deeply personal stuff and… yeah I just feel bad for him,” she said. Alex gave her a look. “It was all really personal otherwise I’d tell you, but... he’s had a bit of a rough time personally and I just feel bad because I think I’m making it worse with all this.”

Alex sighed.

“He said he was in love with you didn’t he.” Kara nodded. “Did you tell him you love someone else?”

She nodded again. Alex sighed.

“He took it well, or as well as he could really. I did tell him way back at the start that I wasn’t looking for that but I still feel kinda bad,” Kara said giving Alex a look. “He’s off clearing his head.”

Alex sighed again and rubbed her neck.

“Well as long as everyone knows where you both stand.”

Kara still felt bad. Alex hesitated.

“He doesn’t know who-”

Kara quickly shook her head. 

“I didn’t really want to tell him after all he’d said. I think it would have felt… I think it might upset him for reasons I can’t say.”

Alex nodded.

“Alright. Well once the wedding and coronation is over we can all sit down and work out the best way to organise everything so… well to minimise heartbreak all around.”

Kara nodded. She fidgeted with her engagement ring. She quickly stopped at Alex’s pointed glare. If not for her fear of losing it she’d have it off her finger more than it was on. Instead she spent a lot of time twisting it. She’d taken it off twice without even realising it and if not for Winn she would have completely forgotten to put it back on.

She sighed at least Nia had been the one to pick the wedding bracelets so she wouldn’t have to constantly worry about losing that. Though some part of her wondered if the ring itself was the problem or if it was what it represented.

Notes for the Chapter:

Show of hands who thinks Mon-el told the absolute 100% truth in that conversation?
