A picture on the front page sends them all into damage control.

12. Too late to change plans now

DAY 24

Lena woke when her door was thrown open and her mother storming in.

“Lena get up!” Lillian yelled.

Lena was instantly fully awake and alert as Lillian thrust a newspaper at her. She looked at the front page and swore flinging back the covers. She grabbed her robe and threw it on making her way out to wake Jack and Sam. Sam was in the room next to hers and was already stumbling out after hearing Lillian’s yelling.

“What-”

Lena handed her the newspaper and went to bang on Jack’s door. He stumbled out in his boxers his hair a mess. 

“What’s the emergency?” he asked blinking sleep from his eyes.

“We have a problem,” Lena said. 

Sam held up the front page and Jack groaned. He grabbed his robe. They emerged from the suite just as Kara came running around the corner wearing a sports bra and some very short shorts. For once Lena wasn’t distracted by all the skin on display.

“Lena we-”

“Already seen it,” she said.

Kara looked a little panicked. 

“What do we-”

“What Luthor’s do best, we have a meeting. Then we make someone pay,” Lillian said.

She turned and pointed at a nearby guard. 

“Go wake the Queen. Tell her we have a situation that requires her attention. We’re having a meeting ten minutes from now in the main breakfast room.”

The guard looked at Kara.

“You heard her,” Kara said. “Also wake my sister and Eliza.”

She whipped out her phone and begun making a call as the guard raced off.

“Nia, are you at the palace? Perfect. Wake up Winn and get to the main breakfast room in ten minutes.”

J’onn appeared looking a little sweaty.

“Princess I would-”

Kara cut him off.

“J’onn we need to have a meeting both entourages and security can we-”

“I’ll have the main breakfast room swept and cleared for you,” J’onn said straightening up.

“Excellent, have Brainy-”

Brainy raced around the corner half dressed in his uniform his shirt and jacket still undone and his sword in one hand.

“Princess!” he saluted.

“Excellent timing Brainy, we need the main breakfast room prepped and cleared. Your presence will be required there’s a security breach. Have-”

Vasquez appeared around the corner. She quickly bowed.

“Is there-”

“Main breakfast room in ten minutes. We need it prepped and cleared,” Lena said cutting her off. “The close entourage of myself and the Princess will be there.”

Vasquez nodded and nearly collided with Mon-el as he walked around the corner.

“Hey babe you-”

“Get showered and changed,” Kara said. “We have a meeting in the main breakfast room in ten minutes.”

She handed a confused Mon-el the newspaper. He laughed and winked at Lena. 

“You look great,” he said.

Lena sighed.

“Thank you. But it’s not about the picture.”

Mon-el gave her a confused look. 

“You’ll understand at breakfast,” Lena said. “Go shower and get changed now. You’re gonna need that smile because we’re all gonna have a busy day today.”

Mon-el looked concerned but nodded. 

They all filed into the main breakfast room ten minutes later everyone dressed and most of them heading straight for the coffee.

“J’onn, Brainy is this room secure?” Astra asked taking her place at the head of the table.

“Yes your majesty,” they chorused. 

“I double checked it myself,” J’onn added.

Astra nodded and they all looked around.

“Right let’s start with the easy part,” Astra said picking up the paper again.

The front page of the Argo Post was a picture clearly showed a smiling Lena through the windows with the fans partially covering her one leg out and her head thrown back. The background was blurred and a headline splashed underneath in white. 

Royal fan-tasy. Wannabe Queen caught doing striptease.

Lillian glared across the table. Kara quickly held up her hands.

“I swear we had nothing-”

“We know Kara,” Lena said. 

“It is interesting however that the picture doesn’t have you in it,” Lillian said taking the paper and looking at the picture again. “Considering what I know of that night.”

Jack hummed his agreement and leant over Lillian's shoulder to look at the picture more closely.

“Nia?” Lena asked.

Nia looked up from her tablet and thought for a moment.

“Okay so either they are on team Kara and hoped to cause drama for Lena in an effort to prop up Kara, or they’re holding the other pictures back with Kara for a bigger splash later-”

“Sorry but can someone explain to me what the hell is such a big deal about this picture?” Mon-el asked. “Like I get that it’s embarrassing for you Lena but why are you all acting like we just woke up to a horse head in our beds.”

Everyone looked at him. Lillian looked at Astra then Kara. They gave her an identical look that conveyed both an apology and a plea for her to be patient.

“The problem Lord Daxam,” Lillian said turning to Mon-el. “Is that this picture should never have made it out of the palace. We engaged blackout protocols remember? Anyone not part of the royal entourage had all their electronic devices checked before coming and going to prevent these kinds of things. The staff all had wired headsets, and the few personal devices allowed were under strict controls. So for this to have gotten out means someone got around that, and then the question becomes, what else they could have gotten out.”

Mon-el looked surprised and then looked around the table.

“Adrea was posting shit all night along with all the other-”

“Yes, but while the video may have been captured on their phones they were uploaded via a computer,” Lena said. “Blackout protocol means that anyone who wanted to post anything needed the assistance of the palace guard to do so.”

Mon-el was looking increasingly concerned.

“But how come my phone wasn’t-”

Jack laughed.

“Dude, royal privileges. We have access to a separate network.”

Mon-el was still looking confused.

“Okay,” Lena said leaning forwards and taking out her phone. “So there’s three different wifi networks here at the palace and they’re named and numbered. You, me, Kara, our families. Essentially everyone collectively under the title of royal entourage, our devices are all connected to the palace wifi though the 789 network which has some pretty heavy protections.”

She spun her phone around so he could see the available networks and her phone connected to one called 789. 

“When you first got here Vasquez had to sign your phone into it. But that is just for the royal family and close entourage. General staff and guests use 123, high level palace staff like Vasquez and security use 456.”

She pointed to each of the networks as she talked.

“The night of the parties the security enacted the blackout protocol because there was a strong likelihood of us doing something really stupid while drunk. We can only do that for certain things and it’s public knowledge whenever we do and why. So that meant they activated a jammer that took out phone service and took the guest wifi offline. So everything that was posted by guests that night was done over the 456 network which required a member of the royal guard to check the post wasn’t in violation of the protocols, log into the wifi, post it, and then log out. Everything was checked before it was allowed.”

Mon-el looked around.

“So them snuggling in bed was approved?” he asked seeming a bit pissed.

Alex, Nia, and Sam laughed.

“They were unconscious and as Alex is a member of the royal family, by her involvement it was allowed,” Astra explained. “If however they’d done something like stripped down and ran naked through the gardens, all footage would have been deleted by the guard. When we enact blackout protocols we have a list for what we’re blocking published at the same time. That night it was the usual party list: state secrets, nudity, bodily functions, sexual activity, and so on.”

Mon-el seemed to be thinking very hard. 

“So the problem is less that she’s doing a fan dance and more that someone got through the jammer,” he said slowly.

“Or got onto the grounds and took the footage,” Lena said. “Though considering you boys were apparently watching…” 

Lena looked at Mon-el and Winn glaring. Jack smiled at them as Winn sank down in his chair a bit.

“It’s unlikely to have been an outsider. So now we need to go down the invite list and check who might have been able to-”

“Uh…” Mon-el was looking very awkward. “So if say I had left my phone unattended a few times… could someone have used that to send themselves the pictures then deleted evidence.”

There was a collective groan around the table.

“Are you serious?” Lillian snarled. “I knew you were an idiot but I would have thought you’d be smart enough to-”

“I was fucking drunk alright!” Mon-el snapped.

Lillian looked murderous and Kara had her head in her hands as she slowly counted to ten. Astra took control.

“Enough!" she said raising her hand as she glared at Lillian and mon-el. "We can check the footage from the night see if we can find who used Mon-el’s phone and-”

“That was all on camera?” Mon-el asked.

Lillian gave him a disgusted look.

“Rao how stupid is this-”

“Mother shut up,” Lena growled. “You’re not helping.”

Mon-el looked around a little alarmed.

“I thought the whole blackout thing was the cameras were off that’s why there were so many guards. At least that’s what the briefing-”

“Yes, for the royal family,” Jack said. “We ain’t royalty, not yet anyway, so we got filmed as per usual. The footage was just put onto a separate hard drive for the night so it wouldn’t be with all the regular stuff in case it caught anything by accident. How much attention did you pay in the security briefing dude?”

“Clearly not much,” Mon-el said in a half hearted attempt at a joke.

“Okay so now we know the probable how,” Vasquez said sighing. “Brainy and I can investigate the breach. It shouldn’t take us long to work out who did it.”

Mon-el sank in his chair and crossed his arms.

“So damage control, what’s our game plan,” Sam asked.

“Well firstly I’m going to be making a very angry call to Edge to find out why I wasn’t asked about this before it ran,” Astra said through gritted teeth.

“He’ll weasel his way out that she’s not an official princess,” Lillian said.

Eliza muttered something and begun rubbing her head.

“At least I’ll be able to block him putting out anything else he might have,” Astra said with a sigh. She looked at Kara and Lena. “Is there anything else they might have been able to-”

“If they got the shot of us at the end when I was in her lap it might look a bit suspicious but that’s easily explainable in context,” Lena said.

Lillian turned to her daughter with a glare.

“You were in her lap. Lena I swear to Rao if you two kissed-”

“We didn’t!” Kara and Lena said immediately.

Everyone looked at them.

“We just… almost… did,” Kara mumbled.

Mon-el was looking at her furious.

“You what?” he demanded.

There was a sudden silence around the table as all eyes returned to him once more. Unbeknown to Mon-el several people ran a series of calculations in their heads and came to the same conclusion. Lena looked at Kara who shook her head. Lena raised an eyebrow. Kara shrugged.

“Please have your conversations out loud,” Astra said cutting them off. 

“Mon-el,” Kara begun turning to him. “So you remember how I explained that I needed a husband for a year and there was a uh… there was someone I had been with before that I would’ve married instead if I could…”

Mon-el was looking at her very still. 

“Well the thing is… if it got out about who that was it might be well… a problem.”

“A problem?” he repeated slowly. “Why would that-”

“Because it was a woman,” Kara said.

Mon-el was quiet for a long moment as his eyes moved around the table and stopped on Lena. She raised an eyebrow giving him a look. Astra cleared her throat drawing everyone’s attention again.

“Cat Grant wants to know what we’re planning. I’m going to call Edge. Sam, Jess what do you want?”

Sam gave Jess a look and Jess nodded.

“We’re going to need an easy interview for Jack and Lena, Lur-Var will do. Preferably tonight and video, they do better with a camera on them. The interview should only have one question about the fan dance and should instead focus on the throne or other political events. We also need him to surrender all other images, just in case. Then we’ll need Kara and Mon-el out and about being highly photogenic. In regards to talking about it, we should laugh it off. It was a hens night bit of fun. And I think we should make it clear that it was Kara’s idea then it’s not just on Lena.”

Jess gave Kara a look. She returned it with a bright smile easily slipping into character.

“When I heard she knew burlesque I couldn’t believe it, so of course we had to see proof and it was amazing!” Kara said.

“Perfect,” Sam said giving her a smile.

“We should also emphasise how late in the evening it was and how drunk everyone was,” Jess said.

Nia nodded her agreement.

“I suggest a standard three key points,” Nia said. “One, it was Andrea who first suggested it then Kara who convinced her to do it. Two, everyone was fairly drunk at that point. And three, she was really good at it. We should also downplay it as much as we can, don’t bring it up first and laugh about it but only for a moment. It was one small thing that night.”

There was a general agreement and Astra picked up her phone calling Edge and putting him on speaker.

“Your majesty,” Edge said sounding far too smug for seven in the morning. “How can I help you?”

“You can explain why you have pictures of a member of the royal family from a blackout event that you didn’t ask me about before splashing it across the front page.”

“She’s a Luthor not an El-”

“She’s the challenger for the throne she has princess standing until the coronation.”

“But this picture doesn’t contain anything on the blackout list,” he argued.

“She specifically stated that no pictures or recordings were to be taken before she did it. The recording devices of all persons in the room were collected beforehand. If you had called to ask I would have been able to tell you. This picture is in violation-”

“Oh come now Astra,” Edge said cutting her off. Astra’s jaw twitched. “I can hardly be held accountable by a third party’s failure to uphold the rules.”

“You’re on very thin ice Edge and I would be very happy to stomp around a bit to see if it sends you under.”

Edge was quiet for a moment clearly thinking. He sighed.

“I am assuming you have a carrot on offer with that stick.”

“Not so fast Edge. Since you wanted to argue semantics. I am officially telling you if you have any other such pictures they were taken by a third party against the wishes of the royal family under a blackout and so unless they contain an illegal action their publication will result in the most serious of consequences.”

Edge sighed.

“I acknowledge your warning and will behave accordingly,” he said.

“Good,” Astra growled. “You’re also going to give them a nice easy on camera interview tonight. Preferably Lur-Var or another equivalent. Responding to that front page is to be a small part of it, only one question, the rest is to be a normal political piece. And you may report on the story as it is already out but your coverage better be wrapped up in the next 48 hours or you’ll find your reporters struggling to get any royal access.”

Edge muttered something but sighed.

“Done. And my carrot?” he asked.

“That depends who gave you the photo.”

“An unknown source. It was emailed to us by what looks like a burner email account.”

“A member of the royal guard will be visiting to go over what you were sent,” Astra said.

“My carrot?” Edge prompted.

“That is the carrot. You got a picture of a royal doing something perfectly legal on the night of a blackout from a burner email and you published it. My not slapping you with a lawsuit and confiscating your computers for three months is the reward for your cooperation.”

Astra hung up on Edge's protest. She looked around the room then her phone chimed with an alert.

“J’onn could you show our latest arrival in. He’s at the gate.” J’onn opened the door and left. “Lena, you’ll join Kara and I at the petitions tomorrow. I want to make sure people are seeing you as a princess too. I’ll send you the petition list so you can go over it in advance.”

Lena nodded her agreement and they started to break up. Eliza and Alex left with Vasquez saying they’d start contacting guests so they could get people on record and see what other stories from that night could be used to distract. Sam, at a look from Lena, decided to join them. Winn and Brainy said they’d start on the footage from the Stag party. Jess and Nia moved to one end of the table talking quietly as they got to work. 

J’onn returned and held the door open for Adam Grant. Kara jumped up and greeted him with a warm hug. He returned it with a bright smile briefly lifting her off her feet.

“Hello again Princess, it’s been far too long,” he said.

“Agreed,” Kara said smiling up at him. “I had hoped to see you earlier for something less work related but things got a bit busy.”

They exchanged a long look and Lena watched Mon-el bristle. 

“Engagements are like that,” Adam said still smiling. “Please allow me to give you my congratulations now. You look very happy together.”

He nodded to Mon-el who seemed somewhat mollified. Lena exchanged a silent look with her mother. Lillian nodded. 

“Is your mother joining us?” Lillian asked looking at Adam. “We’ll want her assistance with this.”

He smiled at Lillian. 

“She’s got the prime minister on her show this morning so she sent me instead. I’m to get the game plan and take it back to her so she can get started on her part.”

Astra cleared her throat drawing everyone’s attention.

“Lillian if you want to speak with Cat, she’s going to drop by tonight.”

“Oh really?” Lillian asked glaring at Astra. “How convenient for you.”

“Yes, we were going to have a late dinner together,” Astra said smirking at Lillian. “You are of course welcome to join us. Eliza will be there too.”

Lillian gritted her teeth.

“This isn’t high school Astra.”

“I am well aware of that Lily.” Astra gave Lillian a very odd smile. It had a lot of teeth. “If it was you’d still be yelling at me for sleeping with your boyfriend.”

Lillian looked murderous and rose suddenly almost as if intending to throw herself at Astra. She glared at Astra for a minute as everyone waited. Astra continued to smile at her, then her smile shifted a tiny bit.

“Dinner’s at nine, I’ll make sure the guards know to let you in,” Astra added.

Lillian stormed from the room slamming the door. Astra chuckled to herself then noticed the rest of the room’s occupants staring at her in shock.

“We all make mistakes. I was seventeen, in a rebellious phase, and horny. I didn’t even know he was her boyfriend at the time.”

Lena and Kara tried to smother their laughter as Adam chuckled and Mon-el looked at Astra seemingly horrified.

“Wait you and Eliza having dinner with Cat Grant tonight?” Kara asked confused. 

“And Lillian,” Astra said getting up. “She’ll complain about it but she always comes. She hates being left out. We were just going to eat, drink, and reminisce about our youth but now I expect we’ll be discussing our game plan regarding today’s developments.”

Astra gave Lena a kiss on the head.

“Lena, see if you can clear a little time later this afternoon so you can be seen going for a ride with me and Kara. Jack you too. If Edge wants to argue you’re not a princess then it’s time we started making it obvious.”

Lena smiled up at Astra.

“Thank you.”

Astra nodded giving Lena a warm smile. She left then giving Adam a fond pat as she passed. Adam sat down in Alex’s vacated seat.

“Alright, what’s going on?” he asked.

Lena noticed Mon-el’s glare at Adam sitting next to Kara. She quickly gave Adam a rundown of events and the plan. Nia and Jess finished their write up and moved down to brief them all. The discussion dragged on.

When it became clear they were going to be a while Mon-el excused himself saying he was going to go see if any of “the boys” had seen whoever used his phone that night. Kara kissed his cheek in farewell as they continued to discuss possible questions that might come up. 

 

Kara motioned to J’onn. He nodded and stepped outside taking a position by the door. Alex, Winn, and Sam returned a minute later. Once they had sat down Adam gave them all a smile and a nod. 

“So I take it there’s more to this,” Adam said. 

J’onn discreetly closed the door most of the way leaving just enough for him to see and hear whatever was happening in the room.

“What do you know?” Lena asked sitting back in her chair.

“Well I know that Jack is putting in a marvellous performance, you and Kara have been trying not to touch for the last ten minutes, and Mon-el’s the jealous type.”

Lena smiled.

“I’m really wishing we could have used you,” she said.

He nodded apologetically. 

“It would have been more fun,” Jack said. 

Adam gave Jack a look and got a grin back. Lena sighed.

“How do you always know everyone?” she asked.

“Because unlike you I could fly under the radar and make more friends,” he said.

“The accent helped,” Adam said with a smile.

Alex tapped the table to silence them.

“As convenient as the number of beards would have been,” she said. “We have other concerns. What are we telling Mon-el?”

“We need him at this point it’s too late to swap him out,” Nia said. “Even if we did have another candidate the announcement has been made and a different groom would raise concerns with the church.”

Kara and Lena exchanged a look. 

“He’s not a bad guy he just…” She sighed in frustration. “He caught his ex fiance cheating on him with her best friend so me and Lena doing anything reminds him of some painful memories. He also thinks he’s in love with me and is holding out hope that I’ll return his affections.”

Kara rubbed her head. She could feel a headache starting. Alex placed a comforting hand on her shoulder. 

“I’ll have a word with him,” she said.

Jack shook his head.

“After knowing him this long I don’t think it will help. He’s impulsive and, I’m sorry, but I think he’ll lash out if he thinks he won’t get what he wants. He nearly flipped a table when Deveraux won an arm wrestling contest against him. He apologised after and said it was because he was drunk and an idiot but… we can’t risk it. We need to give him a story of some kind otherwise he’s going to make up his own and if he loses in that story he might be a problem.”

Kara straightened with a sigh.

“Adam what do you suggest?” she asked turning to the media heir.

“Play into his stereotypes until the wedding. If he works it out Lena you’re the evil whore who’s playing everyone. Kara you’re just a hapless victim, everyone else is in their factions and working together for their own ends. Once you’re married then I think you tell him it’s a political plan.” 

Adam looked at Lena who looked at Sam. Sam thought for a minute the rest of them waiting as she shifted through ideas in her head.

“I’ve got two suggestions,” Sam said leaning forwards. “Option one suggest that the whole challenger thing was a setup to make sure there was another well known heir apparent. We say there’s been threats against Kara and Lena is an old friend. We say she had no intention of challenging but then there were credible threats on Kara’s life to force political change. So she challenged to make sure the line wouldn’t lead straight to election if anything happened to Kara.”

“That could work,” Nia said enthusiastically. “It would also explain to him why you’re both such close friends and why you’re not saying anything bad about each other, and all the behind the scenes cooperation. All we’d have to do is have it be only a select few know about the threats to explain why he wasn’t told earlier. Brainy could easily say something about him not having high enough clearance to know until they were married.”

Alex nodded and looked over at J’onn who opened the door and gave a subtle nod indicating he could find something as ‘evidence’.

“What’s option two?” Adam asked.

“Arranged marriage,” Sam said.

“It is-”

“Between Kara and Lena,” Sam added. “The intention being to resolve the unclear lineage. A behind closed doors deal after the challenge. Kara got the throne so it stays with the house of El but she’s to divorce Mon-el after a year and a day so that she and Lena can marry. It’s closer to the truth and aligns nicely with it but then when it all happens it’s not personal it’s just politics.”

They all exchanged looks. 

“Worst case Lena becomes Queen by default if I don’t marry,” Kara said with a sigh. 

“What if he tells people about your relationship?” Adam asked.

“If he tries mother will murder him,” Lena muttered.

Everyone looked at her surprised.

“If word gets out about us being together the church might annul Jack and my marriage leading to an election and my disqualification as a candidate. We should be able to counter it easily enough though, it’s not like he has proof of anything. They can’t just take his word over everyone else’s for this without serious backlash. We’ve been careful so far but I suggest we now start considering Mon-el a threat until proven otherwise.”

Kara sighed. It felt dishonest and she hated that they were all sitting around debating this. At the same time she found herself increasingly thinking they were right. But still… 

“He’s not that bad,” she said. “He’s got some family and ex stuff that makes him really sensitive. I think if we explain to him the situation he…” 

Lena gave her a look. She leant across the table and held Kara’s hand giving it a gentle squeeze. Kara found herself sighing. Alex spoke up drawing their attention.

“We know Kara. He’s a great guy and has many lovely qualities. He’s fun and charming and we want to trust him. But I also know if you truly trusted him you would have told him already. He might not turn out to be a threat but right now his failure to respect your personal boundaries, tendency to lash out, and jealousy means we can’t risk him knowing anything he can use against you, or against Lena.”

Kara nodded to her sister. She knew they were right. She took a deep breath forcing herself to think about the options as dispassionately as she could.

“I suggest we go with the arranged marriage argument. It’s closer to the truth and I think if we mention the threats angle he’ll get overprotective,” Kara said.

Everyone nodded and she saw Jess, Nia and Winn making notes.

“What was the ex’s name?” Adam asked taking out a notepad.

“Imra, I don’t have a last name but I can try and get it. From what I know she’s American and they were together until quite recently. He said they were engaged so I would guess at least six months but not longer than a year. They apparently became a thing after he won the Krypton 100. She also apparently has debts of some kind, or her family does.”

Adam nodded noting everything down.

“I’ll do some digging in case she has anything that might be a problem later. If she gave him emotional baggage it’ll be useful to know all the details.”

Alex looked around the table.

“Are we all clear on the plan?” she asked.

There was a general murmur of agreement and they all broke off. Kara lingered and so did Lena. Alex gave them a warning look but left closing the door behind herself. Lena came around the table and looked at Kara.

“Be honest with me. Do you think it’s wrong for us to hide our relationship from him?” she asked.

Kara sighed heavily and shook her head.

“No. I think I’m more concerned about dealing with him after the marriage. He’s generally great and I consider him a good friend at this point but I am feeling a bit… stuck with him.”

Lena hummed softly and reached out to stroke Kara’s cheek. Kara sighed falling into the contact.

“What about being married to him concerns you most?” she asked.

“The emotional effort of pretending to love him really. I think all the fake kisses and smiling for three hundred pictures a day is wearing me out,” she confessed. 

Lena gave her a sympathetic smile and Kara covered Lena's hand with her own holding it in place.

“Once you’re Queen you’ll be able to step it back. Winn can claim you’re busy with all the new monarch bureaucracy so you don’t have as much time for relationship posing. The main racing season is also coming up and I suspect it wouldn’t be too hard for him to be sent out for some foreign policy work. A nice trip to America where he tours some racing stuff while you and I are stuck in back to back meetings until late with politicians and UN delegates.”

Lena leaned over giving Kara a smile. 

“We’ll just be working very closely together late into the night,” Kara said.

She grinned and tugged Lena’s other hand pulling her closer. Lena smiled and pressed a soft kiss to the top of Kara’s head.

“Don’t worry love, I’ll be with you every step of the way.”

Kara sighed looking up at Lena and feeling an overwhelming surge of love. She rose and wrapped her arms tightly around Lena. Lena hugged her back the two of them breathing deeply.

“I miss you,” Kara said.

Lena hugged her tighter.

“I miss you too.” She leaned back and sighed brushing her hand over Kara’s face again. “Not long now. Once you’re crowned we can tell him a story and start taking advantage of all those lovely royal privacy laws. If he objects to us continuing our relationship while you’re married, well…” Lena gave her a small smile as her thumb traced Kara’s lips. “I will wait for you, however long it takes.”

Kara sighed at the touch.

“I’m sure racing season will be a very busy time of year for me and I’ll unfortunately not be able to join him for most of it,” she said smiling at Lena.

Lena smiled back.

“There might even be a few large anonymous donations to the team so they can partake in some extra races internationally.”

Kara smiled brighter. For a minute they just looked at each other the temptation to kiss hanging in the air between them. Lena eventually sighed and gave Kara’s hand a final squeeze before she left. 

Kara waited a moment and J’onn came in. She sighed sitting down again. Everything felt so much harder all of a sudden, as though an extra weight had been added to her shoulders. J’onn didn’t say anything he just walked over and placed a comforting hand on her shoulder. Kara looked up at him feeling a little of that crushing weight ease.

“El Mayarah,” he said quietly.

Kara nodded. Then her phone rang. She sighed seeing Mon-el’s name. 

“I know who did it,” he said excitedly.

“What?” she asked confused.

“I decided to try and be all sneaky and so I just called a few of the boys all casual like and I asked them about the picture. Mxy told me everything. He picked up my phone by accident because we both have a red Kryptonian team phone case. So when he realised he sent the pictures to himself, deleted the originals, and then swapped our phones back. He forgot he had them until yesterday. He sent them to Edge from a burner email because he thought it would help you out if there was an election.”

Kara blinked surprised and then motioned to J’onn who started calling everyone back. 

 

A half hour later kara and Astra sat in her office to make the call to the Prime Minister. Kara sat in the armchair listening as Astra went over the situation. Considering who his father was, and Mon-el’s vouching for him as it just being an accidental switch, they’d all decided to let it slide with a warning. 

“I appreciate your coming to me first about this your majesty,” Drom-Mosk said.

“Prime Minister you can understand that while we understand mistakes are sometimes made, but the point remains that he did violate privacy laws. It would be one thing if he did it while under the influence that night, but to have held those photos for days then sending them in a crass attempt to use what I can only describe as American tabloid tactics.”

Drom-Mosk made a noise of agreement.

“I agree your majesty, and I can assure you he understands fully the severity of what he has done. I have also impressed upon him the idiocy of such an action. I swear this is not like my son at all. I am just as surprised as you. He-”

Astra hummed and looked at Kara. Kara shrugged. 

“We wish to make this disappear and quickly. But at the same time you can understand our concerns about having your son involved in certain things if he cannot be trusted to follow the rules.”

“Of course your majesty, whatever you feel is best.”

“Perhaps Mxy can be occupied somewhere else for a week or two and be unwell the day of the wedding. To officially remove him as best man would be tantamount to telling and as I am sure you and your son wish to avoid-”

“Yes of course. Mxy will of course quietly disappear and I shall be keeping a far closer eye on him.”

Astra hummed her agreement again. 

“Good. I shall consider you personally responsible if your son turns up in connection with any such activities.”

“Understood your majesty.”

Astra hung up. She sighed leaning back in her chair.

“That makes Maxwell Lord best man,” Astra said.

Kara sighed and approached her aunt. She picked up a glass ball on Astra's desk rolling it in her hands. 

“He's married, so if anything happens now Lena will become the Queen,” Kara said quietly.

Astra nodded. They were quiet for a long moment then Astra opened her arms and Kara sank gratefully into the hug. Astra pressed a kiss to her head.

“It will all be okay in the end little one.”

 

DAY 25

Breakfast that morning was early and crowded. The two entourages combining so they could go over the petition information for a final time as they ate. Cat Grant had apparently spent the night and was sitting next to Lillian wearing a pair of glasses and turning her nose up at anything but coffee. Eliza was sitting opposite Cat and was apparently struggling with a hangover too as she nibbled on toast and winced everytime anyone raised their voice above quiet speaking volume. Lillian watched her with a smile.

“I told you not to try and keep up with me,” she said. 

Eliza sighed heavily and said nothing. Cat smiled though. Astra looked up from her papers and glared at Lillian.

“Please don’t. We’re all having regrets this morning,” she said raising her voice slightly.

Lillian looked at Astra as Cat and Eliza winced.

“I’m not,” she said showing a lot of teeth. “I’m feeling-”

Lillian stopped talking and looked at Cat as Sam started coughing up her tea. Cat raised an eyebrow at Lillian.

“Lily, stop being a bitch,” Cat muttered. “It’s too early.”

Lillian sighed picking up her teacup. Astra looked back at her papers. Kara and Lena exchanged a look over the table. Lena raised and eyebrow and Kara widened her eyes slightly. Astra tapped the table between them. They looked at her. She silently shook her head. Sam cursed and they all looked at her as she righted the maple syrup she’d knocked over.

“Sam whatever is wrong with you this morning?” Lillian asked.

Sam looked determinedly at her plate.

“Nothing,” Sam said her voice a bit too high. 

Lillian looked at her for a moment.

“I should hope so,” Lillian said in warning. 

Sam nodded quickly and determinedly looked at her plate despite Kara and Lena’s best efforts to catch her eye.

They didn’t have time to chat after breakfast as they had to head directly to the throne room. Things had been mostly set up but they still needed to be wired for the microphones and then there was the arrangement of chairs. As Lena was now officially involved her entourage had to be seated and there was some debate among the staff about who should sit where. It was eventually solved by moving the chairs three feet to the right so cameras would mostly capture Kara’s entourage but Lena’s people would be close enough for her to speak quietly with them.

Eventually everything was arranged to everyone's satisfaction and they were all allowed to sit down. Kara gave Astra a concerned look as she noticed her wince shifting on the throne.

“Are you okay Aunt Astra?”

Astra looked at her then laughed quietly.

“Yes little one. I’m just regretting being a showoff last night." Astra sighed settling herself more comfortably on her throne. "Rao I must be getting old.”

Kara and Lena exchanged another wide eyed look but then the doors opened and the first round of petitioners came in. 

The first round was the most numerous and all were fairly straight forwards. Kara knew this was by design. Get the quick ones out of the way early. They averaged three minutes each and all but three were granted at least in part. 

The last petition in the first round turned out to be a six year old called Lin-Tar who made a petition for his parents to increase his weekly allowance by ten dollars for a period of eight weeks. He did a three minute presentation of his proposal discussing all the increased chores he would do in exchange for the pay rise and presented his recent maths homework to show he would be able to manage his increased finances properly. It took Astra two minutes to work out what he really wanted was a specific action figure of the Hulk.

They all listened as the boy explained how incredible the toy was and all the different things it could do in an impassioned plea his father had clearly heard before based on silent pleas to Rao behind the boy’s back.

Astra looked at the boy’s father and smiled.

“Is there maybe a birthday coming up?” she asked.

The father sighed heavily and shook his head.

“His birthday was two months ago your majesty, when he got Hawkeye and Black Widow from his mother and I, and his grandfather bought him Iron Man. At this point he just needs the Hulk to complete the set.”

There was a wave of laughter from the crowd of petitioners. Lin-Tar dropped to his knees in front of Astra his eyes wide and pleading.

“Please your majesty. I’ll be really good and it’s two whole months to earn the toy. That’s ages!”

There was more laughter and Kara was giving Astra an equally pleading look clearly on the side of Lin-Tar. Astra sighed heavily.

“It’s always petitions like this that are the most difficult,” she said earning another laugh.

The boy looked up at her and Astra leaned forward.

“You’ve clearly done a lot of work and completing the set obviously means a lot to you. And I do want to reward your perserverence and determination. However if I agree to your plan I’m allowing child labour.”

There was another laugh from the other petitioners as well as a few aw's. Lin-Tar held out his hands in prayer as he continued to look at Astra.

“But I’m not alone up here so I’m just going to ask if one of the princesses has any ideas.”

She looked at Lena.

“I already know what she’s going to say, so what is your suggestion?”

Lin-Tar gave Lena a hopeful look as she smiled at him. Lena turned her tablet around to show Lin-Tar.

“Is this the one you want?” she asked.

Lin-Tar moved closer to look and then nodded quickly. Lena sighed. 

“So your majesty as queen you cannot be seen to approve child labour in any form, nor can you enter a contract with a child to give him a reward for certain behaviours. That would suggest you can be bought, or that your favour is in some way conditional. You also cannot simply gift it to him without also needing to reward every other child in Krypton.”

Lin-Tar turned his eyes on Lena adding a lip quiver. Lena turned to smile at Jack.

“Jack dear can I have your hat?”

Jack handed it over with a wide grin. Lena smiled at Lin-Tar as she started shuffling around her papers

“Your majesty can however inform him about a competition being done by a third party where the prize is an toy of the winners choice." There was a ripple of laughter as people started to realise what she was doing. "Like the very special one time competition that I will be holding very shortly.”

“But since Lin-Tar is too young to enter a competition of this nature…” She looked over at his father and got a small nod. “If Lin-Tar’s father entered the competition for the toy and won, then I could send the prize to him and he could give it to Lin-Tar as a special gift as a way to commemorate an important day. Such as perhaps, the coronation of a new Queen.”

Lin-Tar looked very excited as he nodded quickly his eyes on Lena she tore up some paper.

“To make sure it’s an actual competition there will need to be some other names in the hat of course.”

Lena turned to Sam.

“Perhaps you’d be interested in entering for Ruby.”

Sam smiled and Lena looked at Lin-Tar. 

“So I’ll just put this yellow piece of paper here and Lin-Tar if you could maybe take it to your father to put his name on for this competition and if Sam and any other parents or parental figures wanted to use this white paper to put their names in.”

Lin-Tar grinned at Lena and raced off with the post it note to his smiling father. Lena gave a scrap of white paper to Sam then Eliza, then Astra, and much to her surprise her own mother came forward and took one.

The papers were all added to Jack’s hat and to no one’s surprise Astra drew the yellow paper out and declared that Nak-Tar had won. Lin-Tar grinned at his father and quickly bowed to them all.

“Thank you!” he said excitedly. 

Astra smiled at him.

“You’re welcome Lin-Tar. If your father could leave his contact details with the administrators we’ll have someone make arrangements.”

Nak-Tar held out his hands and Lin-Tar ran back to his father. He returned a moment later with an apple as his offering to her table. Astra thanked him and the boy left with his father.

Lena held the hat out to Astra.

“Would you like to award second place?” she asked.

Astra smiled and withdrew another piece of paper.

“Eliza Danvers,” she read.

Lena looked at Alex.

“Which one do you want?” she asked handing the tablet over.

Alex laughed and looked at the options.

“Hawkeye, he can shoot things.”

As the first round of petitioners were being shown out they all took a moment to drink some water and swap over their papers. Kara caught Lena’s eyes and handed her a report. Lena gave her a confused look then she saw the odd highlighted patten.

Lena gave Kara a look but Kara was talking with Astra about the upcoming petitions. She turned the page slightly and saw the highlighted sections made a series of hearts. She smiled and quickly shuffled the papers before anyone else saw.

“You know I really like Captain America…”

Lena sighed as Kara gave her best puppy dog eyes.

“Fine,” she said shaking her head.

Sam leaned forward.

“Ruby really likes Black Widow.”

Lena sighed.

“Okay and-”

“Can I have Thor?” Jess asked.

Lena groaned dropping her head into her hand as everyone laughed. 

“I remind you that you are all adults with steady incomes and can buy your own action figures instead of needing to get me to.”

Sam nodded and leaned forwards to smile at Lena.

“But it’s so much more meaningful if you buy them for us,” she said.

“I don’t know why you’re looking at me. You opened yourself to this the moment you agreed to Kara.” Lillian looked at the tablet Alex was still holding then at Jack. “Get me Loki.”

There was a ripple of laughter from those listening as Lena threw up her hands and a smiling Jack wrote down everyone’s requests.

The second round petitions were far more complicated averaging a half hour each. Finally after nearly a whole day they were down to the last two. Astra seemed to want Kara and Lena to take the lead on these and alternated between them for their opinion all afternoon, disagreeing with them only once on a particularly complicated petition around industrial zoning.

A group of small business owners came about a graffiti problem in the alleyway between their shops. They requested financial assistance to put in place security cameras in the alleyways around their stores to catch those responsible. The cost of doing so was quite high considering the amount of area needing to be watched.

Kara suggested instead of putting in cameras and increasing policing they take advantage of a youth street art program. The throne would pay for some young street artists to put murals on the tagged alley walls. It would cost far less, and as people are much less likely to tag artwork it should significantly decrease the problem. For the cost of maintaining the cameras each year they could simply pay for new murals to be done to cover any tagging that had appeared. But if the problem persisted at the same level despite the murals then the throne would pay for the cameras to be installed. 

The shopkeepers quickly agreed and a box of various food stuffs from was presented as thanks. They all breathed a collective sigh as the last petitioner came forwards. 

It was a request to overturn a council decision to allow the digging of a well on his property. Astra didn’t even bother looking at it she just handed the plans to Lena. Lena pulled up the plans and after about five minutes of silent work folded the plans up and handed it back to Astra.

“The type of well he wants has a high chance of introducing bacteria to the water table, which I suspect is why he’s been rejected already. There are a few different types he could do instead that would remove the risk, but they will be significantly more expensive. Or he could move it over one kilometre. But then he wouldn’t be able to use the well to directly fill his dams.”

Astra nodded and considered the petitioner.

“I’m sorry but unfortunately the throne cannot grant your petition for the reasons stated by Princess Lena. I recommend you either move your planned well or change the style and resubmit to your council.”

The petitioner sighed and nodded. He seemed resigned. He picked up a cage with a chicken.

“I thank you for hearing me your majesty and offer this chicken for you-”

The man slipped as he took a step forwards and let out a curse as the cage opened and the chicken escaped flying directly at Kara. Mon-el leapt to his feet as the chicken took flight. Kara ducked and then went to chase it only to be prevented from leaving her seat by Astra's sudden grip on her shoulder. Mon-el along with several of the staff started to chase the chicken as it flapped around.

The petitioner apologised profusely and was immediately forgiven by Astra as she turned her attention to Kara.

“A Princess does not chase chickens,” Astra said with an amused smile. 

Kara blushed a light pink.

“Yeah Kara,” Lena said with a smirk.

Kara poked her tongue out and Lena returned the gesture. Astra sighed and looked toward Rao as the chicken passed in front of them chased by Mon-el and a couple of the staff.

“I thought you both agreed to act with a little more dignity,” Astra said.

Kara and Lena exchanged a look.

“Sorry your majesty,” they said giving her bright smiles.

“I’ll get you later,” Kara muttered looking back at Lena.

Lena grinned back and Astra cleared her throat. They exchanged a final look and Mon-el let a victory cry as he caught the chicken and carried it back to Astra.

“Your majesty,” he said smiling. 

Astra thanked him and another member of the staff quickly contained the chicken in it's cage again. Kara stepped down and gave him a kiss on the cheek for his efforts. Mon-el threw Lena a smug look. She raised an eyebrow in response and Astra quickly rose to officially end the petitions.

13. The coming wedding

Notes for the Chapter:

Apologies this one is a few days later than planned. I had to write most of this chapter this week so it took a bit longer than normal while I was juggling other projects.

TW: Mon-el being a manipulative dick.

DAY 27

“Yes Sweetheart?”

Alex looked at her mother for a long moment then at the others.

“Well, I was just remembering how when I was sixteen and I came out to you, you were really supportive and understanding, and you… Well you said you understood and were proud of me. When I asked if you thought anyone would have a problem with me being out you said if they did you and Astra would deal with them…”

Eliza’s smile widened a little more as she looked at Alex. She placed a hand on Alex’s shoulder giving her silent encouragement to continue.

Now if you’re all done speculating on my sleeping arrangements, we have a dress fitting in half an hour. I imagine Winn and Jess are already looking for the boys to go buy their suits, so I suggest you all get your things and head to the main entrance. I’ll go see what’s keeping Astra and Lillian.”

Eliza didn’t wait for a response instead heading off towards Astra’s office laughing quietly to herself. Alex turned to Kara then Lena.

He indicated the open lounge they were all in.

There was immediate agreement and they all dispersed at speed.

 

Lillian ran a critical eye over the dress as it was presented. Lena sighed waiting for the inevitable critique to begin. She silently counted down in her head.

“Yes, I’ll be out in a moment,” Lena said turning to do a final check. 

The dress was technically strapless but if anything did slip the lace bodice would probably hide it well enough for her to fix without anyone noticing. Lena turned to see Sam was already by the curtain waiting for her. Lena nodded and picked up the skirt regretting not insisting on getting the shoes first. She walked out and saw Kara already on a small dais in her dress. Kara turned to look at her and Lena felt her heart stop.

Kara’s dress was an off the shoulder design that hugged her figure closely. The pale yellow fabric wrapped around her in layers with some kind of white detail in some of the wraps. Her hair was swept up into a bun and her glasses missing. Someone had handed her a bouquet of pale pink flowers and a tiara had been added. 

Lena watched the moment Kara saw her. Her eyes widened and her lips parted on a soft exhale as she stared at Lena. Lena stared back. In that moment she would have given everything she had for Kara to be walking down the aisle towards her in that dress. She could see tears gathering in Kara’s eyes and felt a matching itch in her own.

“Hey no cat fights allowed here,” Alex said jumping up quickly to break into the moment. She flashed them both a too bright smile. “You’ll ruin the dresses.”

They both nodded turning away from each other and towards the mirrors quickly blinking back tears. Astra leapt up and started fussing over Kara’s dress as Anne and Lillian focused on Lena. Lena managed to keep a smile on her face as they had her stand on the dais and the dressmaker put a few pins in to make small alterations. Anne gushed about how pretty she was and Lillian gave her a shoulder a surprisingly comforting squeeze as she talked about maybe lifting the hem a centimetre.

“We don’t want it dragging behind you do we?” Lillian said looking at Lena.

Lena quickly nodded.

“Of course not mother.”

Lena took a deep breath and Sam handed her a glass of water. She drained it quickly. Then she held three different bouquets for comparison as Lillian, Anne and Sam continued to fuss over her. Anne genuinely, Sam and Lillian seeming more focused on distracting her. After ten minutes she was told by the dressmaker she could step down. She did so gratefully.

“Well I think I’ll get changed back,” Lena said determinedly not looking towards Kara. “I’d hate to ruin it so close to the big day.”

No one argued with her and Lena managed to retreat into the change room. She rested her head on the wall and took several deep breaths. She closed her eyes and all she could see was Kara in that dress and felt the ache in her heart worsen. 

“Do you need Sam to help you with the zip?” Lillian asked quietly from the other side of the curtain.

Lena nodded and sniffed then she remembered Lillian couldn’t see her.

“Yes, thank you. Sam would be great,” she said forcing her voice to sound normal.

She carefully stepped out of the dress trying not to disturb the pins as Sam ducked in. Lena draped the dress over a chair and was quickly wrapped in Sam’s familiar embrace. Sam held her tight enough to hurt and Lena sank into it. Sam let her go after a moment and picked up her shirt. Lena didn’t immediately move as she struggled for another minute to contain her emotions. She knew it was stupid to be upset considering the circumstances. She knew Kara would much rather be wearing it for her and one day she would. 

Lena took a deep breath and looked down to find Sam doing up her shirt buttons. She raised an eyebrow. Sam looked up and Lena smirked.

“Oh shut up,” Sam said smiling at her. “It’s a mum thing.”

Lena opened her mouth to say Lillian had never done up her buttons for her then some part of her said that wasn’t right. Lena frowned trying to remember if Lillian had ever helped her with her clothes. Maybe when she was very small and couldn’t do it very well herself. But she’d had her nanny for a while when she was younger and she’d been the person who did most of that.

“You okay there space cadet,” Sam teased.

Lena looked at Sam and laughed.

“Space cadet?”

Sam smiled shaking her head. Lena managed to put her pants on by herself then Sam was holding out her shoes.

“Shall we take the horses out and you can tell me all about the fitting?”

The moment they were on the trails and away from prying eyes Lena felt her body start to relax then the quiet started to work on her. The comfort of PC’s easy pace and Jack’s gentle presence letting her mind wander back and start to unpack it all. They reached a lookout and Jack tied up the horses as Lena walked over to a tree. She slowly sank to the ground and curled herself at the base.

“He doesn’t deserve her,” she finally whispered.

“No, he doesn’t.”

“I think Mon-el sent that picture,” he said.

Lena looked at him very seriously.

“Why?” she asked.

“I was thinking back over that night. Mxy was in front of me most of the time and when we returned to the main party Mon-el was the last one back. And well, it’s exactly the kind of thing he’d do.”

Lena nodded slowly thinking back over everything.

“He’s not going to believe any of the stories we tell him is he?”

Jack shook his head.

“He wants to be king.”

“Well he’s not going to be,” Lena said firmly. “I’m not letting that idiot sit on my throne.”

“I don’t think you have a choice once they’re married,” Jack said sympathetically.

Lena turned to Jack with a very smug smile.

“Consorts don’t get to sit on the throne until four years or a baby. Technically once Kara is Queen I can sit at her right as the next in line until four years of marriage or she gets pregnant.”

“Really?” 

Lena nodded.

“Mon-el will never sit on my throne.”

Jack laughed.

“He is really not going to like that,” Jack said smiling.

“What makes you say that?”

“Because he was looking forwards to it.”

Lena smiled.

“How mad do you think he’ll be at the coronation when I get to the one sitting next to her while he’s in the crowd?” Lena asked with a smile.

DAY 28

Kara listened as the chants rose but kept her head bowed feigning prayer. Her attention was not on the priest's rising chants but instead on Lena who was sitting beside her in the temple. Kara found her attention drifting to Lena over and over as the service continued. 

Lena’s hand was resting on the bench between them. Kara shifted her hand a little closer until they were just touching. Then she carefully rubbed her little finger over Lena’s. She saw Lena’s immediate smile and she returned the gesture.

14. A deal
