“You will have your chance Mon-el, just how you want it. A whole year without anyone intervening to try and win over Kara. In return you stop your little games now. No more pushing for extra time, no more demands for schedule changes or claiming you can’t go to something you can. You do what she asks when she asks. You will be the perfect partner for her through everything.”

Mon-el nodded slowly. Lena lowered the keys fixing Mon-el with a glare.

“But if you ever breathe a word about Kara and my relationship to anyone, I will destroy your life. I will ruin your reputation, tank your career, take your money, and your title. Everything you hold dear I will take from you, and then I will burn it to the ground in front of you. I don’t care what it costs me, and I don’t care how long it will take to achieve, I will dedicate my life to making yours a living nightmare. Do we understand each other?”

Mon-el was quiet for a long moment looking at Lena a bit taken aback by the ferocity of her threat.

“Why do you care about Kara’s happiness if you’re trying to steal the throne? I would think you’d be happy to hear you were screwing things up between me and Kara.”

Lena scoffed.

“You really are an idiot Mon-el,” she said. “You’ve been in the middle of everything almost from the very start and you still have no idea what’s actually going on.” He glared at her again. “Do I really need to spell it out for you? There is no competition. If she asked me to I would give up my claim in a heartbeat. I would give up my title, my family, my career. I would do anything for her. I would wait fifty years for one night if I had to. I’m in love with Kara.”

Mon-el actually looked surprised. Lena sighed shaking her head. No wonder Rhea was so disappointed in him, the man really was an idiot.

“So, do we have a deal?” Lena asked.

“You leave for a year and a day, no contact, and in return I keep my mouth shut about your past relationship and give Kara everything she wants from a husband.”

Lena nodded and held out a hand. He went to shake but she moved hers away fixing him with a look.

“You will be the perfect husband for her and do whatever she asks of you. You help her become queen and if she chooses to divorce you then you will never discuss anything about her and I for the rest of your life. In return I’ll give you the greatest racing car Krypton has ever known.”

He nodded and they shook hands.

“You do realise she is going to fall in love with me right,” he said not letting go and tightening his grip. “With you gone she will fall for me and when the time is up she won’t ever go back to you.”

Lena scoffed.

“You keep telling yourself that,” she said turning and walking away. 

Notes for the Chapter:

So Lena as usual is going to be all self sacrificing and leave to protect Kara. Alex wants to punch Mon-el and Astra is probably one step away from just disappearing Mon-el down a ravine.
How will Kara react to finding out about Lena's deal? How will Lillian when she finds out what Lena bargained with?

Let me know your thoughts and feelings about this chapter in the comments down below. Stay safe y'all.

Next time: Letters are read and Lena finds out some things she never expected.

15. Letters and stories

Notes for the Chapter:

Just letting y'all know I did write this chapter, and the one before it, while working on an angst fic so some of that intensity might have rubbed off.

Also for those who might not know, a foil is a type of fencing sword. It's long, thin, and... pointy. idk how else to describe it. It's a pointy sword that's often used as the first one people learn with in fencing. It's also probably the one you're picturing when you think of Olympic fencing.

TW:
Some discussion about Mon-el being controlling and the lengths that could go to.
Passing references to prior recreational drug use
Lex, Lena reads his letter and it's mentioned a few times by her and Lillian.

DAY 33

Alex was waiting for Kara in her suite that evening. Kara smiled at her sister and dropped the road and rail folder on the table with a satisfying thump.

“Three hours for everyone to argue over a few minor details and then just agree to what they already agreed. Sometimes I wonder why I want to be Queen.” 

Eliza laughed closing the door behind them. Kara turned and bent down to pat Streaky as she jumped onto the couch with a hopeful look.

“It wasn’t that bad,” Eliza said walking past Kara to sit on the opposite couch.

Kara sighed heavily and sank onto the couch beside Streaky.

“Okay the food was really good, and Mon-el has behaved himself all day today. A new record.”

Kara realised Alex had been silent since they arrived.

“Alex?”

Kara noticed Alex’s somber expression as she stood up a letter held tightly in her hand. 

“I have a letter for you from Lena.”

Kara perked up immediately then looked at Alex’s grim face again. Kara’s enthusiasm faded quickly.

“What’s it say?” she asked nervously.

“I don’t know,” Alex said holding it out. “After we talked last night I went to discuss the problem with her and see if she had any ideas on how to handle him. She was quiet for a very long time said Rhea, and then said she would fix it and to meet her again this evening. Apparently she made some kind of agreement with Mon-el. All she told me was she and Jack will leave right after their wedding as part of it and that it would all work out in the end.”

Kara jumped up from the couch and rushed forwards taking the letter from Alex.

“Lena please tell me you didn’t do what I think you did,” Kara whispered.

She tore the letter open and started reading.

Zhao,

We both know you never had a choice when he gave you that ultimatum so I hope you understand when I say when it came down to it I didn’t either. Your happiness and security is far more important to me than anything else. 

I am sure you will be upset about what I am going to tell you but I beg you, please don’t fight this. Our people need you. Your family need you. I need you. And you need Mon-el to behave himself for a year. I love you and that means I will always protect you, even when you don’t want me to. 

I have made a deal and I hope you will one day forgive me for it. I cannot bear for you to suffer a year as you have these last few days. So I offered Mon-el what he wanted, me gone. In return for his silence and his good behaviour I will have no contact with you or your entourage for a year. The year you need him for.

Once Jack and I marry we will leave on our honeymoon and not return to Krypton for a year and a day. I expect we will stay in England mostly. There’s some interesting nanotech work we can occupy ourselves with for that time. After that if you want me still I am yours. 

In return for his ongoing silence following your divorce I have offered him 45. I am certain the car will soothe his hurt ego and give you the freedom you deserve to choose the person you want to be with for the rest of your life. Even if that person isn’t me.

I’ll still be at your wedding as planned, it will now just be to see you one last time before I leave. As you walk down the aisle know that no matter who stands beside you at that altar your heart is your own, and mine is yours.

It was yours from the moment you tripped over me on that beach and will always be yours no matter what happens. Even if you spend the next fifty years with another I wish you only happiness. For me there will only ever be you. If you ever need me, for anything, just call. No matter why, no matter when, I’ll always pick up for you. 

I’ll miss you every day, more than I can ever express. 

All my love,

Lena.

Kara sank to her knees the letter clutched in her hands as the tears poured down her face. A heartbroken sob forced it’s way out of her chest and then she was wrapped in Alex’s comforting embrace as she broke down completely. Eliza was quickly on her other side whispering words of comfort as she sobbed into Alex’s shoulder.

“She’s leaving for a year,” Kara mumbled into Alex’s shoulder. “Because of him. To make him behave.”

Eliza rubbed her back as fresh sobs overcame her. Alex held her tighter. A door opened. 

“What happened?” Astra asked rushing forwards to hold Kara as Alex moved away with the letter.

Kara managed to slow her tears long enough to look up at her aunt. Astra looked at her.

“What is it little one?” she asked.

Kara tried to speak but all that came out were fresh sobs as she let Eliza and Astra hold her between them.

“Lena made a deal with Mon-el to ensure his good behaviour. In return she is leaving for a year and will have no contact with Kara in that time. After that she’ll give him 45 to ensure his continued silence,” Alex said.

Kara felt Astra stiffen and go deadly quiet for a moment. Kara looked up and saw the absolute fury on Astra’s face. Astra looked at Kara and the expression changed to one of utter heartbreak.

“I promise little one, whatever it takes, I will make sure you can marry your love. That boy will not get to stand between you and your happiness.”

Kara sniffed and nodded even though she didn’t believe it. They were stuck, they all knew it. They could threaten Mon-el, they could promise all kinds of retribution but when it came down to it they all knew there was too much at stake to drop him now.

 

Lena looked over at the picture of Lex. She’d sent Jack and Sam to the Jazz festival without her. She needed time alone, time to let herself finally do what she needed to. She picked up her glass of Scotch and drained it.

“It’s time,” she said quietly. 

Before she could think about it too much she picked up the picture frame and removed the letter smoothing out the page she started from the top. 

Dearest sister, 

I am sorry I cannot stay, but know there is nothing in this world that has made me prouder or happier than being your big brother. Ever since you came to us you have made my world so much brighter.

I wish I could see you take on the world next year, I know you’ll do great. If I can watch from the next life I’ll be there cheering you on. When you hear the crowd know I’m there cheering the loudest, as always.

I’m sorry I’ll never see you get your degree, or degrees knowing you. I’m sorry I won’t be there to drag you from your dorm on adventures like I said I would, but I’m sure you’ll make friends who will.

I’m sorry I won’t be there to watch you fall in love for the first time. It’ll probably be a disaster and you will inevitably have your heart broken.

But I promise one day you’re going to fall in love with someone and they’re going to fall in love right back and you’re going to get married because Astra -if rumour and mother’s drunken mutterings are anything to go by- will make it legal and if she doesn’t you will once you take the throne. 

I know you probably think you need to compromise on your love life to get the throne but don’t. Your good heart has always been one of my favourite things about you and paired with that brilliant mind you can’t go wrong if you follow them. 

One day you’ll meet someone and you’ll know. So please allow me one last wish, when it comes to love follow your heart. Because I know if you do you’ll find a beautiful princess to make a Queen beside you. I know she will be sweet and funny. She will get you out of your head and love you with a strength and fire to rival Rao. You deserve nothing less little sister.

When you marry I know you’ll be the second most beautiful bride in all of Krypton, the first being your bride. I’m sorry I won’t be there to tell you in person how beautiful you look in your dress, or to walk you down the aisle. I’m sorry I’ll never hold my nieces and nephews but tell them I love them anyway. 

If I could stay I would but I can’t. I’m too tired, it all hurts too much, and I’ve held you back long enough. I’m sorry. I’m sorry. I’m sorry. You are going to be the greatest Queen Krypton will ever know. 

Even if you’re not though. Even if you spend the rest of your life doing absolutely nothing I will still love you, and I will still be proud of you no matter what. I simply wish for you to be happy. I will truly be at peace when you are dear sister.

So please do what I never could, follow your heart and be happy. 

Lex.

Lena barely made it to the floor before she was sobbing uncontrollably the pain tearing through her as she thought of his last words. His last wish for her. She was drowning in it.

Her throat felt raw and her heart felt like it had been ripped from her chest as she cried for her brother. As she cried for everything that would never be. She cried for him, and for her, and for Kara.

She had no idea how long it had been when a pair of warm hands touched her back and Lena turned immediately into the contact burying her face in a white cashmere sweater. 

“Hush now baby,” Lillian whispered her fingers gently combing through Lena’s hair.

Lena felt a moment of confusion but then she was sobbing harder as Lillian held her. She let her gently pry the crumpled page from her hands then wrap her tighter in her arms. Lena couldn’t stop the tears as Lillian held her the smell of her perfume so familiar and so foreign at the same time. Then Lillian started to sing. 

It was an old lullaby, one that Lex had sung to her more than once to chase away her nightmares. Lena’s tears slowed as the song continued and she sniffed letting Lillian comfort her. As the song ended Lillian continued to stroke her hair holding her gently as the tears finally stopped and she looked up at her mother.

Lillian looked down at her for a moment then kissed Lena on the head and slowly got up. She collected the note and put it back in the picture frame standing it up again. 

For a moment Lillian stood there looking at the picture then she turned and held her hands out to Lena.

Lena was too wrecked to do anything more than let Lillian pull her to her feet and lead her to the bedroom. Lena followed easily as Lillian guided her to the bed tucking her under the thick blanket and sat beside her. 

For a minute nether of them spoke. Lena was waiting for some kind of explanation and Lillian seemed to be deciding whether to give her one.

“He left me a note too,” Lillian said at last. “He said he couldn’t take it anymore and he just wanted to sleep forever.” She turned to Lena with a deep sigh. “He said that you were the real royal heir and asked me to help you achieve your dreams.”

Lillian fell silent again looking at Lena. Lena opened her mouth to speak but Lillian looked away.

“You should sleep.”

She moved to leave but Lena grabbed her wrist stopping her.

“Yes?” Lillian asked turning back to her.

“I’ve got to ask,” Lena whispered. “The marriage clause in the princess articles, why didn’t you want me to see it?”

Lillian sighed and seemed to age ten years as she looked at Lena.

“Because I knew if you saw it you’d marry someone just to be queen then be foolish enough to stay with them out of some sense of duty.”

For a moment Lena had trouble understanding. Her confusion broke through her emotional fog and she looked at Lillian bewildered. Lillian sighed again.

“I never wanted you to marry for duty, money, or power. I- I didn’t want you to make the same mistake I did.”

Lena sat up straighter looking at Lillian. She’d known her marriage to Lionel wasn’t happy exactly but at the same time-

“If you’d known about the clause you’d have gone out and found some man of suitable qualifications and aesthetics, married him, and spend the rest of your life in a loveless marriage because it was convenient. I’m sure you would have had some kind of agreement but I also knew you’d never let yourself find someone to truly love because you’d feel a sense of duty to stay in your marriage. You’d pick duty over love.”

Lena looked at Lillian vaguely offended.

“I wouldn’t have-”

“Why did you break up with Kara in Australia?” Lillian said cutting her off. “You clearly had strong feelings for each other and you were clearly missing her from the moment you left. So, why didn’t you tell her who you were and see if she was willing to have a longer relationship with you?”

“Because-” Lena looked away from Lillian’s far too knowing expression. “Because I had a duty to become queen, and despite my strong feelings for Kara pursuing a relationship with her would have made that difficult,” she admitted.

Lillian nodded and gave Lena a long undecipherable look. She raised a hand and stroked her fingers down Lena’s face in an unusually tender gesture. Lillian pulled her hand back not looking away from Lena.

“I was a terrible mother to you, and to Lex if we’re being honest. Always far too wrapped up in my fights with Lionel to give either of you the time and attention you really needed, especially in the ways you needed. But that didn’t mean I didn’t care. I did always want you both to be… to have what I never did.”

She looked away from Lena to stare at the picture on the nearby shelf of Lena at her graduation with Jack, Sam, and a very small Ruby. Ruby was on Jack’s shoulders wearing Sam’s graduation cap. Lena and Sam stood on either side of Jack holding up their degrees. All four of them were smiling and sticking their tongues out at the camera.

“Do you remember when you decided you wanted to try fencing so you could play with the swords on the wall?”

“No,” she admitted with a small smile.

Lillian sighed not looking away from the picture.

“Well you did. So I had my assistant find you a fencing school, got you a starter lesson and all the gear and then on the day of it Ernie was sick, and there was no one to take you. I came downstairs to find you sitting by the door with your bag all packed engaging in a rather melodramatic fit of despair, especially considering it could just be rescheduled. But faced with your tears of disappointment at the delay I cancelled my meetings and took you myself. I even buttoned you into that ridiculous fencing jacket Denise bought you because you couldn’t press the buttons together properly.” 

Lena smiled. Every picture of her first year of fencing had her in a bright pink fencing jacket. When she finally outgrew it Lillian had immediately replaced it with a traditional white one.

“I sat there for two hours watching you fail to keep the tip of your little training foil up as the coach tried to get you to move your feet and stab at the same time. You were a danger to yourself and everyone around you. You nearly took out the coach’s eye, fell over eight times, and I had to make you stop after you somehow hit yourself in the face.”

Lena laughed softly. Lillian looked at her and gave her a small smile.

“And you didn’t shut up about how much fun you had for three hours after you were done. Even as you sat in the car holding an ice pack half the size of your head to your eye as it swelled up. So I called the school and got you a whole year of lessons… and asked that they always make you wear a helmet. As you got older I paid for every one of your competitions, made sure you had the best coaches, and even made Lionel go to your junior championship because Lex was away and I knew how much it meant to you to have someone there.”

“Why didn’t you come then?” Lena asked quietly. 

Lillian looked profoundly uncomfortable for a minute. Lena gave her a look complete with eyebrow raise. Lillian looked away with gritted teeth seemingly struggling with something.

“I never really wanted to be a mother. But it was expected and so I became one. I- I did love Lex from the moment I held him.”

Lillian sighed again and looked over at Lena.

“It took a little longer to for me to admit I loved you, even to myself. But after you read that girl’s diary in the fifth grade,” Lillian smiled softly at Lena. “That was when I really knew you were my daughter.”

Lena scoffed.

“Because I did something petty and spiteful?”

“No, because you did something that looked petty and spiteful but was actually to punish your father.”

Lena looked at Lillian impressed.

“How’d you know?” she asked.

Lillian gave her a small smile

“Because when he came home that night he was furious that he’d lost a major deal over some petty schoolgirl drama. Then he pointed a finger at me and said it was my fault you were turning into a bitch.”

Lena laughed.

“What did you say?”

“I said if he wanted to do better I’d gladly get out of his way, and then I went to spend the weekend with Cat.”

Lena’s eyes widened as she remembered Sam’s comment about Cat’s hand on Lillian’s leg.

“With Cat? Wait you and-”

Lillian got a very pained expression. 

“The point of that story,” she said gritting her teeth. “Is that I did and I do care about you, and your happiness. I just… It’s just taken me a long time to realise that happiness for you, and for Lex, was not going to come from- I had previously thought that providing you both with the kind of challenge your intellect warranted would bring with it happiness.”

She looked away.

“I learnt I was wrong. I learnt that the hard way when Lex… died.”

Lillian’s voice cracked slightly.

“All that ever made him feel was inadequate and he would probably have been far happier as a car mechanic. But Lionel and I… we pushed him to always aim for more than a simple life. We pushed him to be a king not a man. If I had been paying attention instead of holding a grudge I would have- would have realised that lesson earlier. I could have- I would have told him I loved him no matter what he did with his life.”

Lillian was silent for a long moment and Lena found herself looking over at the picture of Lex and Ignatius sitting on her dresser. 

“As for you well…” Lillian looked at Lena again “You decided to give up your sporting career and pursue the throne and you said you wanted it. So I decided to help you get it. If I’d known you wanted something else-” 

“I do want the throne,” Lena said quickly.

Lillian seemed to struggle for a moment before she waved a hand.

“Well there you go. In a year she’ll divorce that… boy then you’ll get to marry her and be Queen. Until then you can just have a secret affair with her and-”

“I’m leaving,” Lena said quietly.

Lillian looked at her confused. Lena shrank into herself as she contemplated the long year ahead.

“Explain,” Lillian said.

“Mon-el’s recent behaviour it-” 

Lena grit her teeth and forced down the rush of anger that filled her just thinking about it. 

“It was causing serious problems for Kara, and everyone around her really. Even if he was threatened or bribed unless he thought he had a chance to win over Kara and become King he was going to make everything very difficult for everyone to the best of his ability in an effort to get what he wanted.” 

“Astra and I will find a way to deal with him,” Lillian said with a sigh. 

Lena shook her head.

“It’s not him that we need to worry about. It would be only a matter of time before Rhea found out the truth and once she did we’d all end up in tight spot. her connections with the church and parliament make her a serious threat and the privacy laws can only do so much in this case.”

Lillian pressed a hand to her face and sighed heavily.

“I hate that woman.”

“We all do. But unlike Mon-el Rhea isn’t an idiot and she could use this to effectively kill the monarchy in Krypton if she doesn’t get what she wants, and she wants House Daxam to inherit the throne. She knows we’d never allow her to do that to Krypton so Kara would have to have Mon-el’s child guaranteeing a House Daxam monarch and through Mon-el as the father Rhea would get access to the highest place of power in Krypton.”

Lillian sucked in a breath. Her face twitched as her lip curled.

“Or we could just kill them both,” Lillian said.

“Because that wouldn’t be at all suspicious,” Lena said sarcastically.

Lillian gave her a look.

“A car accident isn’t suspicious in a race, then Rhea can have a nice simple heart attack. Grief over losing her son.”

Lena gave her a look.

“Well unlike you I had an idea that didn’t involve murder. What we really need from Mon-el is time. Preferably we need a year, everyone knows it’s an arranged marriage that’s happening because of the princess articles and a year, no matter what he says afterwards, shows there was an effort to make it work. But to get that means Kara either has to let him walk all over her in their relationship the whole time which will slowly kill her, we had to agree to some kind of ridiculous bribe that will be seen as an obvious payoff for something, even once the truth is obvious, or… Or we let him think he can win still.”

Lena sat up a little straighter and looked at her mother. Lillian gave her a small nod to continue.

“He saw me as the main obstacle to his winning over Kara. I leave on his terms and he’ll play nice for a few months at least. At the point when he realises Kara isn’t going to ever love him back there will have been enough time to show that Kara had been trying to make their arrangement amicable. I stepped aside and had zero contact so he can’t claim she’s broken her vows, and I have no doubt by that point Astra will have enough evidence of him being a terrible person to either blackmail him into silence or bury anyone who tries to argue her divorcing him was just because she had the throne by then.”

“How did you get him to agree to keep quiet?” Lillian asked.

Lena looked at her. Lillian raised an eyebrow.

“I taught you how to make deals. You would have offered him something in return for his ongoing silence. So what did you offer him?”

Lena sighed heavily and rubbed her face.

“I offered him 45.” Lillian’s eyes widened in horror. “I know. But if it buys his silence then it’s worth it. If giving him Lex’s car gets her peace for a year, even if I can’t contact her at all, then I’ll gladly hand it over. I love her and there’s not a line in the universe I wouldn’t cross to protect her.”

Lillian was quiet for a long minute her eyes on some distant memory.

“Sometimes we have to give up something we love for someone we think is more important. I suppose at least you’re picking the right person to give up something for.”

Lena looked at her surprised. 

“Who did you give up?” Lena asked quietly.

There was a long silence between them as Lillian seemed to be deciding whether to answer. Then finally she sighed heavily.

“You’ll probably find out at some point anyway.”

Lillian shifted on the bed to be looking at Lena. She sighed again and looked at the picture of Lex and Ignatius again.

“I didn't exactly give up a person, not like you are I... There is a reason I have never liked the church. My parents, who you thankfully never had the displeasure of meeting, were very much of a traditional mindset following the church teachings. They were not especially loving or supportive people, even to each other much of the time. They believed in following the word of the church and all rules given. I of course was not one to abide by anyone else’s rules very well so as a teenager I rebelled.”

Lena looked at her mother remaining silent. She couldn’t really imagine Lillian as a rebellious teenager. Then again until a week ago she couldn’t really imagine her doing up her shirt buttons either and that had clearly been wrong.

“I had two friends at school, Astra, and Cat. Astra was… she was also rebellious but her family was more tolerant of her behaviours, them being of the let her get it out of her system now school of thought. Cat was at our school on a scholarship and had a bit of a chip on her shoulder as a result. We were all brilliant, angry, and… less bound by certain moral ideas than our peers. So of course the three of us were outcasts and troublemakers. We weren’t foolish enough to do anything that might lose Cat her scholarship or get Astra or I in serious enough trouble that our families would really crack down, but once we turned sixteen we found a particularly enjoyable way to rebel. We… experimented a lot.”

Lena gave Lillian a wide eyed look and slowly tapped her nose in a silent question. Lillian sighed rolling her eyes a little.

“Yes we did that too. Cat makes excellent brownies and Astra is rather good at identifying certain mushroom species.”

“Wow,” Lena said looking at Lillian in a whole new light.

“But that was only a few times a year when we could be sure our parents or teachers wouldn’t find out. We went camping as often as we could get away with for that. Most of our experimentation was of a more…”

Lena watched Lillian struggle for a moment. She cleared her throat and Lena felt herself smiling at her unusual level of awkwardness.

“Well we all knew each other’s bodies rather well. Astra and I had a bit of a… Well we were close. Cat was our best friend but she’s always liked to go off and do her own thing at points before coming back. But between Astra and I there was… an intensity that we rarely acknowledged.”

“You were in love,” Lena suggested quietly.

“We were seventeen and stupid,” Lillian said. Lena tried not to smile at her exasperated tone. “We both kept denying our… feelings and we would have fights and falling outs over the most meaningless of things and both go date some random boy for a few weeks. Eventually we’d get over it, one of us would apologise or we’d just meet up to fight again and we’d fall back together. Sometimes while still dating the boys. That was until I met one particular boy. He was introduced to me at my seventeenth birthday party. A party my parents flatly refused to allow Astra or Cat anywhere near. Astra and I were fighting over something at the time. I think she’d decided she liked some girl in our year and wanted to invite her to join us sometime and I’d called the girl a cow.”

Lena scoffed. Lillian’s lips twitched. 

“So to annoy her I started dating this boy my parents introduced me to and well… As I said I was young and stupid, and I decided that I was in love with him. I know now I wasn’t. I just liked the idea of him. I liked what my family and others thought of me having him. So I threw myself into the relationship. I pushed aside the people I did love and who loved me because for the first time in perhaps my whole life my parents were actually approving of my life choices. I was suddenly popular, my parents were treating me well, I had the approval of dozens of people who welcomed me with open arms because of him. Astra being Astra didn’t like this and so… well you know what she did. I refused to forgive her, but I forgave him.”

Lillian sighed heavily looking much older for a moment.

“I married him a little over two years later.”

Lena went very still as she looked at her mother.

“I never really forgave Astra. In context it was a small thing and not even the first time she slept with someone I had been seeing. We were never exclusive, but she refused to tell me why she did it. She kept insisting it was just for fun, to see what I found so special. I knew that was a lie. I thought she was just jealous because I was suddenly popular and she was still the weird girl who punched boys in sport. So I refused to accept her apology and she got mad and I got furious and it all escalated from there. We fought for nearby four decades, only seeing each other whenever Lionel managed to sufficiently piss me off enough that I’d go to her to spite him. He always thought it was Non that I would see but… well Non wasn’t inclined to care about Astra’s guests so long as they couldn’t get her pregnant.”

Lillian looked at Lena and gave her a smile.

“When he brought you home I spent a whole month with her. We spent two weeks pretending to be working when really we went shopping all over Europe.”

Lena laughed softly. Lillian’s smile slowly faded as her eyes returned to the picture of Lex and Ignatius.

“I called her after Lex died. I told her I needed her and to come over, and she did. I cried in her arms all night then threw her out at dawn... with some regrettable words. We hadn’t really spoken after that, not until I gave your challenge for the throne.”

“Has anything changed since then?” Lena asked quietly.

Lillian gave her a look. 

“You mean are we screwing again?”

Lena made a choking noise as a blush rushed up her face. Lillian smirked.

“Yes, we started again at that supper. Things had been more amicable between us and with Cat there to smooth our more antagonistic streaks it felt rather like high school. And Eliza is remarkably-”

“Okay!” Lena said quickly holding out her hands in an effort to block the images that had just permanently scarred her psyche. “Don’t tell me anything else.”

Lillian gave her a smile.

“Well you did ask,” she said.

“And then I immediately regretted it,” Lena said with a shudder.

Lillian laughed at Lena’s scandalised expression  then she sighed and looked at the clock.

“Are you able to go to sleep now or do I need to distract you further. I can always tell you about this first time we-”

“I’m good! Very tired. Thank you,” Lena said quickly faking a yawn.

Lillian smiled and leant forward  to place a kiss on Lena’s head.

“Goodnight Inah,” she said softly.

“Goodnight Jeju.”

Lillian smiled at her one last time and left turning out the light. Lena sighed and rolled over curling herself around a pillow. She looked at her bedside table and saw a picture of her and Lex sitting in 45 the day he brought it home. Lex smiled out of the picture at her and Lena found herself smiling back. Then she sighed as the sadness crept over her again.

“I promise I’m following my heart as best I can. It just might take a little longer than I would like to marry her.”

Notes for the Chapter:

I can't seem to stop redeeming Lillian just a little bit. I'll have to make her truly evil in something else.
Okay so next we have the wedding and boy is it going to be... Well why don't y'all just put your theories about what's going to happn in the comments along with any part of this chapter that gave you feels. Stay safe y'all.

16. The Wedding

Notes for the Chapter:

Apologies for the delay with this one. Yesterday my brain decided I should spend all my time writing a plot outline for a possible next project which ended up being 15,000 words long so...

But here you go the moment you've all been waiting for, The Wedding. Which is coincidentally I think my favourite part of this whole story. Enjoy.

DAY 35

Sam came to wake her at seven but Lena was already awake and sitting up in bed her eyes on the window. She hadn’t really slept. Lena just turned her head ad looked at her. Sam sighed and just held out a cup of coffee to her.

“Come on sweetheart, we gotta get ready.”

Lena nodded and sat up. She drank some coffee though it didn’t seem to help. She showered, dressed, and ate her breakfast as instructed. Lillian joined her. After a few minutes of silence she sighed and slid a small jewellery bag towards her. Lena looked at her confused.

“In case Mon-el forgets. I don’t trust your beard to take care of these.”

Jack opened his mouth to utter a protest but Sam scoffed and even Lena smiled for a moment. 

“Why is Lena carrying-”

“They can only be held by someone engaged, or the best man,” Sam said.

Jack gave her a look.

“Same reason for your mother always wearing at least four rings to a wedding,” Lena said. “Someone has to bring spares. By Kryptonian tradition an unmarried person must bring a spare set so that should the groom drown and the bracelets not arrive the bride can still marry the best man. Technically Lord should have a set but he's the second best man and he's already married so he won't. But it's also not uncommon for the actual best man to forget so there’s always at least one engaged pair with bracelets at every wedding as a backup.”

Jack nodded. Lena opened the bag and looked at the twin silver bracelets. They had a matching trio of blue and red stones. Lena tucked them away again. The red reminded her of Kara. 

“Lena?” Jack said gently.

“I’m going to go see PC,” Lena said getting up.

She tucked the bracelets in her pants pocket and left passing Jess on her way. Jess seeing her face didn’t follow.

At the stables she quickly made her way to PC's stall. Sensing her mood PC approached slowly and gave her a gentle nudge. Lena reached up and stroked his face. The grooms had clearly given him a bath yesterday. He was freshly brushed and shining. She smiled seeing him so clearly well cared for. Red and yellow ribbons were tied in his mane and his hooves were freshly blackened.

Alex having heard about PC as a children’s horse had quietly come to Lena two weeks ago and asked if she could ride him in the wedding procession. Lena had readily agreed and she’d seen the relief on Alex’s face. Seeing Alex’s nerves when doing a practice ride Lena had understood why. 

Alex apparently had not gone through a horse phase and was a poor rider. She was also terrified of falling off. Reportedly because she was actually thrown off by all but the most placid of horses. Lena couldn't really blame them. PC sensing her nerves didn’t react to the death grip of Alex’s thighs and was as sweet as pie for her as she slowly relaxed in the saddle. Kara had joked PC must think Alex was just a very big child and was trying to reassure her by being extra slow as he walked around the field. Alex had immediately raised her middle finger at Kara and said he was just a horse with manners unlike Britney. Which had of course prompted a round of bickering between her and Kara.

Lena smiled at the memory breathing in the familiar smell of clean horse and fresh hay. PC gave her another gentle nudge prompting her to pat him again.

“At least I’ve still got my sweet boy,” she whispered. “Or should I say handsome boy today huh? Yes you are. You’re looking very handsome in your ribbons.”

PC shuffled closer blowing a breath on her. She smiled and returned the gesture.

“I love you too. I’m guessing you've liked all your pampering. Well I suppose one of us should be happy today.”

There was the sound of someone kicking something over followed by a muffled curse. Lena turned around to see an embarrassed looking Alex in her ceremonial white uniform.

“Sorry, I just came to collect PC but I can use another horse I’m sure.”

“It’s okay,” Lena said giving Alex a sad smile. “We both know you’ll fall off any other horse.”

Alex sighed and nodded. She seemed to make a decision and opened the gate coming into the stall. 

“Yeah, I will,” she said reaching out to pat him gently. 

For a minute they both stood there giving PC a little affection. 

“Are you actually going to keep your promise to Mon-el?” Alex asked.

“Yes,” Lena said with a sigh. “If I don’t he’ll claim she was cheating on him and Rhea will use it to kick Kara off the throne unless she agrees to have Mon-el’s baby.”

“Or I kill him,” Alex muttered.

Lena sighed.

“You and my mother are more alike then you think.”

Alex smiled.

“According to Mum I’m like Astra.” Alex gave Lena a look and Lena returned it. “How close were-”

“In love as teenagers,” Lena said. “They fought for decades because Astra wouldn’t admit she slept with my father because she was in love with Jeju and wanted her to leave him and come back to her.”

Alex sighed.

“Well I’m glad you chose to tell her your feelings instead of sleeping with Mon-el and fighting with her for decades,” Alex said. “Instead you’ll only be apart for a year and Kara has vowed never to forgive him. I suppose that counts as progress.”

Lena laughed quietly. Alex smiled at her and Lena felt a second ache in her stomach.

“I’m going to miss you.”

Alex gave her a smile.

“I’m going to miss you too.” 

Alex took a step forwards and wrapped her arms around Lena holding her tight. Lena sank into the hug and for a moment she felt something familiar and old she hadn’t experienced in a long time.

“Alex,” she whispered.

Alex pulled back and gave her a look.

“Can you do me a favour?” Alex nodded quickly. “I know this is going to sound weird but can you tell me I look beautiful today.”

Alex gave her a look of understanding that eased some of that old pain. She wrapped Lena in her arms again holding her tightly.

“You look beautiful today. Second only to Kara because she is the bride and no one is allowed to outshine her today.”

Lena choked out a laugh as tears filled her eyes and she clung to Alex for a minute. Alex didn’t seem to mind. She just held her until PC decided that was enough and started nudging Lena’s pockets searching for treats. Lena broke away from Alex to turn with watery eyes to PC giving him a kiss.

“Be good for Alex okay. And not like you were for Kara. Can you do that for me?”

PC huffed and sniffed at her pockets. Alex took a step closer pulling a sugar cube out of her pocket and offering it to PC.

“Hey buddy I hear if I give you one of these you’ll love me forever.”

PC happily munched the cube and gave Alex a small nudge with his head. Alex smiled and clipped a lead to his halter.

“Hey Alex,” Lena said.

Alex immediately stopped and looked at her.

“Can you uh, don’t tie him tightly. He’ll stay put I just- in case I can’t-”

Words failed her but Alex understood. She reached out and put a hand on Lena's shoulder.

“I’ll wrap it around the post but not tie it. If you need to run away with him go for it. The guard will stop him escaping properly and I’m sure I can go in the carriage with Astra and Mum if you do.”

Lena gave her a grateful smile and then gave PC a final pat before letting Alex lead him out to be saddled by the grooms. Lena took a final breath and wiping away the last traces of her tears she headed back to her suite. 

Jack was standing by the windows half dressed and letting Anne do his tie saying something about how he was useless at it. Lena felt a small smile tug at her lips seeing Jack roll his eyes at his mother’s fussing. Lillian was politely keeping to the opposite side of the room from her and talking to Jess. Sam appeared and guided Lena back to her room. Lena let Sam help her change shirts to a pale yellow one and after a moment’s consideration Sam added a black waistcoat.

“No point wearing a jacket today. If you get cold steal Jack’s,” she said carefully rolling up Lena’s sleeves making sure they were even.

Lena gave her an absent nod. Sam sighed and turned digging through Lena’s jewellery box taking out her mother’s ribbon. Lena looked at it then Sam. Sam gave her a gentle smile and carefully tied it around her neck. 

“Today is a day you’re going to need a little extra luck.”

Lena didn’t argue. The hairdresser arrived followed by a makeup artist. Apart from one insistence by Sam for waterproof makeup for Lena little else was said to her. An hour later they were all moving and Lena fund herself protectively between Jack and Sam as they headed to the car and got in. 

It was a short journey. Sam got out first followed by Jess. Lillian gave Jack a look and he got out with his mother. Lillian gave Lena a look.

“Head up, you’re a Luthor.”

Lena nodded and Lillian got out holding out her hand to Lena. Lena took it and emerged onto the temple steps. The door was closed and the car left. Lena looked around taking in the crowd of thousands behind the temporary barriers. Cameras were everywhere and she could see reporters from all around the world standing on platforms erected for them to be able to see over the crowd. 

She could feel hundreds of eyes on her as Lillian linked her arm with hers and they headed in heads held high. Jess came to stand on her other side in silent support. Lena looked up as they entered his eyes on the crystal high above as they were directed up to the front taking seats in the row just behind where Kara’s family would sit. 

The temple around them was about two thirds full with nobles and significant figures from around Krypton and the world. Cat Grant arrived within minutes of them. She sat at the end of the aisle with her son Carter, both dressed neatly and Cat wearing a large and impressive yellow hat. She had her phone clutched tightly in her hands seeming impatient. 

Lena sat waiting. She listened to everyone around them and when the bells rang out once she looked up to watch Mon-el come in from a side room in his white suit and yellow tie. A large yellow rose sat in his buttonhole. Lena didn’t look at him as he seated his mother in her place with the grooms men. Instead she turned her attention to the priests positioned around the room at intervals. Kal was by the nearest pillar in his robes a smile on his face.

He saw her looking and she saw a flicker of confusion as he saw her sad expression. He gave her a gentle smile and looked deliberately to the crystal then back at her opening his arms in the sign for prayer and touched his heart looking at her. She nodded slowly the message clear. Look to Rao and pray, he will grant you your hearts desire.

Lena knew Rao could not grant hers but still she tried closing her eyes and tilting her head upwards her arms open by her side as she prayed silently. She prayed for Kara. And in a way her prayer was answered as music started and they all turned rising to their feet as the doors of the temple were opened and the wedding proper begun. 

A ceremonial guard of seven came first led by Brainy looking magnificent in his full uniform his many ribbons and medals in place. Behind the guard came Nia and Winn in black and red carrying the banner of the house of El between them. Then Alex in her full white academic uniform a gold band around her arm and the crest of the house of El in red standing out on her chest. Eliza was behind her in a white dress with a black and white cloak in her arms, a ceremonial sword around her waist. 

The guard, the house, the family, the parents, and then the bride.

Kara walked slowly on Astra’s arm. Astra was in a red gown her crown in place and a yellow cloak over her shoulder but Lena wasn’t looking at her. From the moment she appeared all Lena could see was Kara. She looked even more beautiful than Lena had anticipated. Lena felt her heart start to bleed inside her as she watched Kara walking towards her down the aisle. 

Kara looked up and Lena watched her search the crowd until she saw her. Lena took a step closer. Jess subtly grabbed her by the back of her waistcoat holding her from going out further but letting her stand closest to the aisle. 

As Kara got closer Lena could see the tears slowly streaming down her face. She could see Kara trying to smile but then their eyes met and Lena saw the quiver of her bottom lip.

She got closer and closer and Lena desperately wanted to pull free and race out. She wanted to take Kara’s hand in hers and run away. Run away from all of this and bring a smile back to her face. But she could hear the priests chanting and the whispers of the crowd around them both loud and quiet as Kara walked closer. Lena couldn’t resist one last time. She knew the commentators would go wild with speculation but she was about to give her up for what could be a very long time.

She reached out just a little and Kara did too. The very tips of their fingers brushed and their eyes met before Astra gently guided Kara onwards and Kara was forced to turn her head and look at Mon-el. Jess placed a comforting hand on Lena’s shoulder keeping her in place.

Lena followed Kara with her eyes. She watched her walk those last few metres up to the alter where a beaming Mon-el stood beside Father Zod in his full high priest regalia. The music stopped and Astra in a break from tradition did not give Kara’s hand to Mon-el. She instead positioned her in front of him and gave a final hug before going to her seat in the front row beside Alex.

They all sat. Lena being gently tugged down by Jess as her eyes stayed stuck on Kara. She heard Mon-el’s quiet murmur for Kara to smile but couldn’t hear the response because Father Zod started speaking.

He welcomed them all for coming and started on a long story about the origin of the ceremony and the significance of such undertakings. Lena found herself unable to focus on the words as she looked at Kara. 

One year. Just one year and she would be with her again. She would wait. She would do anything to have Kara again, to marry her one day.

A single ray of light played across Kara's hair. Lena blinked in surprise as the light played on Kara’s neck. A ray from the crystal must be out of place. Her momentary distraction meant the next few words of Father Zod’s speech slipped through.

“And so we stand on this holy ground to bind these two in the strongest of ties.”

Lena’s mind started to work as Father Zod continued speaking about the strong binding ties of the ceremony and then it hit her with the force of a truck.

Lena sucked in a sudden breath her eyes wide as she remembered.

“…the ceremony on the steps is technically an elopement binding… That meant that even should the families or church still disagree and seek to annul their marriage any children of their union would technically be legitimate… It’s actually how the house of El started.”

Lena turned suddenly to Jess and grabbed her pulling her close. Jess looked at her alarmed. Lena started whispering in her ear and she saw Jess’ eyes widen as her jaw dropped. Lena looked at her and Jess held up a finger quickly digging out her phone. 

Jack and Lillian looked at them Lillian widening her eyes in silent censure but Lena shook her head at them not wanting to draw too much attention. Not that she really needed to worry as it was then that Cat started whispering loudly on her phone drawing all the attention around them. 

“Are you sure?” Cat hissed.

Several priests were trying to get their attention to signal them to put their phones away, and one camera had noticed and turned to look at them. Lena had no doubt some commentator was going to have fun talking about the Luthors at the wedding being on their phones. Alex glanced over and Lena gave her a subtle head shake and she turned back immediately.

Lena looked at Jess as she reread and double checked then her eyes lit up as she nodded with a bright smile. Lena felt the weight of everything lift off her as she turned and prepared to cause a scandal when someone beat her to it. 

“Stop the wedding!” 

Everyone turned shocked at Cat Grant who had stood up interrupting the service her phone pressed to her ear.

“Imra Ardeen,” Cat said loudly a look of vicious glee on her face as she looked at Mon-el.

Suddenly Mon-el looked very pale. Cat was clearly listening to someone speaking on the other end of the phone. Mon-el squared his shoulders a little as he looked at Cat.

“What about-”

Cat held up a hand and turned to look at Astra who had also risen hopeful eyes on Cat waiting for confirmation.

“She’s his wife.”

There was a moment of stunned silence as they all looked at the Mon-el.

“Wait so he’s already married?” Alex asked standing up too.

“No,” Rhea said jumping up quickly. “He was but they’re divorced. You can proceed.”

She motioned quickly to Father Zod who seemed to be thinking about it. Before Rhea could add anything else Cat interrupted her.

“No they’re not. They got married five months ago after a spontaneous getaway in Las Vegas. Then three weeks into their marriage she returned early from a work trip and caught him screwing her best friend in the shower. She filed for a quick divorce but he reportedly wanted half her assets. He’d been dragging it out to get her to agree, until last month when suddenly he just wanted everything finalised as fast as possible. But Imra’s been on a sailing trip around the world and so hasn’t signed anything. Adam got her on the radio to confirm the details five minutes ago.”

Rhea’s eyes widened dramatically as she gasped.

“I had no idea. He told me he was divorced I-”

“It didn’t turn up on our checks because all contact has been through Rhea and her lawyers,” Cat added with a smug look. 

She turned to Astra who looked downright delighted at the turn of events. Astra turned with queenly grace and a look rather like an avenging fury.

“Which in the circumstances means Mon-el and his mother have, if memory serves me right, committed treason.”

There was a long silence as all eyes turned from Cat to Mon-el and Rhea.

 

Kara felt strangely calm while piece by piece every lie and over reaction was suddenly explained. As she watched a small group of the royal guard move closer awaiting orders it occurred to her Mon-el hadn’t said anything as he stood next to her all eyes on him. Kara turned slowly from the guard to look at Mon-el. He met her gaze and gave a sort of half shrug. 

After all the shit she had put up with, the lies, the manipulation, him pushing her boundaries for weeks, the ultimatum, and all his other petty bullshit; and he’d been married to someone else this whole time. And now he was being exposed all he did was shrug.

She took a deep calming breath trying to contain the anger rising steadily inside her.

“I told you I needed a husband. I needed to marry to secure my throne. It would have taken two seconds for you to say sorry I’m married and wish me luck instead you- you-”

Words failed her so she just screamed in fury at a stunned Mon-el. He blinked at her looking a little surprised then he offered another little shrug. Words came back.

“Why did you do all this if you were already married?” she yelled. 

Mon-el looked at her for a moment still seeming stunned by the change in events. Father Zod moved forwards to intervene but she shoved him back with one hand her eyes still on the uncomfortable looking Mon-el.

“Well?” she demanded. 

“I wanted to be king,” he said finally.

“You were never going to be King!” she yelled back. “One year I said. I needed a husband for one year!”

“You would have changed your mind.”

Kara had no idea how to even respond to that. She looked at him disgusted. He glared back at her crossing his arms defensively. Of all the selfish-

“Oh it’s not like you can judge me. You’re not so innocent princess. Especially not when you’ve been fucking Lena this-” 

Kara’s fist connected with his face in a satisfying crunch as he collapsed falling down several steps to land at Brainy’s waiting feet.

“Fuck you!” Kara yelled. “You cost me my throne! You pathetic, selfish, little boy. Lena!”

Lena jumped up closely followed by Jack. Kara gave her a nod.

“The wedding’s all yours.”

Kara turned to storm to take a seat. Brainy had Mon-el up from the floor with a look of vicious glee rarely seen on his face as he begun dragging Mon-el away. A couple of the royal guard had already taken hold of a protesting Rhea. Lena seemed a little in shock but she looked excited.

“Okay. Right. Well then, I’ll just need a minute to change then we can proceed,” Lena said quickly. “Uh, Kara could help me with-”

“No!” Zod said loudly. 

They all looked at him confused then Lena sighed rolling her eyes.

“Fine! We’ll all just come back tomorrow when my wedding is set and I’ll get married then. Not like it really makes a difference now.”

“We have compelling evidence you are not entering your marriage in good faith and so we, the church, refuse,” Father Zod said.

“Excuse me?” Lena said looking at Zod. “I have very good intentions in my marriage. Don’t I Jack?”

“The very best, we’ve discussed it in detail,” Jack said quickly nodding. “I'm very happy to marry her today or tomorrow.”

“You are not intending to honour your vows. It is quite clear you and the crown princess are intending to-”

“Oh as if that’d be the first time,” Lena said turning to glare at Father Zod. “There’s a law about no one discussing what happens in the royal bedchambers for a fucking reason. And it’s not like Jack cares. Jack, sweetie, do you give a shit if I repeatedly fuck Kara in our marriage bed?”

“Not at all. In fact I highly encourage it. See no problem. We can just skip that line in the vows or something,” Jack said with a wave of his hand.

Anne seemed to be having a heart attack beside Jack but no one was paying any attention to her. All eyes were on Father Zod who was clearly unimpressed with that argument. Kara scoffed turning to Father Zod.

“You kind of have to. If you don’t let Lena marry then there will be a scramble for the next ruler and the-” 

Kara’s eyes widened as it all made sense. She turned to Lena and they shared a moment. 

“The church has majority vote to elect the next monarch,” Lena finished.

They all turned back to Father Zod. Lena glared at the high priest.

“You’re refusing to let me marry Jack so that you can pick the next monarch.” 

Father Zod to his credit didn’t even bother denying it.

“We will be able to rescind that ridiculous policy on taxation-”

“Seriously!” Kara yelled. “You’re going to screw over the entire country and one of the longest consecutive monarchies in the world because you don’t want to pay taxes?”

“We think it’s less screwing over the country and more, returning it to Rao’s light princess. To a masculine influence,” Father Zod said with a smile.

Alex quickly restrained Astra before she could throw herself at him as everyone looked at them. The majority of the guests and priests seeming unsure what to do as they looked between the two groups.

“Do you honestly think the people of Krypton will let this go?” Kara asked indicating the dozen cameras recording the whole thing. “You’re purposefully screwing up the line of the monarchy to get out of paying taxes. Taxes I remind you that fund most of the health services in this country.”

“We are doing no such thing.” Father Zod said glaring at her. “We are following the word of Rao and marriage is a sacred-”

“Spare us the bullshit,” Astra yelled pulling herself free of Alex to glare at Father Zod. “You will pay for this Zod.”

Father Zod sneered at her.

“You can stomp and scream all you want your majesty but there is a nothing you can do in this-”

“Confession!” Jess said suddenly jumping to her feet.

Lena’s eyes widened and she grinned turning to Kara excitedly.

“Kara you need to confess some sins,” she said quickly.

“What- I already-” Kara looked at her confused.

“Now Kara!” Lena said panicking a little as Jess quickly begun whispering to Jack and Alex then moving to Astra.

“Um okay, I need to seek forgiveness for a sin. I uh… I said the f word in the temple when Mon-el admitted he was already married. Um… and then I hit him, that’s a sin right? Fighting in church?”

She looked at a random priest for confirmation. He nodded quickly seeming a bit surprised to be singled out. Kara looked back at Lena trying to work out what she was supposed to be saying. Lena looked at her and sighed.

“Seriously? That’s it, that’s all you’ve got?” she asked.

“I already confessed my sins an hour ago to be ready for marriage I don’t really have any left,” Kara said throwing up her hands exasperated.

She wasn’t sure what was happening. Lena looked at her trying to convey something. Father Zod seeming to catch onto whatever was happening raised his hand and started to forgive her.

“Did you confess the time you spanked me on the ass and I called you Daddy?” Lena yelled sounding a bit panicked.

There was a moment of stunned silence from the entire temple as Kara stared at Lena in shock her mouth falling open. Father Zod froze halfway through his forgiveness his eyes wide.

“No,” Kara squeaked.

Lena gestured to the shocked Father Zod before her. Kara turned to Father Zod and sighed.

“I have sins to seek forgiveness for,” she said loudly.

There was a sudden wave of laughter from the crowd. Father Zod looked at Kara clearly angry. He sucked in a deep breath his face twisting in fury.

“For what sins do you wish to be forgiven?” Father Zod asked through gritted teeth.

“I wish to be forgiven for…” Kara sighed covering her face as she started blushing. “Deviant behaviour,” she mumbled.

Lena nodded to Jess who took the moment to sneak off as several royal guards were very quickly spoken to. Lena turned back to Kara who was looking at her a little desperate. Lena gave her an apologetic look and motioned for her to continue. Kara could feel the eyes of everyone in the temple looking at her along with every camera.

“A few months ago I was on holiday in Australia and I uh- I was with a woman who I was engaging in a romantic and sexual relationship outside wedlock, also a sin I think… technically. Not that anyone cares these days. And for the purposes of experimentation we decided to- oh Rao this is so embarrassing,”

Kara said covering her face as the temple was filled with a ripple of laughter. Lena wrapped her right arm around Kara’s waist and captured her left hand in hers giving it a comforting squeeze before gently pulling her back a step as everyone stared at them. Lena leaned in to whisper in her ear.

“I’ll remind you which one of us called the other one Daddy. Now keep confessing and walk slowly backwards to the doors with me I have a plan.”

Kara looked at her with a pained expression. Lena gave her an apologetic one in return. Kara looked back at the angry Father Zod.

“For the purposes of experimentation we decided to try… power play,” she said.

There was another laugh from the crowd. Kara’s face was bright red and Lena was slowly walking her backwards.

“Keep talking,” Lena whispered giving her hand another squeeze. “And remember you’re the crown princess.”

Kara sighed and squared her shoulders taking another step backwards.

“During which I repeatedly struck her on the… backside with my hand, pulled her hair and she called me Daddy while I uh… oh Rao save me,” Kara took another look at the apologetic Lena as her blush returned in full force. “While I repeatedly penetrated her with a strap on until she came screaming my name. And for this I seek forgiveness,” she finished in a rush.

There was a long moment of silence as everyone looked at them. Then Father Zod sighed and stormed over waving his hand over her head and heart.

“Rao is all loving and all forgiving. You are forgiven your sin of deviancy and may once again walk in his light. Now-”

“I have another sin to confess,” Kara said quickly.

“What sins do you seek forgiveness for?” Father Zod growled as they took another step backwards.

“The sin of-”

She glanced at Lena.

“Desecration of holy grounds,” Lena whispered her eyes on something Kara couldn’t see.

“Uh, do I really want to confess that one?” Kara asked getting a laugh from the watching crowd.

Lena gave her a look.

“You got a different sin you can confess?” she asked.

Kara sighed. Resigned she looked back at Father Zod. He seemed to be on the verge of an anger induced heart attack. 

“The sin of desecrating holy grounds,” she said loudly.

There was quite a few shocked gasps at this. They were halfway down the aisle and the look Kara was getting from Father Zod was murderous. She glanced around and saw the cameras were tracking them with several gleeful reporters standing by. Lena gave her hand a squeeze and held her a little closer as they took another step back. Kara looked at Father Zod. He cleared his throat impatiently.

“During a visit to this here holy temple, dedicated to the great and kind Rao who sees and forgives all, I had a disagreement with the man I had intended to take as my husband. He objected to my being close with the woman I uh… did the deviant behaviour with, even though we hadn’t done anything at all during the time of my relationship with him. I just want to make that clear, we had behaved very properly despite our feelings for each other. But still he threatened to break off our engagement if I didn’t stop speaking with her. He considered it an insult to his standing that my heart belonged to another when I was marrying him, even though it was an arranged marriage, and I told him so from day one. By the way, what a hypocrite considering all we now know. Like seriously he was getting on me for not being all pure of intentions in our engagement but he-”

Kara hid her still blushing face behind a very smug looking Lena.

“Again it’s not against the rules as they’re technically ours. It’s also a bit of an unofficial tradition for the new monarch to take the crown and cape back after,” Lena said smiling at Cat

“My great grandmother reportedly used to go skinny dipping in her tiara,” Kara added.

“I thought she did that in the crown and her husband wore the tiara,” Lena said looking over her shoulder at Kara.

“That was only when she was drunk, or he’d upset her. Apparently she really disliked when he skipped family dinners even when there was a war going on.”

Lena made a small ‘huh’ noise and they looked back at Cat.

“So does that mean you have tried on the crown, princess?”

Cat’s eyes focused on Lena. Lena hummed pretending to think.

“I can’t recall,” she said smiling. “But if I did it would have been in private. And I’m sure it would have looked fantastic on me.”

Cat sighed.

“We did put the tiara on Streaky, it didn’t fit but she seemed to like it,” Kara said. 

“And you consider that an appropriate use of what are important cultural artifacts?” Cat asked giving Kara a stern look.

“Yes, don’t you?” Kara countered. “Cat Grant, Queen of all media.”

They both gave her a look and Cat gave them the subtlest of nods as she glanced at the camera.

“Yes well, back to politics,” she said ignoring their amused looks. “Your first appearance in parliament was memorable. You entered together and then your majesty, you opened parliament and immediately called for the vote on the marriage equality act.”

Kara straightened a little as she slipped into her role as Queen.

“Yes, it was well past time and the delay for a dozen politicians to all claim how near and dear the issue was to their hearts, despite how long the legislation had been ignored, seemed unfair to the queer community of Krytpon,” Kara said.

“The vote was 112 to 7. Quite the landslide of support,” Cat said.

“And as it was so popular I immediately signed it into law,” Kara said grinning at Lena.

Lena smiled back and kissed her cheek.

“And then you just as immediately left,” Cat said giving Kara a look o censure.

“I had things to do,” Kara said a bit defensively.

Cat looked at Lena.

“Were you things?”

Lena smiled widely.

“Not that time,” Lena said. “It was actually because Kara needed her burns dressing changed. She accidentally stuck her hand in a fish pond two minutes before we went in. As her medical adviser was being looked over for a concussion we had to go to Argo General for the dressing change.”

“And how did Dr Danvers end up with a concussion and her majesty’s hand in a fish pond two minutes before the opening of parliament?” Cat asked her eyes still on Lena.

Kara was blushing again. Lena glanced at Kara then smiled at Cat.

“She uh, apparently was distracted watching me talking to my assistant and stumbled while walking beside the pond. Alex, Dr Danvers, tried to catch her and they both fell. Alex into the pond itself which resulted in her hitting her head and getting the concussion. Kara however was caught at the last second by her security but her hands did go into the water soaking the glove.”

Cat sighed.

“Well speaking of falls let’s move onto the fall from grace for the house of Daxam. Now I understand there is an ongoing legal battle so you cannot comment your majesty, but in your opinion princess, how likely do you think it is they will escape the treason charges.”

Kara huffed sitting back and crossing her arms. Lena patted her leg in a comforting gesture.

“Mon-el I think could avoid the treason charges for lying about his marital status. It will strongly depend on whether the judge considers that the wedding did not take place, as no vows were made, to be a key point. He did admit his marital status when asked, but at the same time he was standing at the altar and gave no indication he wasn’t going to go through with the ceremony before being confronted. Rhea will have a harder time as she did lie to the Queen Regent about his marital status when she first called about the possible marriage and that call was recorded. As well as her declarations in the temple. But I doubt she’ll actually serve jail time unless there’s something else in the emails between her and Imra’s lawyers. She is of course now banned from any public service roles and the seat for Daxam in the house of nobles is currently sitting empty until the charges are settled whereupon Lord Daxam will need to take it or give the place to a cousin.”

Cat nodded and looked at Kara.

“I believe Lord Daxam sued you princess. For assault and battery.”

“Yes he did,” Kara said with a sigh. “As I am Queen he couldn’t sue for compensation in civil court but I did get charged with assault and battery in the criminal one. I plead guilty and in light of the extenuating circumstances I was given the mnimum sentence, 200 hours of community service. We’re still trying to work out how I can fulfil that without it just being my current royal duties.”

Lena laughed softly and Cat looked at her with a small smile.

“Now a question much of Krypton has been asking, your highness, your relationship with Jack Spheer…”

Lena threw her head back as she laughed then looked at the camera.

“I’m sorry to disappoint everyone but Jack is very gay. We’ve been each other’s beard multiple times. That is to say we’ve pretended to be in a romantic relationship to appear straight to others. Everytime he made some comment or joke about his beard it was usually about me. He would like it known that he is very single at this time and would love to meet a nice Kryptonian man to settle down with, preferably one who,” Lena sighed. “Knows how to ride well.”

Kara hid her snicker as Lena rolled her eyes and turned back to Cat.

“So when is the official wedding set?” Cat asked.

“We’re just going to do a small private ceremony. Close friends and family,” Kara said. “Pictures will be released but we really feel like we’ve done the big wedding already and want to do something that better fits us for the second one, which let’s face it, is really just paperwork.”

Cat nodded then gave them a look.

“I do have an invitation yes?”

“Of course, and it’s going to be a blackout event,” Lena said smiling a little too brightly at Cat. “Astra’s request, something about camping in the grounds with friends…”

There was the tiniest twitch of Cat’s lips. She looked at her notes and straightened a little.

“Final question. Do you have any regrets, apart from not thinking of the elopement earlier, about your wedding?”

Kara and Lena exchanged a look. Lena nodded a soft smile on her face.

“That my brother never got to see. He would have loved every minute of the drama and I can only hope he is now truly at peace knowing I did follow my heart.”

Cat’s face softened the tiniest bit and she nodded.

“I am sure Rao in his kindness would have allowed his soul the chance to bear witness.”

They were all quiet for a moment then Cat nodded to them.

“It’s always a pleasure to see you both, thank you for coming down today.”

They smiled back and the lights changed. Lena immediately turned and gave Kara a quick kiss.

“Well I suppose I should be grateful you managed to keep your hands mostly off each other for the interview,” Cat said. 

“You’re welcome,” Lena said and quickly led Kara away before the media queen could say some kind of retort.

 

The museum was delighted to see them and Lena gave 45 a final loving caress before handing the keys over for them to do the final preparations before the car went on display. The museum curator, Lyra Strayd, was happy to show them through the new exhibit.

“We are still preparing some of the exhibit pieces for the unveiling next week, and we’ll be moving the relevant trophies from the Krypton 100 display to this one the night before,” Lyra said indicating the glass display case where they would go. “But the main renovations have all been completed.”

She waved her hand to indicate the timeline painted on the wall showing the car’s history ending at the raised platform 45 would be placed on. Lyra was still talking but Lena’s attention was caught by the final pictures. She quietly excused herself for a moment and went to look at the end of the timeline. 

There was a space at the very end that would likely be filled by some official photo from the unveiling the caption beneath noting it’s donation to the museum. But there was one before that she didn’t expect. It was a picture from the parade with her and Kara standing in front of 45 holding Streaky and smiling at each other. Jack and Mon-el were visible to one side. She read the caption.

45 made a public appearance in the closing parade of the Krypton 100 driven by Jack Spheer. During the parade 45 was partially dismantled by then Crown Princess Kara Zor-el and Lena Luthor to retrieve a kitten trapped in the engine. The kitten was later adopted by the Crown Princess and named Streaky.

Lena smiled and looked at the next picture. Her breath caught and she felt her heart stutter for a moment. The picture was of her and Lex sitting in 45 wearing their red team Krypton hats. Lena was in the driver’s seat smiling up at the camera looking a little nervous. Lex was leaning close to her clearly caught in the middle of telling her something with one hand on her headrest the other pointing to the dash and his head tilted up towards the camera with a quick smile.

“We contacted your family to see if there was a picture of 45 when your brother first acquired it,” Lyra said quietly beside her. “Your mother said this one would better show it’s history with your family.”

Lena nodded her eyes going to the photo caption.

Photo by Lillian Luthor. Alexander Luthor age 24 (left) and Lena Luthor age 15 (right) sitting in 45. Photo title by Alexander Luthor: Giving the Princess her first driving lesson.

Kara wrapped her arms around Lena holding her close. Lena leaned into her wife and looked back at her brother’s smiling face. She turned and kissed Kara’s cheek. They thanked Lyra and left. 

“Are you okay?” Kara asked gently.

Lena looked at her and smiled.

“Yes,” she said stepping closer wrapping her arms around Kara’s neck. “I’ve got you don’t I.”

“Always,” Kara whispered pulling her closer.
